THE MOST REMARKABLE RELIGIOUS PHENOMENON of all time is The Movement known as "Psychiana". Not only does this 

Movement reveal the Power of God to men and women, it has been able from the very start to show men and women everywhere how they may actually and literally use the invisible Power of God right here on earth.

So remarkable have been the manifestations of this Power, that it can be safely asserted that The Spirit of God has chosen this Movement to bring to humanity a new revelation of the Power of God. Not a new Power-just a new revelation of that Power. For the Power has always existed. For some reason or other, men and women have not been told of this fact. Those who have taken upon themselves the noble task of revealing God to the rest of us, have, in some strange manner, missed completely the most amazing facts concerning God.

Much time and effort has been expended in telling us what we must do in order to inherit eternal life "in heaven".

But no time has been spent in introducing to us the Power of God here and now, when we need it most, and when we can enjoy it best. Of course-it would be very nice to have the assurance that when we die physically we shall at once be transplanted into a realm of eternal bliss, provided we believe certain theological theories about God. 

That-is nice. That is-it's nice if true. Unfortunately, however, no evidence of such a state of eternal bliss has 1

YOUR GOD-POWER 

been forthcoming. No evidence whatsoever of the truth of that theory can be adduced, because it does not exist. 

None has ever returned from the great beyond to give us authentic information about what goes on there. This is an amazing thing. In the light of the evident lack of such evidence, you and I may very safely doubt just a little bit. 

We may very safely do a bit of experimenting in the Realm of The Spirit of God. If we do this, it is entirely likely that we may discover truths of the present existence of God which our churches have entirely missed. 

If one takes the stand that we cannot possibly know anything about God, or the Power of God here on the earth, of course we won't. That avenue remains blocked completely. And we are the ones who block it. However, this author many years ago decided to do a bit of experimenting in the Realm of The Spirit of God. He had a very deep-seated belief, not in church theories of God, but in the present existence of The Spirit of God, not so much "beyond the tomb" as here on the earth. And he built his life on that premise. 

What has happened since that day speaks for itself. It is at once evident to the most casual observer that the author did most certainly make a spiritual discovery of the Power of God, which had never been made before by any theological organization. He discovered that the most potent dynamic Force or Power on this earth today is the Power of The Spirit of God. More than that, he has proven beyond a shadow of doubt that every normal man and woman may here and now discover and draw upon all the Power there is in the Invisible Realm of The Spirit of God. 

This was a staggering discovery to make. Yet it was made, as many hundreds of thousands of men and women all over the world can testify. What this amazing discovery

really means though, has not yet been grasped by our national leaders. It cannot be said that it has been grasped by the American people as a whole. 

It is remarkable-yes-it's hard to understand how, when the solution to all the problems of life is shown to men and women, it takes the nation as a whole so very long to awaken to that staggering fact. Of course, the nation, yes, the whole world will awaken to this fact before too long. 

But a lot of lives could be saved, and countless homes which death will blight could be spared that terrible calamity if the national leaders would only pay a little bit of attention to the Power of The Spirit of God, and the remarkable discovery which was made by the Founder of 

The "Psychiana" Movement, years ago.

For if it is a fact that all men and women can find and use the Power of God, in a perfectly natural normal way, here and now, it just simply means that they can, collectively, throw the Power of God against those opposite forces which are devastating the earth, even as I write this. 

For it would be sheer nonsense and imbecility to state or even infer that the actual and literal Power of Almighty God cannot stop such mad-men as Hitler, Hirohito, and Mussolini.

It would be equally imbecilic to state that when The Spirit of God is known and its Power recognized, that Great Spirit cannot bring to the nations of the earth, an abundance of peace, prosperity, and spiritual satisfaction. 

In fact-all this earth needs is The Spirit of God in it. 

Rather may I say, all the earth needs is a consciousness of the Power of The Spirit of God. That Spirit exists here and now. Every normal man and woman may find and use that 

Power. So then, the Power existing, it is just the conscious

YOUR GOD-POWER 

knowledge of that Power and what It can do that the human race needs.

In other words, while 99% of the American people are asking themselves why all this bloodshed, hate, and misery, the answer lies close at hand. It need not be. There is absolutely no more need for one more life to be taken than there is for the proverbial cow to jump over the moon. And yet, in between the time this book is written and its release from the press, millions perhaps, of our best young men will lie dying on some foreign field of battle. Hundreds of thousands more will lie suffering, shattered in both mind and body, in our hospitals. And there is no need of it at all. The remedy for this madness is here. It exists. It is so potent and powerful that no man in his right mind can question the effectiveness of the remedy. 

For that remedy is The Spirit of God, and all the Power there is in that Spirit. If this Power exists, and if the human race is oblivious to, or unconscious of, this staggering fact, then there is but one path before me—that is, to tell men and women of the existence of the Power of The Spirit of 

God, not "in heaven” or “in hell”, but right here on the earth, freely and fully available to all.

It amazes me when I see the absolute indifference of our national leaders to the Power of The Spirit of God. It is a well known fact that this religious phenomenon called "Psychiana" is revealing the Power of God to the multitudes, yet those in authority over us seem to be not in the slightest degree interested in the one Power which, at their own bidding, can halt this brutal conflict and can halt it very speedily. 1 I am an American citizen. I have a beautiful son flying in the U. S. Navy. I would give my life any time for this country, and be glad to do it. Yet I cannot refrain at this

point, knowing the Power of God as I do, from making the prediction that neither of the warring nations, Allies or Axis, will ever see the day when they will completely obliterate the other side. 

Such human parasites as Hitler, Mussolini, and Hirohito may, and will be, dethroned. But so far as the complete annihilation of the German, Japanese, or Italian people goes-well, I don't believe the Allied nations want that anyhow. The point I am trying to make is that there does exist now, the Power of Almighty God, and, if the American people and their leaders will, that staggering Power can be thrown against the Axis, and its leaders can be dethroned by the Power of God, and that, without firing another shot.

Certainly the dazzling, scintillating Power which created this universe can do that. Every thinking American will agree that this can be done. But why the unbelief when it comes to the existence of the Power of The Spirit of God and the ability of that Spirit to dethrone these mad-men? 

That's what I can't understand.

Yet in a way I do understand it. It seems too incredible to be true that down thru the countless ages of time, there has existed on this earth, available at any and all times to mankind, The Spirit of God. Being God, that invisible 

Spirit, known and used, certainly could have saved humanity from the scores of frightful wars, plagues, pestilences which as far back as we have history, have plagued and decimated the human race.

There is not an economic question or problem that cannot be immediately solved by the Power of God. There is not one of those problems which would or could have arisen, had the existence of God on the earth been brought to the attention of man. But this Power has not been

YOUR GOD-POWER 

brought to the attention of the peoples of the earth by anyone, prior to the "Psychiana" Movement, and its teachings.

We take no credit for being the medium thru which the Spirit of God is operating. Your author has no powers you do not possess. He is no closer, perhaps, to God than you are. There is only one difference between the author and you. He, thru years of active experience with hundreds of thousands of people, has definitely proven beyond a shadow of doubt that man can contact and talk with God. He has proven, also, beyond the peradventure of doubt, that all the Powers of The Spirit of God are available to all normal men and women everywhere, anytime. 

There is absolutely no question about this amazing fact, staggering as it is. There is no room here for even a little bit of the evidence we have. But that evidence exists. Over the past fifteen years I have seen everything from the raising of the dead to the complete annihilation of every problem which plagued those who, thru our humble efforts, have discovered the amazing infallibility of the Power of The Spirit of God. May I repeat in this book what I have so many times stated in other books-There are absolutely no limitations to the Power of God, in the life of any man. 

And I mean here and now.

Your common sense tells you that the theory that all men must die before they can know God is a very useless and faithless theory. Why should Almighty God draw such a plan as that? Why is it that, if The Creator exists, He cannot reveal Himself to the human race now, stopping once and for all this ghastly human slaughter?

The answer is-There is no reason at all why God cannot do this. As a matter of fact, the Almighty is dying to do just that. All the staggering Power there is in the Realm

of The Spirit of God was placed at the disposal of the human race a long time ago. It came at birth. It has been there ever since. Then why is it that humanity knows nothing about this limitless Power of God? The answer to that is simple also. No one has ever told us of the existence of such Power.

Theological organizations of ages ago, not being willing to understand the present existence of God, conjured up a "god" whom they placed "in the sky". They told their followers that God is so great and so big that man cannot, in this life, know anything whatsoever about God. Then, perhaps to further their own ambitions, they instituted a series of practices and exercises and beliefs which, they said, would insure us a "home in heaven" after we have shuffled off this mortal coil.

The common sense of the American people should tell them that such a concept of Almighty God cannot be true. 

No concept of God which came out of the dark ages can be true. Nor can it fit the American mind today. Any system of religion which claims that the Almighty created man “in sin”, and needing a “savior" to save him from that “sin”, is a false concept of God. Yet this is the only concept offered to humanity. And one look at the world tells us very plainly where that false concept has brought us. 

Had not the "Psychiana" Movement been born, there would be nothing but black night and human extermination ahead of the whole human race. As it is, the actual and literal truths of God are now available to men, women, and nations. At last, there is a Movement on earth thru which the Spirit of God is manifesting its Power. That is something the world has never known before. 

Naturally this Movement, and the Power of God it manifests is very far removed from the pagan concepts of God

YOUR GOD-POWER 

which the world holds today. They have nothing in common, and they can have nothing in common. One is true, the other false. The false concept of God, with its "inbred sin”, its “virgin birth", its “immaculate conception”, its “resurrection", etc., has left this earth a bleeding mass of human suffering. And there is nothing any existing system of religion can do about it. All they can do is what they are doing-stand on the side-lines, scratch their heads, and wonder where God is. Certainly they do not know. Equally certain is it that they haven't made much effort to find out. 

They have depended solely upon their "traditions" for the truth. But the truth was not in those traditions, and so, in a crisis like the present one, when the whole earth is crying out for the Power of God, not a single theological organization in existence can answer that cry.

The Almighty will let man go just so far. It is a Law of God that when the need for the Spirit of God, and its Power becomes a real need, the Almighty will raise up someone who, because of his faith in God, is willing to throw creeds, rites, rituals, and traditions out of the window, in order to deal direct with God. Such an one inevitably appears. True, the world has never sunk as low as it is today, and there has been no need therefore for such staggering manifestations of the Power of God as we of "Psychiana" are witnessing.

It is quite safe to say that this Movement is the descent of The Spirit of God to the earth, if you will understand me. What it really is, is this—It is the Movement which is bringing to the earth for the first time in its history, not theories of God but the actual and literal Power of The Spirit of God. The Movement has never been less than

that. Of course, those whose theology is founded on the traditions of a pagan past, swear by all that's holy that "this man Robinson" is "of the devil". They could hardly say anything else. For the actual truths of God, as we are revealing those truths to the world, must be diametrically opposed to what theology teaches. 

If they were not, Almighty God would not have needed The "Psychiana" Movement thru which He could reveal His Power. Instead, if the churches were teaching the truths of God instead of an old pagan dark-age tradition, the Spirit of God would be manifesting thru them instead of thru us. You may be sure that where The Spirit of God is, there also will the manifestations be. And you may also be sure that where the manifestations are not, The Spirit of God is not.

Where The Spirit of God is, there is Life. There too, is 

Power. There too is an abundant peace. None of these things exists on the earth today. Therefore, in spite of all our churches and other theological institutions, The Spirit of God still remains a totally unknown factor on the earth. 

Is it not safe therefore to assume that the teachings of present-day theology have none of God in them? I think it is. This Movement has demonstrated that fact so many, many times. Not that we are opposed to these systems of theology-we are not. We wish they would allow us to bring the manifestations of The Spirit of God to earth thru them. But that is an impossibility. Or so it seems. For the only things these good theological organizations are interested in, is "standing fast in the traditions they have been taught."

But truth is seldom in tradition. It is. No one brought it to earth originally. It came when God manifested Life on the earth. And America will find God much faster keeping

YOUR GOD-POWER 

far away from present theological preachments than it will find God thru them. God cannot be found thru them.

Truth is never found in error. God can be found, however, because God exists here and now. The Spirit of God, with all its Power, is available to all men, not because of traditions, but in spite of them. That Power does exist, here and now-for all men and women. 

With it, such monsters as Hitler and his crew, with their damnable philosophies, can be speedily banished, and perhaps millions of our sons can be spared the ruthless bullet of the killer. Had it not been for theological traditions, this war, and all past wars, never could have been. It is the complete absence of The Spirit of God from church teachings which has made humanity drink the bitter dregs of despair we are drinking now. And those dregs will become more bitter before the end, which is not yet in sight. 

For many years your author has seen an ever-increasing demand for a set of Lessons in finding the Power of God, put out in book form. So, busy as he is, he is taking time out to produce such a work. These are not the original "Psychiana" Lessons which were written nearly 16 years ago. These Lessons were written early in the year 1943. 

They contain many things which are not in the original Lessons. But do not confound this book with the first inspiration I received directly from The Spirit of God. 

A few months ago I released another book entitled "The Pathway to God". Many will find God thru what is written in that book. Hundreds of thousands have found 

God thru the original Lessons. Many more hundreds of thousands will find God thru what is written in this book. 

I can safely say that the instructions given herein, will, if

followed, reveal to every one of you, not church theories of God, not pagan traditions of God, but the actual and literal Power of The Spirit of God Itself. 

I do not want you to read this book thru. I want you to read it Lesson by Lesson, allowing two weeks between each Lesson. This will give you time for the simple little exercises which will reveal The Spirit of God to you. It is a pity that the human race has sunk so low in unbelief that it needs months and months of study to eliminate that unbelief. But that's how it is. That is the condition of 

Christendom today. And the sooner we get down to the fundamentals of God, the sooner will the human race be lifted out of this pit of horrible, death-dealing unbelief.

It has been a hard job, believe me. Every conceivable effort has been made by the churches, Catholic and Protestant, to stop The Spirit of God from manifesting on earth thru the "Psychiana" Movement. But is that possible?

Certainly not. If it were possible for anyone to stop this 

Movement, The Spirit of God could not be in it. But The 

Spirit of God is in it. And so, it has gotten far beyond the place where any human organization can even begin to interfere with its work, and for this we thank God. 

So then let us go to our studies together. If you will follow me step by step, I will lead you into the most powerfully blessed experience ever to come to you. I will lead you to God. I won't try to interpret God-I shall let God interpret Himself to you.

THE AUTHOR

MY DEAR FELLOW-STUDENT: 

You and I are about to begin a very wonderful journey together. We shall travel along the most beautiful path ever known. Little did you suspect that such a journey as this could be possible on this earth. The indescribable beauty of it all will make you stand aghast in amazement as the beautiful scene slowly unfolds to your wondering eyes. For the scene is beautiful-believe me, it is. 

This pathway upon which we have now set our feet is the pathway that leads to God. Many hundreds of thousands of our fellow-Americans have had the idea that the pathway to God must lead first thru the tomb. They have been taught that none can ever really know anything about God while on this earth. Only after they die. But my fellow-student, nothing could be farther from the truth than that. For will you please believe me when I say to you that all the scintillating Beauty, all the matchless

Might, all the dynamic Power there is in the Realm of God is available to you. Now-not beyond the tomb. 

You will probably blink your eyes and really wonder whether I know whereof I speak when you read this statement. But may I say to you that there lies before you the most amazingly beautiful and powerful Realm of The 

Spirit of God. And you, whoever you are, are invited by me to take my hand for the next few months, and I'll endeavor to lead you into this fairy-land of God. For it really 12

seems like a fairy-land. Actually it isn't-it's the most real 

Realm that ever could exist. It's an invisible Realm to be sure, for God never was anything other than invisible 

Spirit. Yet this invisible Realm, and this invisible Spirit actually created the universe and all that is in it. 

This wonderful Power-Realm into which I shall endeavor to lead you, is, in itself, invisible. But-the manifestations of this Realm of the Spirit of God are material, physical manifestations. Everything on this earth-the trees, the beautiful singing birds, the wonderfully lovely flowers, the little children who, as I write this to you, are playing outside my bedroom window here in Moscow, Idaho-all these lovely manifestations of the Power of The Spirit of God are physical, material manifestations. 

When humanity, and its religious teachers conceived the idea that nothing whatsoever can be known about God here on the earth, they made the most fatal mistake of their lives. That mistake has brought to the earth all the misery, all the death, all the suffering, which is plaguing humanity today.

As I write this, a horrible war is being waged. It won't last too long. But the ghastly effects of it will not ever be wiped from the memories of the millions of men and women, many of whom will have taken from them, one, or maybe more, of their beloved sons. Mine is there. He is a Naval Ensign flying a torpedo-bomber. I don't know if Alfie will ever come back to us. And there are hundreds of thousands of others just like me. I would willingly die for Alfie any time.

If the truths of God had ever been brought to humanity by the religious teachers of the present or past-if the Power of The Spirit of God had ever been known on the earth, do you think such a ghastly calamity as this could

YOUR GOD-POWER 

have happened? Of course it could not have happened. 

The very fact that it is happening is absolute proof that Almighty God, and the beautiful Realm of God I am about to lead you into, is positively unknown on the earth today.

Oh yes-I know. You have certain religious beliefs. Your priest or your preacher or your church has taught you certain things which they told you were the absolute truths of Almighty God. But that is only what they taught. They never were able to prove anything.

Your common sense tells you unerringly that if the Power of God was even only slightly known on the earth, that Power could most certainly be used to wipe out of the earth the forces which have been, and are responsible, not only for this war, but for every other war humanity has ever known. How can any one man, or any three men, plunge the whole world into this ghastly calamity of death and horror if the Power of The Spirit of God is known? 

They just couldn't do it. And you know they couldn't do it. You know as well as I, that while this earth is flooded with theories of God, the actual Power of God is so completely unknown that the great majority of Americans don't believe there is any God whose Power can be known here and now.

Oh yes--they believe that somewhere, above the sky, there is some sort of a Power that created the universe. 

But as far as contacting and knowing that Power, and using it to drive wholesale murder, crime, suffering, illness, and death from the earth-well, you know as well as I know that no such Power as that has ever been taught by any religion. No priest or preacher has ever introduced us to such a Power. All religion has done to date is to issue a very questionable “life-insurance” and “fire-insurance” policy which only matures after we die, and even then gives

absolutely no proof that there ever was need of such a policy. Religion, as we have known it, has never been able to reveal the Power of God to anyone. So it is not true religion. 

Where, my friend, have your religious theories, if you have any, gotten you? Just how much do you know about the scintillating, shining, lovely Power of The Spirit of 

God? How long is it since you used the Power of God to bring into your life the material or spiritual things you need? How long is it since you used the Power of The 

Spirit of God against illness, debt, want, or any other thing lacking in your life? Well you know the answer to that.

And so do I. We both know that you know absolutely nothing about such a Power. That is the sort of a Power we are not supposed to know anything about until after we die. But what you and I want, is absolutely conclusive knowledge that such a Power exists here and now, and can be used by every normal human being, not "after he or she dies" but before he or she dies. 

Of what use is religion to you if you cannot use it now? 

Of what use is a "faith" if that "faith" cannot reveal to you now, right here on this earth-the Power of The Spirit of 

God? No use whatsoever. Yet that's all we have. Just "faiths". Honestly believed "faiths" to be sure. But “faiths” and "faiths" only. They may be true-they may be false.

Well-my friend-you and I are going out of that realm of “faith” which never can reveal God. We're going to enter into a Realm which is so pregnant with Spiritual Power that the whole world will stand amazed at the significance of it all when it begins to realize that such a 

Realm really exists. The "Psychiana” Movement is the most staggeringly significant Movement of God this earth has ever known. You are now an Associate-Member in the

YOUR GOD-POWER 

only religion which is really bringing to men and women the actual and literal Power of The Spirit of God. We have been doing that for 15 years and more. 

And what has happened? Well-plenty of things have happened, as you probably know. The wonderfully illuminating Power of The Spirit of God has been made a living Reality to hundreds of thousands of men and women all over the world. So much evidence has been sent to us of this that we haven't been able to keep it all. Not a mail comes in to us that doesn't contain many letters telling us what The Spirit of God has accomplished in human lives all over the world.

I wish I could begin to tell you what The Spirit of God has actually done in all these lives, but that is impossible. 

You have already received samples of this evidence, and now, you have come to me for the real truths of God. All right, my fellow-member-I'll give them to you. And I'll give them to you straight from the shoulder. I'll be as kind and as patient as I can. And when you have finished these 

Lessons in The Spirit of God, you'll know that I know what 

I'm talking about. You will be supremely happy. 

I can't promise you a home in heaven. Nor can I promise you that you will sing forever round the throne of God in heaven. I wouldn't if I could. For I know absolutely nothing about heaven. I've never been there, and probably never shall be there. Nor do I particularly want to be there. 

Singing songs around the throne of God in heaven seems to me to be a somewhat useless and senseless task. Some of us cannot sing. So-if you are looking for just another religious theory-please turn in your Membership Card, and we'll separate company.

I have not a single moment of time to give to advocating another theory of God. I'm not on the earth for that pur-

pose.

What I am here for is to disclose to the human race the staggering fact that all the Power of The Spirit of God is available to all men, here and now. So while I cannot and will not give you just another theory of God, I can and will bring to you something far better. I'll bring to you the actual and literal Power of The Spirit of God. At least I'll show you beyond a shadow of doubt, how you can find and use this magical, superhuman, wondrously beautiful

Power of The Spirit of God.

God cannot bring to you-not "beyond the tomb" but here and now. you.

This revelation of The Spirit of God dawned upon me, and came to me after a life vainly spent in trying to find the Power of God thru the theories of God advanced by the religion I had the fortune or misfortune to be born in. 

That happened to be the Baptist sect. And you know what they are by reputation. “Hard-shelled Baptists" they are sometimes called. 

Well-my good honest parents pumped hell-fire and damnation into me in my early years. They tried to push it down my throat. But it wouldn't go down. It stuck there for many years. When I became old enough to reason things out for myself, my common-sense told me that the way to the Power of God most certainly did not lie down that path. I knew there was some sort of a superhuman 

Power which created the universe and everything in it.

Well-let those believe that who care to. I don't. And don't either. Put them in coldstorage while you are travelling with me. Then, when our journey together is ended, if you care to, you can go back to the cold-storage plant and get your old theories out again. At the beginning of our journey into the Realm of The 

Spirit of God, I shall ask you to do a few things without

This world stands on the verge of the greatest and most momentous spiritual discovery in the history of the human race. Those who travel with me will be among the very first to make this astounding discovery. 

For the next two weeks, until your next Study-Lesson comes to you, I want you to spend 15 minutes a day absolutely alone. You will discover later that you will not be alone, but will have as your guest and companion the greatest and most dynamic Spiritual Power there is in this world—the Power of The Spirit of God. I have a few exercises to give you. They are necessary to the finding of God. 

What is first necessary, however, is that you realize the staggering fact that you could not get away from the Power of The Spirit of God if you wanted to.

Yes, I mean just that. I mean to say to you that it is only your own blighted mind, your own unseeing eyes, clouded by religious superstition and tradition, which keep you from knowing the fullness of the Power of The Spirit of God-now. There is no way I know of whereby I can, in one Lesson, clear those scales from your eyes. They've been there too long. Your hereditary traits have embedded them so deeply into your nature, and your parents' natures, that

YOUR GOD-POWER 

you will have to spend some time in thinking God out. 

All it will take is the proper instruction. You have brains. 

You have intelligence. You're not a fool. You can see the

Light when it shines. All you need is someone to point out the Light to you. To show you where it is. What you have thought to be light, may be darkness, and what you have thought to be darkness, may be light.

So then, we leave preconceived notions of God in coldstorage, and we start out to actually discover and experience, not just another theory of God, but the actual and literal Power of The Spirit of God itself. Only those who know and know well should ever talk to others about the Spirit of God.

But do not go out of your way to talk with anyone, except perhaps your closest friend, about the Pathway to God which you are now treading.

The most precious and priceless Truths of God will be revealed to you in the silence in which God operates. 

There's never any noise about the operations of The Spirit of God. It's as silent as the tomb. Yet the Power which comes out of that silence is staggering. Remember-God 

Almighty is Spirit. And Spirit is not material in any sense of the world. You, as a material being, commune with The 

Spirit of God, and that Invisible Spirit fills you, slowly but surely, with the very essence of The Power which is God. 

You use this Power. You achieve what you will. But until the time comes when you are thrilled, filled, rapturously

YOUR GOD-POWER 

happy with The Spirit of God-keep your mouth closed. 

Don't tell anyone—yet.

I want you to think a great deal about the newly-discovered Power you are conscious of. I want you to continue your affirmation-“I am finding the Power of The Spirit of God". It will be well, whenever you are riding along in your car, all alone, to make this statement out loud. Should you be driving in the country, where none can hear, shout it out at the top of your lungs, and keep on shouting it out. You may not understand fully what is involved, but 

I shall explain that later to you. 

What you will find is that the more often you loudly repeat this statement of fact, the more real will The Spirit of God become to you. So don't hesitate to shout aloud.

There's nothing to be ashamed of. And it is always thru the spoken word that The Spirit of God operates. Always. 

When material man, in communion with The Spirit of God, speaks a fact loudly, or loudly expresses a desire, The Spirit of God cannot fail to respond. Do you believe that? I know it is true. For there is not a single step you will take in your travels with me that I haven't taken long before you took it. 

I know this trail. For sixteen years this religious phenomenon called "Psychiana” has been revealing the Power of The Spirit of God to the human race. So you follow me carefully, and the day will soon come when all the Power and Glory there is in the Realm of The Spirit of God will be available to you.

A FEW AFTER-THOUGHTS TO  

Some years ago I was driving along a country road somewhere among the hills of sunny Southern California.

As usual, my thoughts were on the Power of The Spirit of God. They always are. From the first thing in the morning till bed-time, the one thought which takes precedence over all other thoughts in my life is The Spirit of God and the 

Power there is in that God-Spirit.

Well, this particular day I pulled the Peerless to a stop alongside the road and cut the motor. I wanted to have a few moments quiet communion with God. And I long since learned the priceless treasures of Spiritual Power which flood into the life when it thinks enough of the Spirit of God to deliberately interrupt the cares of the day and take a few moments out, when, the world forgotten, definite communion and conversation can be had with The Great 

Spirit of all creation.

I was pretty high up on the mountain-side that day. On my way up to Mt. Wilson, as a matter of fact. It was in the late afternoon, and a wondrous sun was gilding a floor of fleecy clouds with a tint of gold. I stood there sort of awed at the majesty of the scene. Then, sitting on the runningboard of the car, I closed my eyes and entered into that silence where God is. This realm of the "silence that is 

God" is completely removed from things of this earth. Here 

I was, sitting on a mountain-side, alone with God, and yet, in the twinkling of an eye, I was in an entirely different 

Realm. The Realm of The Spirit of God. 

Well-I stayed there enjoying the spiritual beauties of God for quite a while. And then, just when I was about to continue my journey, the fullness of the Power I am trying to show you how to contact came into my soul without measure. I'm not going to try to describe those moments. 

I have them, but not too often. In any event, there came to me this picture-and for sheer beauty, well, no earthly painter could ever depict such scenes. For they are spirit-

YOUR GOD-POWER 

ual in their entirety. They are from God, of God, and only are revealed to the true mystic who has found the fullness of God, and can enter into that Realm any time he cares to. 

What God said to me in pictures was this:-"Some years from now, about twenty, you will bring to this earth the truths of what I am. You will tell men and women, but greater than this, My Power shall speak thru you, and as a result of your life and ministry, there will be shed abroad on the earth the fullness of My Power. But a great tribulation shall come first. Blood will flow and bathe the whole 

God-less human race. But after this, or during this time of tribulation, I shall speak thru you, and as a result, the human race shall know what it has never known, but could have known, had it but looked within for Me.” 

Then, before my vision, was a train speeding along a track. It was going very fast, and was exceedingly dirty. 

Alongside the train was a beautiful paved highway. The sun was shining on it and trees and wonderful shrubbery were growing alongside of it. It was quite a contrast to the train, for on the train the coaches were jammed. The windows were filled with people looking out. It was a long train, and smoke was pouring from the smoke-stack of the engine, while on the beautiful highway there was not a soul. It was empty. As I sat and looked at the scene, utterly oblivious of where I was, there came into the picture a hand of gold. In that hand was a sign which read: “The time is short-soon the earth shall be filled with the glory of the knowledge of The Spirit of God."

You can never bring these spiritual experiences back once they leave. The true mystic knows better than to try. 

However, the vision faded and I opened my eyes, shouting at the top of my voice: "Glory to God". Now I don't want you to confuse this with the emotional and, most of the

time, fanatical exudations which fall from the lips of those unable to think, at some "old-fashioned" revival meeting. 

I am not an emotional man. I have been called a "coldblooded, hard, business man". That is not true, but in no sense of the word can I be swayed by what anyone says or does or writes.

This particular afternoon, and other afternoons and evenings too, when these visitations from God come to me, I just cannot resist the urge to shout "Glory to God". I'm not talking about Jesus, or Buddha, or Chrishna, or any of the rest of the score of crucified "gods" which are supposed to have descended from heaven-I'm talking about the indwelling Power of The Spirit of God, the Creator of this universe, who lived billions of eons ago, long before man ever appeared on the earth. That God pays no attention to theories of God originated and advanced by theological organizations. That God knows nothing about Jesus. 

I am talking about the indwelling Power which, as I sit at my typewriter here in Moscow, Idaho, gives me the urge to write and tell you how very close God is to you all. This indwelling Spirit which is Life was, that afternoon, a million miles removed from anthropomorphic ideas of God.

There is no connection between the Spirit of God and the religious traditions of the present day. There cannot be. 

The two wouldn't mix. One is the pure Spirit of God, the other has nothing of God in it. It is a man-made, churchoriginated theory of God that never had any truth in it. 

However, what I am trying to say is that not fifteen minutes ago, here in my home-study, this same vision was repeated. But with this difference. The train had come to a stop. The people were getting off. The highway, alongside the track, was scattered with men and women who, looking back at the train, were making their way to the

YOUR GOD-POWER 

beautiful sunshine which pervaded the whole atmosphere of the highway.

I interpret that vision something like this: This world has gone as far as it can go without God. The masses who have looked here for God and there for God, finally deciding that there is no God whose Power is attainable now, had forgotten all about God. Now-these people are coming back. Instead of the road to God being a narrow, steep incline over which very few "pilgrims" shall pass, here it was in all its beauty. The road to God is not narrow, but wide enough to hold the population of the world if they but knew it. And the road I am taking you down is not narrow. It's as wide as God. It can abundantly take care of you and yours, and it will open up to you, as it often opens to me, if you will use your God-given common-sense in dealing with the things of God. you

Now this Realm of The Spirit of God is ever-present. 

I won't anticipate, but will discover as you progress, that whenever and wherever you think enough of God, and the Power of God to deliberately take a few moments out to be with God, there, in that spot, wherever it may be, you will find God.

So then, at the end of this Fourth Lesson, let's be in earnest. Make these periods spent in the presence of God very essential to your whole life. Let nothing disturb them. 

Never give them up. A picture-show or a saloon, or even a church-house has nothing to offer which can faintly compare with the beauties you will find as you travel along this trail with me. This trail, at the end of which you will discover how to find and use the Power of the Spirit of Godin you.

IT IS NOW JUST TWO MONTHS since you began your studies with me. Although two months is not very long when compared with your whole life to date, there have happened many things during the past two months. Enough to convince you beyond the shadow of doubt that this writer knows whereof he speaks when he is dealing with Almighty God.

There has come into your life, first of all, a complete freedom from religious and any other sort of fear. This is always the most noticeable effect and the first effect of the coming of The Spirit of God into a human life. Fear goes out. If you were raised in a home full of "religion", as I was, you doubtless were filled to the brim with the many different theories of God, all of which implanted in your young life the very thing that never should have been implanted there-fear. 

You were probably told that if you did not "fear" God and obey God you would land in a terrible place where the imps of darkness would shovel coal on the fires of "hell" and then delight in the tortures you would experience. 

That, of course, in all probability has never been believed by you, for no man or woman in his or her right mind can digest such a foolish theory of Almighty God. Nevertheless, that wrong impression of God perhaps left in your life a semi-fear of God. You may have felt that, while the hell- 65

YOUR GOD-POWER 

fire doctrine was not true, there might be something else not quite so bad which was true. 

But since you began your studies with me, all such fears have left. If they have not, they soon will. For no man can learn the actual Truths of God and believe in such foolish theories as were taught when you and I were children. So then, the first thing the Power of God does in a human life is cast out fear. And as this sense of freedom from fear comes to you, it brings with it a sense of another freedomfreedom from want. As a matter of fact, it has never been necessary for us to fight cruel, heartless, ghastly wars to establish the famous "four freedoms" of Mr. Roosevelt.

All that is necessary to establish them is to discover and use the Power of The Spirit of God. There you have a much more effective weapon. And it operates without the shedding of the blood of one of our beloved sons. What a pity we do not have leadership which knows personally, in its own life, what The Spirit of God can accomplish here in our own beloved America. Yes-ten thousand pities. But that's how it is. Some day it will all be changed. Man still has to learn thru seas of human blood that God Almighty still lives, and still is the controlling factor in every human life and every national life. 

Not only individuals, but nations will come to see that day. For life without God is incomplete. Nations, without a definite experience of the Power of The Spirit of God are incomplete. And until the world discovers this fascinating, invulnerable Power of God, they too will be incomplete. Another war will follow this one, and still another will follow that one. Wars will never, never be banished by the sword. Only the Sword of The Spirit of God can banish wars. 

Only The Spirit of God can bring the "four freedoms"

to humanity. We all, of course, admire the honest attempts of both Mr. Roosevelt and Mr. Churchill to bring these "four freedoms" to the world, but they are forgetting one thing. They are forgetting God. And they have left God completely out of their reckonings. If Roosevelt and 

Churchill could bring those "four freedoms" to humanity there would be no need of God. These two men would have successfully supplanted God. Of course, no man can supplant God. Many have tried that, and they all have failed.

So then, when the actual Spirit of God begins to be recognized by you in your life, fear goes out, poverty goes out, worry goes out, and everything which is negative in itself begins to disappear. Can you imagine what your life will be like when you come to the end of your studies with me and you have actually found and begun to use The 

Power of The Spirit of God? Of course you can. You have seen in the past two months, just the faintest little bit of The Light that is God. What you need to recognize now, and what you will recognize as the months and years go by, is the staggering fact of the immense Power God has, and the closeness of this Power to you, day and night. As a matter of fact, it is under your own control.

In my home there is a small aquarium, and in it are half a dozen gold-fish. It is a pet hobby of mine to look after those fish when I am at home here in Moscow, Idaho. I feed them, wash the bowl, and change the water every night. Whenever I walk across the room, there will be excitement in the gold-fish bowl. The little yellow fellows will dart round, swim to the top of the water, and if I go close to the bowl, they will open their mouths for food. 

Tonight, while changing the fish water, I stopped dead my tracks. For a thought had come to me, and I'm going in

YOUR GOD-POWER 

to pass that thought along to you, for it will perhaps demonstrate, in a natural sort of way, the immense truth of life and of God, which truth has made this Movement worldrenowned. As I was changing the water, the thought came to me that these little fish were deriving their life from the water in the bowl. A simple thing, of course, but a very fundamental thing too. In other words, those fish swim round in what is really an ocean of life. The water is life to them. Yet it's questionable whether or not these fish even know what water is. Certainly the water doesn't know what the fish are. Neither does it know that the fish are swimming round in it. Neither does it care. The beautiful mountain streams here in Idaho, teeming with Mountain 

Trout, neither know nor care whether fish are in them.

Yet-these mountain streams, like my gold-fish water, are supplying life to the fish in them. 

These fish are literally swimming round in vast quantities of life, yet the fish are not conscious of the life-giving water, nor is the life-giving water conscious of the fish. 

Fish may swim in it if they care to, or not. The water is there. If fish do swim in it-they live. The only way the fish would be conscious of the life-giving water, is if they were taken out of it. Then, they would know that death was at hand.

This simple illustration brings home to us a dynamic truth, and will serve wonderfully to illustrate this Lesson 

Number Five. For you and I, and the whole human race, are like those fish. We, too, are literally drawing our existence from an invisible source of life. We live, in that life. 

We may not be conscious of that fact-very few are. But the fact exists, nevertheless. All round us, even in the air we breathe, there exists a vast ocean of life. This life is invisible, like the water to the fish. Yet, like the fish, we very

you moment by moment derive our very life itself from the great invisible source of life all around us. If think we don't, then let someone hold your head under water and see how quickly the life-supply is cut off. 

Yet we are not, and never have been, conscious of this staggering fact. You don't believe that? All right-let's see just exactly who and what you really are. You call yourself a human being. And you are just that. But let's get down to brass tacks-shall we? Let us see just who and what you really are, and also let us see from whence you derive your life. "Oh-that will always be a mystery", I hear some say. 

Is that so? But tens of thousands of your fellow human beings know that it is not a mystery at all. Now who are you? What are you? Where did you come from, and who or what sustains you as a living entity here on this earth?

I'm talking about you physically now. Some years ago your parents complied with the Law which is called nature's law, or God's Law, for all natural laws are Laws of God. 

They complied with the God-Law governing childbirth, and you came along. You say that it is a perfectly natural process. Granted. But what sort of process was it?—an earthly process or a spiritual process? Were you created just simply a body of flesh? Or are you something more than that? Let us see.

When your parents complied with the Law of God governing childbirth, they had done all they could possibly do. The Law did the rest, and the Law, which is God, created you. When your two parents had joined themselves together in the act of reproduction they had complied with the God-Law governing childbirth. There was then a union of two little germ-cells. The spermatozoa of the male, and the ovum of the female. Your parents had nothing whatsoever to do with that union. All they did was supply the

YOUR GOD-POWER 

two fluids containing the two germ-cells, and cause them to come into contact with each other. They did not have anything to say about which two, out of the thousands of little germ-cells, were to unite. They couldn't have anything to say about that-could they?

But someone had something to say about it. For at that point, after your parents had complied with the law governing childbirth, some other law stepped into the picture and took complete charge of it. Your parents had nothing whatsoever to say about the building processes which took place after they had made their physical contact. Until you were born, they didn't even know whether the child would be male or female. But someone did. For those processes of building a new life were, and still are, processes which no human being could ever duplicate. 

Did it ever occur to you that it is beyond human reason what took place in between the time the sexual contact was made and the delivery of the child nine months later? 

Do you know what happened? Could you do that? Does any physician live who can explain how these marvelous laws, and the marvelous Intelligence behind those laws operate in the birth of that child? They can show the results of the law nine months later. But as for medical or any other sort of science even beginning to duplicate the growth of a child from impregnation of the mother to its birth-well-they cannot do that. Too great a Power is being employed. Too marvelous a Law is in operation. 

Think of all the staggering things this Law has to figure out, or to know, in order that flesh, bones, eyes, ears, etc., be made from nothing in nine short months. For this must be an intelligent law, certainly, or a dog or a cat might be produced instead of a sweet little child. You may call it "nature" if you wish. If you do, then nature must be a

very wonderful thing. But the word "nature" does not quite fill the bill. The accepted sense of "nature" means trees, birds, forests, the stars above, the rivers, the streams, and that sort of thing. But I'm afraid that "nature" won't quite answer the question satisfactorily. It's a bigger power than "nature". It's nature's God. Please grasp that fact. 

Now I shall go back again to your birth. We have seen how some invisible Power took two little germs, and transformed them, over a period of nine months, into a living human soul. And it took some Power to do that. Now let me ask you a question-Do you think that the Power which transformed those two little germ-cells into a human body stopped operating when the child was born? You know better than that. This Power, or Law, or whatever it was did not say to your parents at your birth-"Well-here you are-there's your baby. It can paddle its own canoe from now on." How foolish that sounds, doesn't it? The baby is still alive, isn't it? Much larger now than it was at birth.

So it's evident that the building processes which began at the conception of the mother are still going on in you. In other words, the Power, or Law, or whatever it may be that took those two tiny little germs and changed them into you, is still operating in you. 

I wonder if you will be able to that? I think you will. It's common sense, isn't it? Think it over again. When your parents complied with the Law governing childbirth, they did all they could possibly do towards bringing you into existence. Another, and far greater Power, then stepped into the picture, and finished for your parents what they had started. They themselves couldn't finish the job. grasp

But the job was finished. And finished well, too. Now, once more, may I ask you to carefully grasp what I have said-I repeat it here, for it lies at the heart of whatever

YOUR GOD-POWER 

is to come to you through this Teaching, "Psychiana.” It is a grasping of these phenomenal facts, that has brought to thousands a realization of the Power they now use. 

The Power which brought you into existence and which changed two little germ-cells into a living soul, still exists and operates in you. More than that, you can draw upon this staggering Power for the things you need. If it can work such miraculous work as it has already done in you, it can finish the job.

It can bring to you an abundance of everything you can possibly need while on this earth to make your life abundantly happy, abundantly successful, and, more than that, it can go one step further. It can show you how to use yourself, the Power which gave you life, and which still sustains your life. Now what do you think this Power is? 

Shall I tell you what it is? It is the Power of The Spirit which is God. In other words, the Power which is God, not only created you, but still lives in you. And you, through this dynamic Spiritual Power, can, if you wish to, use it for the accomplishment and achievement of every right desire of your heart. As a matter of fact, the very life principle which is in you, and which gives you the power to live, is the very same life principle which created every living thing in the universe. That Power is God. 

Now what does this make you? We have already seen that this God-Power is invisible. We have seen that it is silent in its operations. You could not hear yourself being born, neither could your parents. This tremendous operation of the God-Law made no noise. Nor could it be seen, could it? That it must be a Spirit of Life is self-evident, for it produced life. Therefore, and mark me carefully here, the life you live here and now-the invisible Power which started to work when your parents complied with the law

little governing childbirth, is the very same thing as the Life- Spirit which created the universe. It may take you a while to grasp that statement fully. But when you do grasp it, it will put into your hands a Power you have never known before. And with this Power you can do what you will.

Think of it. You have thought that God did not exist? 

You have thought that you never could find God? Please don't think that any more. For the Power which is God, is at this very moment operating in you. And if it is, then you have at your disposal, all the Power of the Realm of The 

Spirit of God. You have never known this before. You have never realized it. If you had, your life would be now whatever you wanted it to be. It still can be. And it will be when you learn to use this staggering God-Power for the achievement of the things you so sadly need. God in you. 

God in you for your own use. What a staggering thought.

Yet a perfectly true thought. A reasonable thought. What you have always wanted to believe. 

One of the reasons for the stupendous growth and success of this Movement lies in the fact that it has brought

And should anyone tell you that you were born in sin and shapen in iniquity, therefore having need of his offices to "save" you, just politely inform such a one that the Power which gave you life in the first place, is still operating in you and is abundantly able to take care of you just as long as you will allow it to. Perhaps before I close this Lesson, it may be helpful to you if I open up the Realm of The Spirit of God a little bit, and disclose to you the source of Your God-Power. 

For, if such a Power as we are talking about is available, then it must have a definite source somewhere. It may be "faith" in the wrong thing. And in these studies you will find that the author will definitely and abundantly explain to you and show you, step by step, just what is involved in drawing from the "faucet" which is God, the things you need.

We have seen that the Realm of The Spirit of God is a Realm of immutable, mighty, never-failing Spiritual Law. 

There is absolutely not the slightest chance of your complying with this Law, only to find you have been disappointed. God is immutable Spiritual Law. Invisible-yes. But spiritual dynamic Law just the same. 

I have asked you many times so far, and shall ask you many times yet, to set aside certain moments of the day for your exercises in discovering your God-Power. Now why is this? Why do I ask you to repeat a certain affirmation thousands of times? Let me explain before we close this Fifth Lesson. 

Now, what are you anyway? You are a physical body created according to the Law of God governing the creation of the human race. In a coming Lesson we shall go

This, of course, makes you much more than an animal. course, until you know of this Divine Spirit in you, you will live your life completely in the physical consciousness. 

So far as you are concerned there is no God whose Power can be actually found and used while you live on the earth. 

But you would not be studying with me if you did not know beyond the shadow of a doubt that God lives, and can be found, right here on the earth. What you came to me for was to ask me if I can show you the way to God.

You know by this time that I not only can, but will. 

So then, you are a physical body which has, living in it, all the creative Power there is in The Spirit of God who created that body of yours. Physically, and when dead, your “home”—that "house" in which you live is worth about seventy cents-but-you have now, living in you, a Spirit of such staggering import that all the wealth in the world must fade into insignificance beside this Invisible Spirit of 

God. That Spirit does live in you. If you ever find the 

Power of God on the earth, you'll find it in you. You'll never find it anywhere else.

The difference between you and a church-member who believes in Jesus or some other killed “god” is that you can know the fullness of the Power of God here and now. You can prove, to your entire satisfaction, and beyond your wildest dreams that the Spirit of God does live in you here and now. The poor follower of some theological organization has no hope in this world. His whole hope of ever knowing the Power of God lies "beyond the tomb” and

YOUR GOD-POWER 

even then he's not so sure that he'll ever know God. If some priest or preacher was to tell him he was a "lost soul" he'd believe it.

But I don't have that type of "belief” for my followers. 

They cannot believe in an anthropomorphic "god" and follow after me. For we deal with all the Powers God has and we deal with those Powers now-not "beyond the tomb”. All right then-you are a physical body, created physically by two other human beings, but having in you, consciously, the Power of your Creator-Almighty God. 

Now what am I trying to do when I ask you to get away from the hustle and bustle of the day and be alone with God, shutting out all else? I am simply trying to get you to quieten the physical so that the pathway to the Eternal God in you may be found. When you sit alone and close your eyes, you shut out all pictures of this world. You completely close the physical window. Nothing of the material world can be seen by you.

Then, breathing quietly and easily, you stop your thoughts. As your thoughts must be of physical things when you are looking at the physical world around you, just so then are your thoughts at once transferred to the invisible Realm of The Spirit of God when your eyes are closed, and when you are consciously and intelligently carrying out an exercise which is designed to reveal to you the Power of The Spirit of God-that Unseen Guest who lives in you. 

So you keep quiet. You stop thinking. You make your physical mind as complete a blank as possible. Then what happens? You will discover as you progress in your exercises that as you quieten the physical body and the physical mind, you will approach a Realm which is very different from any 

Realm you have ever known before.

Naturally, when you have forgotten the physical body and the active physical mind which automatically goes with that body, then the other part of you-the spiritual part, is the part which is operating and which comes into fullness in such instances as this. When your physical body is quiet, and when you have completely quit thinking, just quietly and expectantly waiting for some manifestation from the "other self", then do you not see that the Life- Spirit in you can do nothing else than make Its presence known to you? 

God Almighty-that indwelling Spirit-cannot possibly do that while your mind and body are occupied with the material things of this world. There must be those periods when you withdraw, and when you consciously, knowing full well what you are doing, set aside periods of meditation and contemplation on the things which are of God. 

In such moments as these, continued, you enter into that other Realm which is the greater part of you, but you enter into it consciously. 

You deliberately sit alone. You deliberately want the

Power of God to manifest in and thru you. So you definitely set aside certain periods of time for that specific purpose. 

All right. When the physical is quiet, the Power of God begins to move. And that amazing Power does not begin to move somewhere up in the sky, It begins to move in you, where It belongs and where It lives. 

Movements of the Spirit of God "up in the sky" or in someone else's life could be of no particular interest or help to you. It is the movement of the Spirit of God in you which you want—is it not? Of course it is. So then I believe you will grasp what I am after in suggesting these quiet periods and their affirmations. I won't tell you yet why the

YOUR GOD-POWER 

affirmations are necessary. But I will the time being, necessary. say that they are, for

In this Fifth Lesson I want you to understand this one thing. I want you to know that when the physical body and mind are quiet, and you are expecting the Realm of 

God to open up to you, that Realm will open. There's nothing mysterious about it. The Spirit of God lives in you. 

So long as you are busy in your physical body, worrying about this material thing and that material thing, there is no possible way in which the Spirit of God in you can come into your consciousness. But push these material things aside for a while. Get them out of your mind. Concentrate on the indwelling Spirit of God in you, and you'll find out how very quickly you become conscious of the fact that the very important part of you-the real part of you, in you, stands ready to spring into action any time you need it to.

For you do have, in you, all the Power there is in the 

Realm of the Spirit of God. And that Power is under your own control. I don't control it. All I do is show men and women how to find and use this amazing God-Power. 

Sometimes I allude to the indwelling Spirit of God as a “sleeping giant". It is. It lives in you, but it is absolutely essential that you take time out in which to develop your sense of recognition of this Invisible God. Don't ever worry about God not revealing Himself to you when you get in earnest. I never worry about that. The only worry I have in life is trying to get men and women to take enough "time out" to allow the Power of God to actually and literally manifest in their lives.

This world is so sodden with materialism, whiskey, sexsatisfaction, that very few seem to want to find God in them, or anywhere else. This is because our religious or-

ganizations, instead of discovering the Truth about God and teaching that Truth, have handed men and women a theory of God which is as false as hell itself is false, and which was hoary with age thousands of years before their "crucified gods" were ever heard of.

And man, not anticipating that priests and preachers could be lying to him, or could be misinforming him, and being unable to accept the foolish fallacies of the churches, has made up his mind there is no God. And my work is to undo the harm the churches have done. It's a big job. But it is getting done. And it is getting thoroly done, too, for the Spirit of God is behind the undoing of those false theories of God. you are

Now in closing this Fifth Lesson, remember that a dual personality. You are man and you are God, for you have the Spirit of God living in you. To the exact degree that you quieten your physical body and the physical mind that goes with it, to that exact degree will the Invisible Realm of The Spirit of God be made evident to you. 

So, realizing this, be very much in earnest in your quiet hours with God. Force nothing. That is not necessary, and may be harmful. All I ask you to do is to get the sense of the physical completely out of your consciousness. Then, the Realm of God will be where you can begin to enter it. 

But you cannot enter the Realm of your God-Power if your thoughts are on things material. They must be solely and completely fixed on the inner self which is the Spirit of God.

IN OUR LAST LESSON, Number Five, you will recall that we had two glimpses of the living God-Law in actual operation. First, in the gold-fish bowl, and second, in your own life. The main point you grasped from Lesson Five was the amazing fact that the Power which changed two little lifegerms into you, still is operating in you, and still is the Creative Power which created everything in this universe. 

I trust you grasped that significant, staggering statement fully.

Now shall we look around a little bit and see if we can find any more evidences of the actual working and operations of this God-Law in another place or two? Yesterday, while driving thru the country, I watched these Idaho farmers at harvest. The combines were threshing the golden grain. Thousands-yes, millions of ears of it went into those combined harvesters, and in due time you may be eating some of this grain on your table as bread. 

Now just what did I see yesterday? Where did all this beautiful grain come from? Will any more grow next year?

Yes-it will. Some few months ago I saw these same farmers sowing their grain. All they did was put it in the ground and cover it up. Just as simple as that. Had they dug it up to see whether it was growing or not, there would be no harvest now. But these Idaho farmers for years have planted the grain in the spring, and, in the fall, have reaped thousands of ears of it for every single grain they planted. 82

Now why is this? Where does the additional grain come from? How does the farmer know that he'll have another crop next year?

All right-let's see if we can find out. I have here before me two barrels. One contains sawdust, and the other contains soil. Into both barrels I put a few grains of wheat. 

Then I cover them up and wait to see what happens. In a few months, in the barrel containing the wheat and the soil, I shall see little green shoots rearing their tiny heads above the top of the soil. In the barrel containing the wheat and the sawdust, I see nothing. I can wait till the crack of doom, and when it cracks there will be no little green shoots in the sawdust. If I let the wheat and soil stay there, it won't be long until full-grown wheat is growing in that barrel. Now, why is that? Surely the God-Law cannot be working in a barrel of soil with a few grains of wheat in it, can it?

Let's see. Lying before me now are several grains of wheat. They are inert things. They contain no life of any sort. They certainly are not possessed of reasoning, thinking ability. They don't have the power to think, and I leave them here on my desk. They will still be here as grains of wheat a thousand years from now. So we can truthfully say that the grains of wheat possess no possess no intelligence of any sort. 

Well-maybe the intelligence which causes the ripe ears of grain to grow is in the soil-maybe that's it. Do you think it is? I don't. For thousands of acres of soil have lain on this earth for millions of years and no wheat has grown there. It would have grown had the wheat grains been planted in it. But they were not, so there was no harvest. 

By no stretch of the imagination can one say that the soil is possessed of sufficient intelligence to change one grain of wheat into a thousand grains. Soil, as such, cannot do

YOUR GOD-POWER 

that. For it, like the grains of wheat, possesses no thinking ability of any sort. Neither the soil nor the grains of wheat know anything about chemistry. They are both inert substances, possessing no power whatsoever. Yet the wheat grows in the soil and not in the sawdust. 

Now shall we take another example? In my back yard here in Moscow, Idaho, sitting on top of a clothes-line pole, is a blue-bird's nest. It's been there for years. Every spring, two little blue-birds come to my back yard, and build their nest on the top of the same clothes-line pole. They mate, little tiny blue eggs appear, and in due time, four or five little blue-birds will be born to that couple of parents. 

Now the eggs which are laid are a combination of calcium salts and albumin. Not a very scientific statement but one which will understand. Let me ask you-do you think you that either the calcium salts in the egg shells or the albumin inside has enough thinking ability to think themselves into blue-birds? "Of course not"-I hear you say. But little blue-birds will come from those eggs just the same, and 

I'm asking you how you account for it? You must not answer me by saying, “Oh—just nature,” because we are trying to get behind nature to nature's God. We are trying to find out just what happens.

So commonplace have the harvests here become, and so commonplace is the laying of an egg, that these things have been considered too unimportant to notice. Yet what better place could there be to find the Author of life than by studying life itself? Let's not pass up these simple everyday happenings, because, thru them, we may find God.

Certainly the Power of the Life-Spirit must be operating in the case of both the blue-birds and the wheat. For we have seen that neither soil, nor wheat, nor eggs, are, in themselves, capable of producing new life. And new life,

in different forms, is produced. It was produced in you as you saw in your last Lesson. You may say that it was necessary for the blue-birds to sit on those eggs for a certain length of time before life manifested. But that is not true. 

Hen's eggs are placed in incubators and new chickens are born without the hen ever seeing the eggs after she has laid them. So that is not the answer.

Well, let's look a little further and then see what we can find. My little daughter, Florence, a sweet little thing if ever there was one, asked me to buy her a few packets of seeds not long ago. I brought them home and gave her a few tiny flower-pots. She filled the flower-pots with soil (the same soil that wheat grows in) and put them out in the back garden. Into the soil she placed the seeds. Five or six different kinds of seeds. What happened? In a little while her flowers began to peep through. In each pot a different flower. Why was that? Because she put different seeds in each pot. The pansy seed didn't grow a marigold.

The marigold seed didn't grow a pansy. They each grew their own kind and sort. Did the seeds know what they were supposed to grow? Did the two blue-birds know, by their own intelligence, how to bring tiny blue-birds from their eggs? Why did not four or five scorpions appear from the blue-bird's eggs? 

Foolish-is it? Not quite so foolish as you may at first think. For there was an Intelligence manifested there which absolutely prohibited scorpions ever coming from blue-birds' eggs. And this same Intelligence absolutely prohibits pansies growing from marigold seeds. And it also prohibited a cat or a dog from being born instead of you.

It must be some Power. It is. It's the very Power I want you to know how to use. For you can use it. Moreover, if it is of such a high degree of intelligence that it can change

YOUR GOD-POWER 

one grain of wheat into a thousand, and if it is of such a high order of intelligence that it can create little blue-birds from the eggs of the parent birds, it's a power so great that you cannot understand it. Can you do that? Can any human being alive duplicate such marvelous operations?

I don't think so. Then the Power, whatever it may be, is not a human Power. There's only one other Power it can be.

Shall we go a little further along this line and see what we can discover? Half a dozen homing pigeons, placed in a shipping crate in Hartford, Conn., are loaded into an express car, railroaded all one day and the next night, and taken out the second day at Salisbury, N. C., and set free.

They circled a few times, and then headed directly back into Hartford. How did they determine their direction?

A dog is carried in an automobile 20 miles and left. He has never made the journey before in his life. He cannot read the road-signs. He does not know the names of the towns. He has no knowledge of the geography or the lay of the land. Yet on the second day, he is back home, travelworn and hungry, and tired. How did it find its way home?

A cat is placed in a basket and carried 12 miles and left in a new home. It has never been half a mile from the barn in which it was born. Yet in 24 hours the cat came back home again. How did it find its way home?

The bee wanders in the woods in search of nectar, gets two miles from his hive, loads up, circles once or twice, and strikes a "bee-line" for his hive. How does he determine direction so unerringly, and how does this bee differentiate other hives to the north, east, south, and west? 

To my knowledge, none of these creatures possesses an organ devoted to direction finding. Their sense organs appear to correspond to our own. Yet we cannot approach

them in direction finding. Our only contact with the world is through one or more of five senses-sight, hearing, smell, touch, and taste. A person born without any of them could never know he was not the only human being in existence. 

If the dumb creatures, too, are limited to five senses, which ones are used in determining direction? Not the sense of sight, for they do not see their distant homes beyond the mountains, forests, buildings, or even the horizon itself. 

Not the sense of hearing, for homes make no sound that reaches out several miles to be caught by even their most sensitive ears. Obviously it is not the sense of touch. 

Can there be some sort of an animal radio system? Certainly there operates some sort of a Power which can do things for animals, things which we humans cannot do. 

And this invisible Power is what we are trying to find. 

You will with me, agree

Lesson who have known nothing but sorrow and regret and poverty all their lives. It seems to be their "lot” in life.

No tranquillity nor peace in the lives of thousands who have written to me for the truths of God.

THE THING I LIKE BEST about the Realm of The Spirit of 

God is the fact that there is no such thing as "faith" in the whole Realm. You don't have to "believe" anything. The

Power of the Spirit of God proves Its own existence. It is not a case of “believing" something here and getting all the benefits of that “belief” after we die. That has always seemed very unsatisfactory to me. I have never been able to understand why religion insisted on confining the operations of God to the questionable state we may or may not enter after we die. 

Surely if Almighty God lives, the Power of God can be manifested here and now, right here on this earth, when we need the Power so very much and when we can enjoy it to the very full. No one knows what goes on after we die. 

It may be that if we can only come to know the fullness of the Power of God down here, death will not be at all necessary. It doesn't fit into the picture, take it any way you will. It never can fit into the picture. The Power and the existence of Almighty God cannot be fully manifested so long as there is physical death on the earth. 

For death is the opposite of life. And Life is God. Therefore, death and God cannot live together. Where the Spirit of God is there is Life. And Life is Life and not death in any sense of the word. The two are at swords' points. We may, then, state with absolute truth, the fact that the more 94

we come to know of the Power of the Spirit of God here on the earth, the farther we shall get from death. 

I don't believe there is a man alive who has seen more manifestations of the Power of the Spirit of God than this work has disclosed to me. Wherever the Power of God comes in contact with illness, it is perfectly possible for that illness to disappear at once. I have seen this so many times. I have in my files authentic cases of Life coming again to a physically dead body, thru some Student of mine who had learned his lesson well. To me, that is entirely and completely possible. The trouble is to find the man or woman who will give time enough and study enough to the things of God to bring to that one some larger measure of the Power of God. you

Certain it is that if, during your studies with me, you should be taken physically ill, the indwelling Power of the Spirit of God, once called upon and thrown into actual operation, will drive that illness so far from that you'll never know you had it. Of course, the man or woman who finds a large measure of the Power of the Spirit of God is the man or woman who, probably thru trial and tribulation, has had born in him or her a consciousness of the present-abiding Spirit of God which is so real that neither time nor eternity can take it away.

This morning I was in conversation with one of the professors at the University of Idaho. He is a very clever bacteriologist, and, like myself, he is interested in discovering how the Power of God can banish this phenomenon called death. In the course of the conversation he said to me: "Dr. Robinson-just how much faith in God have youyou must have a lot?" Here is my reply: "Dr. H——, there is not a single thing I know of that I would not do in and thru the Power of the Spirit of God in me-there is nothing
