I can trust God for that could ever bring failure on the part of God to manifest."

That's how I feel about God. Thru fifty-eight years I have had my trials. I have had my sufferings. I have known what it means to "tread the winepress alone." Yes—I have known what it meant to have the whole Christian world opposed to me, trying to eliminate me from this earthly picture. I have also known what it means to stand there, firm in the knowledge that the Spirit of God is in me, and defy every attempt to upset me. I have known in the past, and I know now, what it means to have no one else to lean on, except the indwelling Spirit of God. And, as a testimony to the Power of God, there is no situation which can possibly arise which I cannot overthrow thru the Power of the Spirit of God.

This is where I am trying to lead you. I want you to get to the place where you recognize the fact that you can do anything necessary thru the Power of God in you. Did it ever occur to you that God Almighty cannot make His Power and Presence known to this world in any manner other than a material manner? We love to sing about the glories which await us "up there." But what about the glories which can be ours down here? It is the very same 

God, is it not? Or are we to believe those who tell us that half a handful of Roman soldiers killed Almighty God on a cross? If we believe that, we can have but little hope of ever finding the actual and literal Power of God down here. 

But that is a long way from the Truth, my friend. God lives. God lives but for one purpose. That is to make known to mankind His own existence here and now. All the Power of Almighty God awaits you-not “in heaven,” but now.

Right here. Did it ever occur to you to ask yourself just why it is that God cannot manifest Himself and His Power

to us here and now? If you ask yourself that question you automatically have the answer. God can. But why then does not God? Simply because we have been taught by theology that God cannot do a blessed thing for us either materially or spiritually until we cross the Styx.

If it is something else you need in your life apart from the healing of yourself or someone else, the procedure will be essentially the same, but, of course, there will be no laying on of the hands. Instead, there will come to you, when your connection with the Spirit of God is as bright as it should be, a very definite leading to do a certain thing or see a certain person. This leading will come direct from the Spirit of God in you. You will know it, and you must obey it instantly. If you do not, you will forget all about it and it will never return.

When the Spirit of God is ready to manifest something

YOUR GOD-POWER 

you need, It will first advise you thru your inner spiritual perception. The time will then be ripe. But you must follow that leading. For, when you get that leading, you will automatically know that the Spirit of God is ready to act, and act thru you. So, once you have recognized that leading, follow it. If it should be, as often happens, that you do not at first recognize the leadings of the Spirit of God pertaining to that matter, that Spirit will continue to offer you that leading. It will offer it to you repeatedly. Time and time again. As a matter of fact, the Spirit of God will keep on knocking at your inner consciousness until such time as It feels there is little more use. Then It will leave and you will never accomplish what you want to. You will have refused to recognize It. 

You will see by this that it is absolutely necessary for you to keep on the qui vive at all times. Keep those affirmations running thru your mind continually. Keep in earnest. The more you are in earnest the surer will the manifestations be. Remember this. Remember also that it is utterly impossible for God to fail you, if you know what you want, realize the Spirit of God in you, and direct that 

Spirit in the proper channel.

Not only does this apply in any emergency, it applies in your every act of life. It applies in your everyday life. If you never let the consciousness of the indwelling Power of God leave you, I can assure you that your life, linked to the Power of God, will be a phenomenal life. 

It will be a phenomenal life in that it will be surcharged with the Power of the Spirit of God. That means, all the wisdom, all the intelligence, all the power, all the peace, all the love there is in this vast invisible creative Realm of 

God will be yours. That, I believe, is sufficient to make your life so radiantly beautiful and powerful that when

you look back on your life to date, you'll admit that you have not been living at all.

The question will arise as to just how the invisible Power of God in you can be changed into material physical power. Please follow me closely here, for it is a point which causes many to stumble. They cannot grasp the con- nection which exists between the invisible Power-Realm of God and their present physical material life. There should be no trouble in grasping this, but there is. All this trouble arises from a tendency to couple up the invisible Power- 

Realm of God with emotion and feeling.

Once we realize that the Realm of the Spirit of God is a Realm completely devoid of feeling or emotion, we begin to place the Power of God in its proper perspective. The point to grasp is that while the Realm of God is charged and supercharged with Power, that Power is silent, motionless, emotionless, and completely devoid of feeling. There is no emotion or feeling in electricity. Yet it can kill you, and, being a law, will kill you every time you come into the correct voltage. You cannot get away from the law which is electricity. There is no way you can stop it from killing you under the proper conditions. 

And so in the Realm of the Spirit of God. That Realm is a Realm of Spiritual Law. It cannot fail. No emotion. 

No feeling. Just creative, spiritual God-Power. When you spend certain hours of the day in silent communion with God-when you keep your affirmations constantly in your mind, you are, in reality, making the channel clear thru which the Power of God can manifest in your life. Remember, the Power is invisible, emotionless, feelingless, but powerful in the extreme. The human being who is on this trail to the Power of God, and who is so much in earnest that he or she is doing what these Lessons pre-

YOUR GOD-POWER 

scribe, makes the pathway clear for the Spirit of God to transmit the Power in it, into actual and physical or material manifestation. 

It is a sense of inner spiritual perception which first comes to every soul who is in earnest enough to desire it to come, and to work for it to come. Inner spiritual perception or awareness-that's what it is. Remembering here that there is neither feeling nor emotion in the Realm of God, the Power of God comes to one thru this quiet inner sense of spiritual perception. And then, once that is established, you personally are the means thru which this silent, emotionless, feelingless Spiritual Power is changed into material things. You are the one who, charged and supercharged with the emotionless, feelingless Spirit of God, brings into physical and material existence the things you so much need.

Because the Power of God is invisible, feelingless, and emotionless, you are very apt to make the mistake of thinking that It cannot possibly exist in you. This is a mistake. 

But, believing this Power cannot exist unless you can feel it, you do not use it at all. And you lose. But when you know that the invisible Spirit of God in you is so very powerful, and when you forget all about feeling or emotion, then you are in the true place to bring the material things you need into actual manifestation. For the Spirit of God in you will create whether you know the processes or not, and whether you are conscious of Its creating ability or not.

So then, once your mind realizes the fact that the emotionless, feelingless Spirit of God is in you, you yourself throw this amazing Power into creation by your own physical efforts. The difference between you and the man who is trying to do something without the Power of God is this-

you know that the Power of the Spirit of God is working in and thru you, but he doesn't know this. Now then, completely without feeling or emotion, your reason tells you that the Spirit of God is in you, and will work thru you, in whatever manner you desire it to, and whenever you direct that Power as you would have it go.

It can all be summed up in a nutshell. You live your life in the conscious knowledge that the Power of the Spirit of God lives in you, and is abundantly able and willing to manifest that Power in and thru you, in any necessary manner. The "other fellow" does the best he can, and often succeeds without ever thinking of God. Many fail too. But, if that "other fellow" were to have conscious individual knowledge of the fact that Almighty God, the Creator of everything, lives in him, and has all the Power possible instantly available, then do you not see how much more that chap could accomplish with the consciousness of the 

Power of God in him? I'm sure you do.

It will be necessary for you to completely push out of your mind all reference to or thought about gods being killed on crosses for the salvation of the race. You will need to completely eradicate from your consciousness, all thoughts that you are a "sinner, standing in the need of prayer." The only thing you stand in need of is the Spirit of God, and that Spirit exists and lives in you. How foolish then for you not to take full advantage of this amazing 

Spirit of superlative Power.

Inner spiritual perception is brought about over the weeks and months and years, by setting aside every day, a period of time in which you keep very quiet and commune with the God in you. Like learning to play the organ or piano, days and weeks of finger exercises are necessary. In the 

Realm of the invisible Spirit of God, days, weeks, and

YOUR GOD-POWER 

months of quiet daily communion are necessary if you are to be able to manifest the Power of God whenever you want to, and for whatever purpose (good) you want to. 

The Power is there. It lives in you. To realize this fact is to make you a living dynamo of the Power of God. Yet you can walk down the street and no one will have the faintest suspicion of the Power you have in you. But let them start dealing with you, or talking to you, and it will not take them very long to know that there is a "something" about you which they realize to be of power, but cannot explain. 

You won't go chasing round to every evangelistic service you see. You won't go round telling men and women that 

Jesus has washed your sins away and given you a “home in heaven." None of that. But you will go thru life manifesting the Power of God in everything you do, and what you are able to do will speak far more eloquently than anything you can say. So it is very necessary that you remember that the Power of God in you is emotionless, feelingless, but dynamic in the extreme. 

Also remember this. You will never know how the Power of God operates. You will never see It operate. You will never know the methods employed. You will see the results. You will be able to create for yourself everything you need, but you will never know the processes employed by the Spirit of God to bring these things to you. You will never "feel" the Spirit of God in operation. Not until you go to work, with the serene consciousness of the fact that the Spirit of God does live and operate in you, will you have evidence that It is operating at all. 

But carry that consciousness of God with you and watch things get out of your way. Watch sicknesses disappear. 

Watch that good job show up. Watch that money come to you. Watch that domestic unhappiness disappear. You

won't know how, and you won't care how, but it will go, and you will be the one who, thru the indwelling Power of the Spirit of God, will make it go. You will just simply, and probably all unconsciously, throw the invisible Power of God in you against these things, and the negative thing does not exist which can stand against the Power of the 

Spirit of God in you. Actually, all negative things can only exist in the absence of God. 

Forget emotion. Forget feeling. Concentrate on the emotionless, feelingless, powerful God-Law in you. You can win the world if you choose to. At least you can win your own battle of Life. You can so submerge your physical self into the Realm of the God-Self, that the predominating efforts of your life shall be the things which are God, and then the things which pertain to God. Do not mistake silence and lack of emotion for lack of Power. It is in the silence which is God the Power lies. Find that Power. Use that Power. 

Help me make it known to the whole world.

MANY YEARS AGO I wrote a little Xmas story which had a very wide distribution. There is something in this simple little story which seems to go right to the heart, so I have decided to include it in this book. It points very clearly to what these Lessons in Truth are emphasizing, and it discriminates clearly between the actual existence and Power of God and obsolete theories of God. 

Until humanity as a whole realizes and recognizes the existing Power of God, and sees the actual Truths of God, systems of religion will consider me their enemy. This is rather a ludicrous situation. The Spirit of God has seen fit to reveal the liberating Truths of God to the human race thru this humble writer and his Movement. Yet the very organizations which are supposed to be dealing with the Truths of God say that I am teaching something that is not true, they, of course, having all the Truth. 

Until such time comes then, when the human race as a whole finally comes to see what is Truth, I shall have to be considered an interloper in the Realm of the Spirit of God. But that is as it ever was. The church always crucifies a prophet. If religion as it exists today did not attack me, I'd be halfway inclined to think there was some truth in it. 

But when it does attack me, I know I am true. There is no way the Truths of God can be brought to humanity and the one who brings them meet with the approval of the churches. 124

If they had the truth there would be no need for Almighty God to speak thru me. Not having the truth, but being sodden thru and thru with obsolete and many times pagan theories of God, naturally they attack the very Truths of God Almighty, and they will continue to attack these Truths no matter who brings them, until the time when public opinion, and the growth of the Truths of God forces them to admit their error. I understand perfectly, and have never felt the slightest resentment. 

Let us get to this little story, though. It is very pertinent to our book, as it shows the difference between the actual truths of God and the myths of antiquity. Here it is:- 

GOD'S CHRISTMAS PRESENT 

It was Christmas Eve. Outside in the silent, starry night, the snowflakes were falling fast, silently, and as the hours sped slowly by the heavy white blanket Mother Nature was depositing upon the earth increased in thickness. Perhaps she knew that additional covering would be necessary to protect her this chilly night. All was silence among the backwoods, except, perhaps, for the snapping of a twig, or the hooting of an owl, or the snarl of a coyote. 

Inside the little cabin, miles from anywhere, nestling as it did close to mother earth, amid this wondrous Christmas beauty, all was warmth and happiness. The blazing log fire sent up wisps of smoke, and gathered round this Christmas hearth were the entire family of Jacques Chauvet, pioneer backwoodsman of the northern Ontario forests. A little 

Christmas tree stood on the table in the corner of the large living room, and from it hung the little stockings, and toys of almost every conceivable kind. Tinsel decorated the walls of the cabin, and dangling from the suspended lamp

YOUR GOD-POWER 

in the middle of the room was a sheaf of holly. Every picture in the room was draped with pine needles, and altogether the atmosphere was typically Christmas.

Jacques had worked all this day, for he was under contract to produce so many ties for a railroad company, the only railroad which penetrated into the heart of that vast Canadian forest. But the day's work was over, and the dinner dishes had been washed and put away, for this house, humble though it was, was kept spotlessly clean. 

Jacques was a powerful type of man. He stood six feet two inches tall, and weighed nearly two hundred pounds. Born of French-Canadian parentage, he had learned to love those woods, for he had grown up in them, and his superb physical frame gave evidence of what a clean life lived with nature and nature's god can do. 

Mrs. Chauvet was a typical little French-Canadian lady, and to her, of course, the sun rose and set on Jacques. She was a frail little thing, and much in contrast to her powerful, brawny husband, yet she had brought into the world two lovely little children, who, on this Christmas Eve, were sitting on pine boughs before the log fire. The older of these two children, Charles, was a romping, rollicking type of a boy, and in his cheeks showed the health that only the pine forest of northern Ontario can give. It would be difficult to choose a favorite child in that home, although the second one was a little two-year-old baby girl. She was a bright little tot, though, and seemed to comprehend something of the meaning of Christmas. At least she knew something unusual was happening all around her. Santa Claus was going to be good to that little tot, though, and already he had sent in advance a little musical rattle with which she played there on the hearth.

Jacques reached for his black briar pipe, and filling it

with Hudson Bay Company's strong, though sweet tobacco, lighted this huge pipe and settled himself at ease in the armchair of his own building. All the furniture in that home had been hewn from pine trees by Jacques himself, assisted, of course, by little Charles. A copy of a magazine containing a Christmas story was close at hand and thinking to enjoy the reading of this story, Jacques relaxed and prepared to enjoy this Christmas Eve at peace with the world, for this good French-Canadian backwoodsman owed no man anything. He owned two acres where the little cabin stood in the forest, and he made a fairly tidy living by contracting with the railroad company for a certain number of ties each year. The little neighboring town of Hogan, twenty miles away, supplied his grocery and clothing needs, which were very meager, for game and fish abounded in this northern Ontario country. A beautiful trout stream ran thru the woods, and on Saturday afternoon Jacques would take little Charlie and they would fish until such time as they had caught sufficient to tide them over the week-end. Grouse, partridges, and deer were all plentiful in this forest, and these contributed to no little extent in reducing the grocery and meat bills in the little trading post in Hogan.

Jacques was enabled thru his frugal nature to build a comfortable little bank account, which he kept for a rainy day. Perhaps he kept it for the advent of another youngster, but he had the sum of $1700 safely stored in the bank at Belleville, two hundred and fifty miles away. His pay checks were deposited by mail, and he issued checks for his needs at Hogan.

Suddenly thru the stillness of the night outside, broken only by the crackling of the pine logs on the hearth, the wail of the coyote was heard outside the door. "Them var-

YOUR GOD-POWER 

mints seem to be getting braver and braver," said Mrs.

Chauvet, noticing Jacques prick up his ears at the sound. "Perhaps they know it is Christmas Eve," said little Charlie. "Yes, maybe they do, son," said Jacques. "But what is Christmas Eve for, Daddy?” asked the little fellow, while the two-year-old on the floor, with her tiny little hands flapping the air, said, “Krismass-Krismass.'

Jacques was not a very religious man as far as orthodox ideas on religion go, but he was the type of a man that lived a natural life, and enjoyed this life to the very full. He came of Catholic parentage, but he had made his own way in the world for so long that he gave very little attention to creeds or teachings of any religious denomination. Instinctively he knew that the little kiddies playing before him this Christmas Eve also had a creator which must be behind the physical creation. As he saw the grouse and quail and the partridges building their nests and homes in the woods, and as he saw them supplying their young with food, he was intelligent enough to know that somewhere beyond it all was a great Creative Intelligence of some sort. 

Being alone with nature, and nature's god, Jacques probably was in a better position to understand the secrets of nature and nature's god than most men were. He had nothing to distract his attention from these marvelous manifestations of the god of nature. He earned an honest living by doing an honest day's work, and as far as the future went, he provided reasonably against it, and paid not very much thought to any other life than the one he was living now. Some few months back an itinerant travelling preacher had attempted to conduct a revival campaign in the little town of Hogan, and, yielding to the persuasions of his wife, he had hitched up the team to the bobsleigh and

driven in to Hogan to hear this preacher man. At the end of the service the usual call for "converts" was sent out, but no one responded. After the service the preacher had attempted to interest Jacques in the subject of what he called "your soul's salvation." Jacques' reply was quite typical of the man, and he very effectively silenced the travelling preacher. "I don't know what it is all about, and neither do you, but I am perfectly satisfied in resting my soul's salvation in the hands of the great Creator who gave me the soul in the first place," was Jacques' reply. This in general was this gaunt backwoodsman's attitude toward religion. As religious periodicals had drifted to him thru the mails, he carefully read them all, but invariably cast them to one side, choosing to live alone with nature and nature's god, as he was doing. 

Thoughts of religion on this Christmas Eve somehow or other seemed to come back to Jacques, perhaps thru little 

Charlie's remark as to what Christmas meant. Mrs. Chauvet at this point took up the conversation and told the kiddies the well-known story of the shepherds abiding with their flocks by night in the fields near Bethlehem. She told of the hosts of angels which descended, bringing to these shepherds the message of "Peace on earth-good will to men. There is born to you this day a saviour." The little kiddies listened with rapt attention to the story as unfolded by the mother, when, much to her disgust perhaps, Jacques interpolated with the question, “But you don't really believe that story, do you?" "Well, I don't know whether I believe it or not. I have always heard that it was true, though," replied Mrs. Chauvet. "Well, let us ask little Charles what he thinks about it, for you can usually get the truth from the mind of a child,"

YOUR GOD-POWER 

said Jacques, and turning to the little nine-year-old boy, he said, "What do you think of that story, son?" "Well, but where did these angels come from?” said Charles. "They are supposed to have come out of heaven," replied Jacques. "Well, where is heaven?" asked the insistent little Charles. 

The mother interpolated once more by stating that heaven was on the other side of the stars, and above the sky. "But what is the sky?” asked Charles.

At this point Mrs. Chauvet sensed that she was getting into rather deep water, and made an attempt to evade answering this question. Jacques, however, who was always open to know the truth, encouraged the little fellow to continue his questions, with the result that before the conversation was over, the conclusion had been reached that there could be no being of any sort living in any realm beyond the stars. "It sure seems hard to understand,” said

Mrs. Chauvet, at which little Charles asked the question a child naturally would ask, and he wanted to know who made God and who put Him in the skies.

At this point the conversation was interrupted by a heavy knocking on the door. This was very unusual, for visitors in those northern parts were few and very far between. "Wonder who that is?" said Jacques. “Maybe someone lost in the woods," suggested Mrs. Chauvet. "Well, we will go and see," said Jacques, as he laid down his pipe and opening the door, swung it wide open. A curious figure attired in the garb of a woodsman met his view, and this curious figure was invited inside. On throwing off his mackinaw and removing his wool cap, the fea-

tures of a young man were displayed. He was about the same build as Jacques, and his face showed evidences of good home surroundings, and probably educational advantages. "Where do you come from at this time of night?" queried

Jacques. 

The man replied that he was a member of a hunting party and had somehow or other become separated from his companions. As those who get lost in the woods do, the visitor had walked around in circles until finally he had spied the light from the cabin of Jacques in the clearing. 

A cup of hot coffee and Mrs. Chauvet's well-known flapjacks were soon provided, and as the visitor thawed out he proved to be a conversational sort of a chap. Little 

Charles took to him instinctively and began to pursue his questions regarding Christmas and the angels, and God up in the skies, etc. To the amazement of Mrs. Chauvet, the visitor said, taking little Charles on his knee, "But surely you do not believe there is any God in the sky, do you, son?" "No, I don't believe that," said Charles, "because who made Him and who there?" put Him up

Mrs. Chauvet was a little indignant and a little resentful, perhaps, at the strange ideas brought into that home by the visitor. Jacques, however, was interested, and persuaded the visitor to give his ideas of God. The visitor seemed quite at home handling this subject, and Jacques, his pipe once more aglow, listened with rapt attention as every word fell from the stranger's mouth. "You know, folks, I was born and raised in a large city.

My father was a minister and I was sent to college to be trained also for the ministry. There in college I was told the same story that you have been discussing tonight, and

YOUR GOD-POWER 

years for several I tried to persuade others to be converted. 

But as I grew older and traveled around considerably, I began to question the truth of the story the theological seminary had taught me, so I stopped preaching it." 

At this point, little Charles could not resist the tempta- "And what did you do then?" tion to say, Whereupon the stranger smiled and said, "Listen, little man, and I will tell you a story. Some years ago I had a similar experience to the one that has happened to me tonight. This time I was hunting in the woods alone, and, like I did today, I lost my way. The night was bitter cold, and there was nothing to break the stillness, save the hooting of the owls and the occasional scream of the coyote or the wolf. It was a far more desolate country than this, and 

I was reaching the stage of exhaustion, although physically I am very strong. I wandered round and round, and seemed to sense an enemy close behind. It was a strange sensation, but some unseen Power seemed to warn me of danger. 

Turning in my tracks as quickly as a flash, I saw three loping forms with their red tongues lolling out of their mouths, and fast approaching to me. Wolves. I had shot my last round of ammunition on the hunt, and there I was with what looked like instant death near by, and without ammunition, of course, I was positively helpless against the attack of those three hungry timber wolves. I thought rapidly, and my boyhood training came back to me, and, while not at all afraid, I seemed to sense that unless something intervened, it would only be but a few moments before this hungry pack of wolves would leap upon me and tear me to pieces."

Intense silence reigned in the little cottage at this point. 

Mrs. Chauvet, thinking she knew what was coming next, said, "Did you pray?"

"That is what I was going to tell you about," the stranger replied. "Of course I thought about praying, but having no confidence in the story of God as taught to me by my parents and in theological seminary, I did not fall to my knees and ask this great being in the sky to help me, and yet I was helped and got safely out of danger with no difficulty whatsoever, for I seemed to know that the story I had been told and asked to preach was not as true as it might be. 

I seemed to know that far beyond any story of that description there must exist another and far greater Power than could ever be embodied in any miraculously-born man of any kind. I also knew that many, many years before these angels appeared on the plains of Bethlehem that there had been many other experiences like that. I had discovered that these angels and the three wise men had also appeared many hundreds of years before, announcing the birth of other gods, and this, of course, made me lose faith in the religion I was taught in college." 

Mrs. Chauvet did not know what was coming next, although this information the stranger had just vouchsafed to her was entirely new. She thought the only time the wise men and the angels appeared was on that starry night two thousand years ago on the plains of Bethlehem, and to hear that the same story had been told many times before concerning the advent of other saviours was information certainly to her. So she listened with rapt attention as the visitor continued. "So, you see, I could not pray to someone in whom I had no confidence, and I had always been conscious of the fact that there must be an overwhelming, surrounding Presence, which Presence could and would protect me from all harm. So instead of getting down in the snow and praying to God to save me from those wolves, I stood erect.

YOUR GOD-POWER 

I turned in my tracks, and with just perhaps a faint word directed to the great Creative Intelligence behind this universe, I said, “Those wolves will never get me.' I folded my arms and waited, and what do you think happened?” "Did the wolves come and get you and eat you up?” asked little Charlie. "No," the visitor replied, "they didn't eat me up, or I couldn't be here now. But listen and I will tell you what did happen, although I really do not know what did happen. Those three wolves stood there gaping at me with open mouths for a few moments, and then slunk away, and I have never seen them since."

This story created marked silence upon the listeners, and finally Jacques opened the conversation once more. "Well, stranger, what you really did was to have confidence in the Creative Power that made you and that made these woods, and that made those wolves, didn't you?" "Perfectly correct,” replied the stranger, “although I do not know to this day, and do not want to know what that Power is. I am afraid that if I did know, I would be able to understand it and comprehend it, and then, don't you see, if I did that, there would be no need of this great Power at all. If I could understand it and its workings, I would not need it, and I would know as much as it knows, and therefore it would be of no use to me." 

Mrs. Chauvet saw the logic of the statement made by the visitor and said to him, "Well, do you mind telling me if you thought this Power was God?" To which the stranger replied, "Of course it is God. I have learned to know that these stories told to us of unnatural births and angelic visions are not only man-made stories, perfectly honestly made though, but still only man-made traditional stories. 

This is Christmas Eve, and I see that this little home is

celebrating the eve of the anniversary of the hero of one of these stories, and I hope you have a good time, but, you see, you can understand that story, for you can understand a baby being born in a manger, and you can understand angels coming down and announcing his birth, and therefore because you can understand that story it is useless to you and to me. My idea of God is a great invisible Power in whom we live and move and have our being. This great 

Power is the Spirit of life. Yes, it is more than that—it is life itself. That little baby there on the hearth cannot swing that rattle unless the Power of this great invisible Spirit permits it to do so. The snowflakes fall outside because of this great Power, also, and let me tell you something else. 

The reason I did not pray when the wolves were after me was because this great unseen Power is a law, and, being a law, I know that if I complied with the law, nothing could harm me."

Jacques, at this point had agreed with every word the visitor had said, and he was overwhelmed with the desire to know how to comply with this great God-Law. The stranger continued and soon came to that. He pointed out that the law covering the great unseen Realm of God was a law of simple trust. He explained to this backwoods family that if he had shown the slightest fear as to the outcome of his encounter with the wolves, he would not have been there then, telling them this story. He was very emphatic in trying to make them understand that at no time must they endeavor to comprehend in human terms just what this great Power was or how it operated. He pointed out that the Spiritual Realm, or the realm of the invisible God, was so far above this realm that a human mind could not grasp it. What a human mind could do would be to so prove this Spiritual Law that he could speak with absolute

YOUR GOD-POWER 

certainty as to its operations when the Law was complied with. He pointed out that it would be utterly foolish for any finite being to attempt to comprehend the existence of the great Infinite Being, and before his visit was over this family had entered into an entirely new and much more real conception of God. Christmas Eve that night took on a new meaning for them. As it had been, this "saviour" of theirs had been cruelly murdered two thousand years ago and had gone to "Heaven" and never been heard from since. Therefore, any help from that "saviour" was quite problematical. Now they saw that the God of the stranger was an ever-present, although unseen, Spirit, which spirit possessed absolute control over every movement of every created being. They also grasped the fact that an absolute, though simple belief in the presence of this great Spiritual God-Law fulfilled the very things in their lives they had been looking for. Instead of attempting to influence Deity by their own prayers, they saw that all that was necessary in order to receive perfect spiritual communion was to first of all recognize the existence of the great Life Spirit itself, and then never question its personal existence at all. 

The stranger stayed with the wonderful little backwoods family over night and ate a hearty breakfast in the morning. So impressed was the little family with his visit that 

Barney and Madge, the gray team, were hitched to the bobsleigh and the stranger, whose name they never learned, was driven to Hogan, where he took the northern Ontario train back to civilization again. 

Many years later the same stranger visited the little town of Hogan, and inquired after Jacques Chauvet. "Jacques 

Chauvet," said old Dennis Hogan. "Why, Jacques Chauvet is president of the railroad that brought you in here."

"Well, well,” replied the stranger, "and where is his office?" "Why, he has swell offices in Toronto, right on Yonge 

Street," replied Mr. Hogan. "Do you know him?"

The stranger replied that he did and took the next train out of Hogan. A few days later he presented a card to Mr. 

Chauvet's secretary, requesting an interview. On the card was written: "The man who was saved from three wolves."

The card gained him instant admission, and Jacques almost burst into tears at seeing his visitor once more. 

Several hours were spent in the conversation which followed, and I cannot recount them here. Jacques Chauvet informed our stranger friend what had happened since he had begun to recognize the existence of the great Power of God here and now, materially. Shortly after he had begun to ponder over this new conception of God, a contract for ties came in so large that Jacques had to hire many men and many teams to fill the order. The railroad company informed him his ties were so much better than any other contract ties. Rich financial awards had come to Jacques thru his contracting business, and he was enabled to purchase a few shares in the railroad which hauled these ties from Hogan into the larger cities. Then the officials of the railroad noticed something a little different about Jacques Chauvet. He seemed to pay no attention to surroundings, and yet he seemed to have a miraculous insight into every angle of business. So when a vacancy occurred on the 

Board of Directors, Jacques Chauvet, to his intense surprise, was offered the position at a good large salary, and he accepted. Less than one year ago, he was elected to the presidency of this railroad, and standing before him now

YOUR GOD-POWER 

was the man whose simple little story a few years ago had made that success and that happiness possible. 

A beautiful home stood out in the Rosewood Addition of Toronto, and Jacques insisted that the visitor spend the night with him once more, for it was Christmas Eve. This the stranger did, and tears fell down Mrs. Chauvet's face as she recognized her visitor of a few years back. Little 

Charles was twenty-one now, and the baby was in high school. Many past experiences in Jacques' rise to fame were recounted that night, and naturally the conversation drifted once more to the subject of God. "I have a confession to make to you, Mr. Stranger," said 

Mrs. Chauvet. "When you told us in our little shanty that the story of the angels and the wise men was a man-made story and not true, I became very indignant and I thought that you knew nothing about God at all. But after thinking over what you said, a strange something began to dawn on and I found myself discarding the story I had been taught to believe in since childhood." me,

The stranger's face was intense and was lit up by a beautiful smile, for he knew what Mrs. Chauvet was going to say next. He anticipated the question a little by asking her, "Do you find that by disbelieving the miraculous story you lost all faith in God?" "No," replied Mrs. Chauvet, "by discarding any and all stories which separated God from me, I really found out who and what God really is. Jacques and I decided the day after you left that we would recognize in our life the existence of this great Spiritual Law which seemed to do nothing, and yet does everything, and from that day on everything changed. I used to be afraid to do something that was wrong, but now I am never afraid because I know that if I want to do a thing, it is right."

"How about the children?" asked the visitor. "Did my story to them destroy their confidence in God?" "No," replied Mrs. Chauvet, "quite to the contrary. Little

Charles used to believe that God was in the sky and that he could not know anything about Him until after he died, when he would be changed into an angel or something like that. Now, however, Charles does not make any move without recognizing the Spirit of the invisible God-Law in that move. But we all do, as a matter of fact, for our confidence in this great God Realm far removed from our own superstitious realm has been abundantly justified." 

The stranger left the next day for his home, and there we will leave him.

EVERY GREAT DISCOVERY in the realm of religion and God is made by one man. At first it is not believed by the multitude. Only by the few. The multitude does not matter, for the great part it is an inert mass of humanity, never having claimed or used the God-given faculty of thinking for themselves.

In religion, the great inert mass of the population has been perfectly content to allow priest or preacher to do their religious thinking for them. To attempt to find God outside of the charted course of some theological organization takes too much trouble. It's too much bother. "My church says it is so, so it must be," is the theory of most men and women today. They neglect to ask their church for proof. 

If they did, they would have their eyes opened. 

During the first three years of his life, Mohammet only gathered round him thirteen followers. Then, later, millions believed him, and still believe him. The overall picture he presented had the essence of truth in it. So, later, when the inert multitudes did become interested, they followed him by the millions. But he made the discovery alone. Only one believed in him and that was his wife. "She believed in me when none else would," he said.

So it is here in America. The staggering discoveries of the Power of God which came to me direct from the Spirit of God, were made by me alone. That was many years ago. 

It is now only 15 years since I began to tell the world what 140

a staggeringly potent Force the Spirit of God is. True, I gained followers by the thousands. And then by the tens of thousands. And then by the hundreds of thousands. 

While Mohammet only succeeded in getting thirteen followers in three years, I was fortunate enough to be able to put this Teaching into 67 countries in its first year. 

Of course, times are different. Modes of travel are different. We have now the steamship, the post office, the airplane, and it is very much easier to spread the Truths of God now than it was then. The point I wish to make here is that when Almighty God decides it is time for some new phase of His Truths to be made manifest to mankind, He always brings those Truths thru someone who is not in organized religion. He cannot manifest those Truths thru anyone who believes in any of the many various phases of what we know as "organized religion" today. 

For the Truths of God as revealed to your writer and thru him to the world, are very different from the supposed truths taught by any existing system of organized religion. 

They must be. For organized religion has failed, completely and miserably, to reveal one faint jot or tittle of the actual Power of God to the human race. They have taught theories of God-yes-galore. They have taught old-fashioned traditions about God by the bushel. Digging into the misty recesses of the past for the Truths of God, they grabbed what they cared to, regardless of the truth of what they grabbed, and then they began to organize a system of religion, and sell it to "whosoever will". 

The trouble with this is that the "past" is the wrong place to look for God. The ancient unprovable traditions have not revealed God, and they never will. The place to look for God is in the "here and now." It is in the future. 

Instead of looking into the pagan past for the Truths of

Almighty God exists, the true prophet will begin to investigate and try to discover why it is that there is so much crime, sin, want, misery, fear in the world. AT THIS POINT in our studies together, shall we look a little closer at the word "God"? I have given you in previous

Lessons, the picture that God is not some anthropomorphic being, having hands and feet, etc. That concept of God is fast fading out. It is being replaced by a far more ennobling picture than that. Those who subscribe to that theory of 

God belong to a dying civilization-a dying "faith".

For whether they who belong to "faiths" teaching those things like it or not, we face facts. And the one outstanding fact this war brought out is the fact, not doubted by any thinker, that while humanity brutally slaughters itself, human beings of one race ruthlessly murdering those of other races, our systems of religion, claiming to be "agents of God" have been compelled to stand powerless on the side-lines and watch this gruesome, wholesale slaughter without being able to do one thing about it. 

If I advertise myself as an engineer, when you consult me for advice you naturally expect me to know something about that whereof I speak. If a bridge on my farm breaks away, and I send for an engineer, I naturally expect that engineer to know how to repair the break. And he does know how to do just that. But this civilization has broken down. There's no question about that. And yet-those who wear the uniform of God, and make their living by expounding what they say are the "truths of God", stand idly by, utterly unable to do one blessed thing to repair hu- 156

manity. They know there's been a break in the relationship which should exist between God and the human race, somewhere, but where that break is, or how to repair it, they know not.

He knows his message, and the world will know that he knows his message, and the Power behind it. 

However, without the consciousness of the divine Spirit of God in human life and affairs, humanity will continue to degenerate. We must have first a knowledge of the 

Power of The Spirit of God. We must follow that knowledge, and we must abide by the Spiritual Law which is God. If this is not done, and it never has been done yet, humanity will continue to go to the dogs. There is no other alternative I can see.

For man, without God, is incomplete. He is only about 

5% of a man. He lives with absolutely no conscious knowledge of God. And without authentic knowledge of the Almighty, in his own life, there is no way man can live a true life. He is little more than an animal, some of which I sometimes think have more of the consciousness of God in their make-up than many men and women I have met. 

My young son Alfred, a Naval Flying Officer, wrote me a letter the other day. He digs very deeply into what his father teaches, and, like the great majority of our young

YOUR GOD-POWER 

men, he wants to know the truth about God. Naturally, to date, he has not found that truth because he has been thrown into church surroundings. However, a few weeks ago

I received a letter from Alfie. He stated that he had bought a book written by some scientist. In that book, this man of letters stated that the only function of man was to eat, thereby gaining physical energy for his body, and reproduce the species. "Dad-I don't believe that's all God put man on the earth for-do you?” Alfie asked me. I told him in my reply that if that is the object of Almighty God, we might just as well start eating as much as we can and reproducing the species as fast as possible. No. That man wasn't a scientist, he was a crass materialist, and not a very good one either. 

For scientists are now universally recognizing the actual existence of a Power, or Force, invisible and limitless, in all nature and thru all nature. They are beginning to find out something about the existence of Almighty God. I am glad. For God can and will be found thru a careful intelligent study of his own creation. God cannot, and will not, be found in foolish theories of virgin births, immaculate conceptions, crucifixions, resurrections, and the other false teachings of our theological organizations. For God is not in them. But God is in you. God is in nature. God does occupy all space. So, then, let us continue our researches for God where we know God is to be found. We won't get far off the track if we do that. 

It is perfectly safe to say, then, that all presentations of God brought to us by our theological organizations are but hallucinations of their own minds. They teach God as they have been taught to believe God is, and as they would like God to be-not as God is. Some years ago I wandered thru the ruins of old Pompeii. I saw there a "god" or rather the

mother of a "god" which had been carved out of stone. 

One half of the face of that idol had been actually worn away by men and women kissing it, in the belief that they had been kissing the mother of God. Could anything, I ask you, be more preposterous than imagining that a piece of stone, or rock, represented the mother of God?

Yet millions of good honest people have been actually taught that Almighty God was born in the womb of a virgin, and therefore had a mother, but no father. I say to you, whoever you may be, that it is this teaching which is all this world has to depend upon in any emergency. 

Almighty God, I say to you as a prophet of God, is not capable of being born in anyone's womb. No man has ever seen God as a material being, and any system of so-called religion which teaches that God ever occupied solely and exclusively the body of any man, born of a woman, is teaching falsity in its very essence. Yet that is the ludicrous and ridiculous tapestry which our churches offer us, even in this day and age, as the truths of Almighty God. God pity them, Catholic and Protestant alike.

No wonder the human race is ruthlessly and brutally slaughtering itself. No wonder men and women who know how to think are looking somewhere else for God. No wonder that with over 135,000,000 good, honest, loyal Americans, there are less than 15,000,000 who attend all the churches in the United States combined. As a true prophet of God I must say to you that the quicker you get away from such fabrications, the sooner your feet will be on the track and down the trail, at the end of which is-God. 

The very foolishness of such teachings brands them, in the mind of the true prophet of God, what they are—old pagan myths and superstitions, born in the dark of the evolution of man towards God. ages

I suppose they had to be. I suppose there had to be many stages in the evolution of man towards God. For that is the end of evolution. It will not stop until man and 

God are, once and for all, completely united. That may be a long way off, but all the elements of that unification exist now. You-whoever you may be-can be united with God right here on the earth. It is not a question of the abilty of 

God to manifest the fullness of His Power to humanityit's a question of humanity finding the true concept of God, which concept will surely and speedily lead man direct into the fullness of the Power of God. I say to you, that can be done now. It cannot be done however by believing that 2000 years ago Almighty God was miraculously conceived in the womb of a virgin, later being killed on a cross to save humanity from its "sins".

There is only one "sin" in the human race today, and that is doubt of the existence of God here and now. I'm not so sure that is a sin. It may be just simply because no one has yet appeared on the religous horizon who has seen the glorious truths of the present existence of Almighty God. Certainly there is no one in any theological organization who can possibly see or know those truths. The prophet God raises up will be entirely removed from religion as it exists today. But those fundamentals of truth which invariably and inevitably lead to God, exist now.

For there is Power for you. Yes, there is. Dynamic, dazzling, pulsing, surging, throbbing Power. The Power of

Had you had no mental preparation for them, you probably would have said, "Oh-that's too impossible-no such a Power as that exists." But such a Power does exist. I know, for I've seen Its operations over a period of more than fifteen years, and there is no man alive can convince me of the error of what I know.

More than that, The Spirit of God has spoken directly to me in a manner which, because of the dazzling brilliance of it all, I won't relate to you in these Lessons. But this revelation was very real. of the world discovers them. So cherish them. Follow me carefully. I cannot lead you to any other place than the

That is my mission in life. For this purpose was I born. From the dawn of civilization-down thru the weary ages of advance, the questing eager face of mankind has turned again and again towards the Light. Up to the limitless stars-always in search of some mighty Power which can enrich and sustain Life. This instinct is sure. 

How very sure, you know. For you, whoever you may be, have many times hungered, and you still hunger for this staggering Power which is God in action. 

Now I can say to you that out of the chasm of the void is coming to you the flaming truth of the Spirit of God. 

Surging with Power, filled with strength, this new concept of Almighty God is truly coming into your life. It will bring peace. It will bring Power. God in you. 

Time after ageless time, the constellations have wheeled in their orbits, and always the mighty Power of the God- Law, The Spirit of God has been present, willing, and ready to supply mankind with all our hearts hold dear.

But the way had not been opened. Under the old theories of Almighty God separated from the human race while it

lives, and available only after death, the way could not be opened. Men and women could not possibly know of the shining, glittering Power of The Spirit of God.

God can make you what you want to become. Are you poor?-the irresistible Spirit of God in you can you can make rich. It can give you an abundance of material and spiritual supply. Yes-It can, and It will reveal the fullness of the Power of God-in you. For that's the only place you can know for an absolute certainty that God is. In you. 

God may be in the heavens. He may have a nice home prepared for you after you die-but the only place where you can actually know and feel the Power of Almighty God, with all the staggering fullness that implies, is in you-here and now.

I shall continue this discussion of the ever-abiding Spirit of God in you in your next Lesson. I want you to read this one many times. of God. It has opened your eyes a little bit wider to the amazing Power which you have allowed to take up residence in you. Of course, The Spirit of God lives in you whether you realize this or not. But this Lesson will make you realize it to a far greater degree than ever before. 

So read it carefully. Read it often. Sit down alone with 

God, and as you sit there, make this your affirmation from this point on: "I live in the Power of The Spirit of God."

This is an actual fact. You do live, actually and literally, in the Power of The Spirit of God. I am trying to drive home to you this staggering fact, and am taking sensible, accepted, educational methods to bring the consciousness of the Spirit of God to you. So cooperate. Let this affirmation live in you, and keep and with you, it up always, until I change it, if I do. But more than all, spend those few minutes, or hours, alone with God. For it is alone with God, amid the mystic shadows of the Power of God, you will absorb all the Power you can possibly need, or use. “I live in the Power of The Spirit of God." 

I live in the Power of The Spirit of God. I wonder just how long it will take you to realize to the very full the staggering import of that truth. For it is Truth. It is a fact of life. It is the most supreme fact of life. It is impossible to get back of such a staggering spiritual revelation. It answers every problem life can offer and it provides the key to the so-called mystery of life. 

There is no mystery in life to the one who knows that he or she "lives in the Power of The Spirit of God." Instead

of there being mystery connected with the Realm of God, all is a beautiful simplicity when viewed from the standpoint of this amazing fact. So wonderful is this simplicity that any attempt to make mystery of it completely fails. 

If the Spirit of the Creator-Almighty God, lives in you, then the statement that you live in the Power of the Spirit of God is absolutely and fundamentally true. 

Now what does this all mean? Why it means that, living in the Power of God, and with all the Power God has instantly available to you, you automatically become complete master of every and all conditions under which your life is lived. It means that whatever things came into your life because you knew not the present existence of the 

Spirit of God, must, if undesirable to you, immediately leave. And you are the one that possesses the Power to order them to leave.

We have seen that the Power of the Spirit of God is the Life that is Almighty God. We have seen too that your little Life, lived in the physical body people mistake for the real you, is, in essence, the very Life that is God. We have also seen that the part cannot be separated from the whole. Therefore, again I call your attention to the fact that in the moment that you realize that you do live in and thru and by the Power of the Spirit of God, in that moment there is available to you more spiritual Power than you will ever be able to use. 

Whether you use that Power or not is something for you to decide. Whether you choose the ways of the physical body, completely ignoring the spiritual ways of God, wellthat's for you to decide also. What we know now is that if you will, whenever you will, wherever you will, there, closer than hands and feet, closer than death, lives in you the invisible Power of the Spirit of God, the Creator of the

universe. And It lives there for a distinct purpose. That purpose is to reveal Itself to you. When the revelations of the actual and literal Power of God in you begin to make themselves evident in your life, your neighbors will know that something has happened in your experience. 

The first thing they will notice is an inner peace, which will show itself outwardly. There will be a calm serenity about you which nothing can disturb. For when you learn the lessons of the present existence of God well, you will leave behind you all the cares and worries of the day. You won't even recognize them because you are living your life in the Power of the Spirit of God, and that Great Spirit knows nothing about cares, worries, and the like. They come only from the physical body and the accompanying physical mind-that mind which is born of the physical brain.

But the Spirit of God is not even interested in your cares or worries, and for that simple reason. They are flesh-born, while the invisible Spirit of God in you is God-born. And the two can never meet. The physical was given to you in order that you might glorify it because there lives in that body all the Power there is in the Realm of the Spirit of God. In the absence of conscious knowledge of the existence of God in you, naturally, there creep in worries, cares, and a thousand other man-made things which, when the Power of God comes up against them in the same life, at once have to disappear. God and worry cannot live in the same body. God and fear cannot live in the same body.

Where one is the other cannot manifest. If the knowledge of God in you is completely ignored, and if you are completely ignorant of the Life of God in you, then that means that the only consciousness you have is bodily physical consciousness, and that is the very antithesis of God.

Now, whether you know it or not, you have to live in either one of these two states of consciousness. You cannot live in both. I do not mean that when you find God in your life all sense of the physical leaves forever. No-but 

I do mean this:-I mean that when you realize the Life of 

God-the Power of God in you, as the very Life of you, your thoughts will always center in that Spiritual Realm. 

It is utterly impossible for a man to have even the slightest experimental knowledge of the Spirit of God in him, and live a life in which bodily and material things predominate. 

Where your treasure is, there will your heart be also. 

Where God is, there will your heart be also. And where is 

God? Why in you. And where is the Power of God? Why in you. And you live your life then, in, by, and thru the

Power of the Spirit of the Living God. From this inherent 

Life of God in you, you derive "your God-Power" and all the staggering powers that Life of God in you puts at your disposal, and the source of them all is in you. 

Suppose that for some reason or other you lose your job. 

You have bills which must be paid and you think you can't get another job. So you tramp the street if you do not know God, and the chances are those bills will have to wait a long time before they are paid. You do the best you can. You go from one place to another, and maybe you find a job and maybe you don't. In any event, whether you do or not depends entirely on what someone else does, not upon what you do. This is what happens in the life of the one who does not know of the existence of the Spirit of God in him or in her.

Now what happens in the life of the one who does live in the Power of the Spirit of God? Well, in the first place that one won't lose the job unless there appears another and a better job first. But in the event that he or she

YOUR GOD-POWER 

should, what is the first thing that person will do? Will he or she start to worry about that lost job? Will they run round from one place to another, losing sleep because the bills are not paid? They will not. For the one in tune with 

God, the one who knows of the existence and Power of the Spirit of God in his or her life, will at once talk with the Power within, and that Power, ever responding, will provide the answer.

That answer will probably be provided in a manner you little suspect, for no human mind knows how the mind which is God operates. But we don't need to know. Suffice it to know that God does live in you, and you therefore live your life in the Power of the Spirit of God. A quiet conference with God about that lost job will provide the answer faster, and much more surely and satisfactorily than can happen in the life of the one who has not yet learned that Almighty God, with all His Power, does actually live in your life and does actually have the answer to every problem that can confront you. Aye-to every problem life can offer.

After a while, as you get closer and closer to God, you will find that life will never offer you a problem. For as the consciousness of the existence of God in you is more and more fully known, there can be no room for anything else in your life. Those petty annoyances, those sicknesses, those problems over which you have spent so much time and worry, well, there's just no room for them. For God cannot hold problems. God cannot hold worries. And when your life is as close as it should be to God, nothing under the sun will cause you to give a second thought to it. 

For yours will be a "hidden life" from now on. You will live the life of the Spirit of God. And as the Life of the 

Spirit of God dominates your physical body and the ma-

terial things with which you are surrounded, then your life will be hidden so deep under the Life which is God that absolutely nothing can begin to get down deep enough to bother you. When this happens you will be truly living the life you should. That life will be a life of wonderful accomplishment. It will be a life that demonstrates how very easy it is to obtain, thru the Power of God in you, complete victory and mastery over the things which, had God been ever known on the earth, never could have appeared to plague anyone.

It is the absence of knowledge of the indwelling Spirit of God which is the direct cause of the ills of the world today. I shall repeat that statement many times while I live. For where God is, these horrible murderings cannot be. The German, Italian, and Japanese despots could not possibly exist if the Power of God were known. If they knew of this Power the last thing they would think about would be killing anyone. And if our national leaders knew of the existence of the Power of God in their lives, they would know how to bring such monsters to their knees without firing a single shot, and without shedding the life of one precious American boy.

But they do not know of this God-Power within. None has ever told them, so how could they know? So far as I know, I happen to be one of the very few prophets who has brought to the world this closeness of the Power of

Now just before we close this Eleventh Lesson, let me suggest that not only the worries and cares of life flee away when you realize the indwelling Power of God in you, but you can develop the habit of actually walking and talking with God. You will find that there will open up to you a 

Realm of God so supremely beautiful that you'll wonder why someone did not call your attention to it before. 

Well, let's not look back and criticize too much. The main point is that we do know now of the existence of this beautiful Realm of the Spirit of God.
