Knowing all these things, what is there to stand in the way of your making your life the most beautiful thing you ever lived?

I'm reminded of a prominent publisher in New York City. 

I was explaining the deep things of the Spirit of God to him not so very long ago, and he said to me: "But Doctor

Robinson-I'm over seventy-is there much use of my starting in all over again and living the life you ask me to?” To which I replied: "My friend-it is not a case of starting in all over again. It is a case of starting in for the first time. You are good for ten or twenty more years, and that length of time, lived in the consciousness of the Power of God, can be much more profitable and enjoyable than the seventy years you have now wasted."

That is true. This man has not really wasted his life, for he is a prominent publisher. What he has done is live his life in complete ignorance of the Power of God. Although he has been a very active church-member all his life, until he read some of my books he never even suspected that he could find and use the Power of God right here on the earth. Little did that man dream that some years ago, when he underwent a serious operation, he had, living in him, all the Power of the Spirit of God, which Power could have healed him without the loss of a certain part of his physical body.

The fact that the Spirit of God lives in the physical body is absolute assurance that no disease can ever stay in any body whose owner knows the fact of the presence of the Spirit of God in that body and commands the disease to leave. For the Almighty is just as anxious to live in a good "house" as you are to have Him.

I've been trying all day to think of some circumstance which might happen in a human life which the Power of the Indwelling God could not handle. And I'm absolutely unable to find any such circumstances. What seems impossible to the human race in this stage of its existence, when the Power of God is completely unknown to it, becomes the beautifully possible when man awakens to his God- 

Power within.

YOUR GOD-POWER 

For don't you see, the ingenuity, the ability to create, and the absolutely higher Power of God can be thrown against nothing which cannot be made subservient to God. 

For what greater power than the Power of God can there be? What greater wisdom, ingenuity, Power anywhere than the Power of the God I am teaching you how to find? 

And the amazing thing about all this is that this Power lives in you, and you can consciously use this Power any time you desire to. And you can do this with the absolute assurance that this Power can never fail or disappoint you. 

With such a prodigious Power in you, why do you doubt 

God for anything?

I would so very much like to tell you of the so-called miracles I have seen over the past sixteen years, but space forbids. In a previous Lesson I included just a few. But 

I have seen things happen which absolutely defy human reason. A few years ago I was driving my Cadillac to 

Portland, Oregon. Between Boardman and Arlington, I blew a front tire. I had no way of fixing it, and never gave the tire a thought. Instead, I turned my thoughts inward to where God is.

I sat on the running board of the car, and for about fifteen minutes communed with God. The flat tire was completely forgotten. When my moments of communion with God were over, I climbed into the car, started the motor, and drove to Arlington. Suddenly I remembered the flat tire. Stopping the car I looked, and the tire was up to its regular pressure and not a soul had touched it. 

On another occasion I saw a man fall from a moving train going at a high rate of speed. Stopping the train, I left it and walked back along the tracks until I found this man. He looked as if he were dead, but I didn't care whether he was dead or not. The Power of God was upon

me, and operating thru me. So taking the man by the hand 

I said "Get up". He did. We talked for a while about his fall from the train, but there was not a single scratch upon him when I left him.

These and scores of other demonstrations of the Power of God have been my lot to see. If I told you some of the things I personally do every day, it would stagger your imagination. I'll just close this Lesson by stating that the promises of God are sure. All you need to do is to get close into the Realm of the Spirit of God in you, and you'll know something about the limitless Power of the God of the universe.

THE SPIRIT OF GOD is the most amazing thing a life can find. There is nothing in the whole realm of human experience which can even begin to faintly approach this Realm of staggering Spiritual Power. So amazing is it, that when man begins to find and comprehend what is involved in this Power in him, the reason seems incapable of conceiving very much of it.

The amazing thing is this-we have all been taught from childhood, if we had any religious training at all, that God was unknowable. He is far removed from the human race. 

This life is a sort of testing-place to determine whether or not we are good enough to enter a place called "heaven”, there, and there only to see God, after we die. So then, having fully believed this, when a prophet of God comes along and begins to open the minds and the hearts of men and women to the actual truths of the existence of God, quite a bit of consternation follows.

Having held these traditional beliefs "about" God, we have imagined that our life here should be lived as best we could, never even suspecting for the fraction of a second that all the Power there is in this amazing, scintillating Realm of God is here and now available to us all. Nothing like that was ever taught on the earth before by anyone, systems of religion least of all. And yet-why should not that be? By what rule of logic or reasoning power must we consider Almighty God as far removed from His creation? 176

Whose idea is it that Almighty God cannot manifest the fullness of His Power on the earth today?

Certainly no one who has had the slightest experience with God ever advanced such a theory as that. Equally certain it is that no man with the slightest scintilla of faith in Almighty God advanced it either. That theory came from someone who never knew God, and who had no faith whatsoever in the ability of God to make known His Power to men and women right here on the earth, where we need it most. True faith-true belief in Almighty God puts no limitations on Him whatsoever. Only unbelief does that. 

And it is a pretty safe proposition, that is. No one has ever come back from beyond the tomb to tell us what goes on there, so they who advocate that impossible theory of the unwillingness or inability of God to make His presence known to us here and now, have nothing to fear. 

Yes-they have one thing to fear. They fear the man or woman who has made actual contact with the Power of God, and thru that contact, is bringing to humanity the actual truths of God. Religion fears that man. It always has. No true prophet of God has arisen who did not arouse the animosity and the enmity and the darts of organized religion, right off the bat. Because no true prophet of God has arisen who gave credence to anything religion teaches as being of God. Of course, I'd be the last one to say there is nothing good in religion as we have it today. What I say is that any theory which denies the Power and the ability of Almighty God to manifest His Power on the earth here and now, to every one of His creatures, is a false theory of God and a false system of religion. That will be evident to everyone able to think freely, and not bound down by creed, rite, and ritual.

But the tragedy of it all is that there does exist, here and

YOUR GOD-POWER 

now, the most dynamic Power this world ever can know. 

Yet we travel along the pathway of life, utterly and completely ignorant of such a Power. No one we know of has had any experience with such a Power. Or so we think. 

But we make a mistake. For the Power of God does not lie in the show of religion made by those with whom we are thrown in contact. Our next door neighbor may never even mention religion to us, and yet he may be so very close to the Power of God that if we only knew how close, we might take off our hats in his presence. 

Yes-we live and move and have our being in the everlasting Power of the Spirit of God. This Power is always available to us. It can never fail to bring to us every good thing we can need thru life. And yet-not one in 500,000,000 knows anything at all about the Power of God, living in them, and therefore at the instant call of each of them. 

The old anthropomorphic ideas were deeply laid. It will take a few more generations to eradicate them. Perhaps the war will do a lot to eradicate them, for every religion extant must hang its head in shame when it looks at the condition of the world today, and admits its inability to demonstrate the slightest grain of the Power of God, against the mad murderers who brought this thing to us. 

So, then, talk about God-forms of religion-these have nothing whatsoever to do with the Power of God. This 

Power must be, and will be manifested direct from God to us. We ourselves shall decide whether we find the Power of God or not. None can act as a "go-between”. The definite connection must be made by us. If we ever make it, it will be. For to find God only means finding our true self. 

Our true self is God. The very essence of our life is God. 

The breath of God that was given to us when we came into the world, was God. Therefore, to "find God" only

means that someone makes us conscious of the fact that 

God is in us.

To "find God" means only that we recognize the staggering fact that all the Power there is in the Realm of the Spirit of God lives in us, as our very life-this-and then act upon it. For the Power of God is utterly useless unless we use it. It will still give us life. It will still cause the heart to beat. It will still control the involuntary muscles. 

It will still keep life flowing thru us while we sleep, whether we are conscious of this unknown Guest or not. 

We'll live just the same. But why live that way? Why live without Power when the Originator of all Power lives in us? Why not recognize that Power, and use it? There is not one blessed thing stopping any of us from finding and using the Power of God if we will. 

So few have ever been told of the existence in them of this amazing Power that it still is practically unknown on the earth today. Only they who have studied under this author can possibly have seen the revelation of truth Almighty God is bringing to the human race thru this humble prophet of God. Who else is teaching these truths? Where can you go on the face of this earth to find the actual manifestations of the Spirit of God, except thru this prophet? 

There is no place you can go. away,

As the years go by, others will catch the vision, and when this prophet is laid this Movement will sweep the world for God by the Power of God. But that is not likely while this writer is alive. It has never happened that way. It will not happen this way with this prophet of God. 

My work is only to lay the foundation. My work is only to plant the seed. My work is to bear the brunt of the attacks of orthodox religions on the truths of God. Others will come along, and on the foundations this prophet has laid,

YOUR GOD-POWER 

there will be built the structure which shall, ere too long, reveal the fullness of the Power of The Spirit of God to the whole human family. Thrice fortunate are you, beloved, to have this message in your hands. Thrice fortunate are you to be among the first to have the opportunity to actually know and experience the fullness of the Power of 

The Spirit of God. 

For this vast ocean of Cosmic God-Power stands knocking at your door, asking you, yes, begging you to realize that all the Power God has lives in you, and thru that Power in you, you can use, if necessary, the whole spiritual resources of the Almighty, right while you live on the earth. But you will come to a realization of who and what you are-all alone. You will be made "at one" with Godall alone. You won't find that Power thru a sermon. You won't find it by going to the altar in some revival meeting. 

You won't find it by eating the Lord's Supper. Nor will you find it by reading the Bible, unless you read very deeply. 

For the letter killeth-but the Spirit of God maketh alive.

I know men and women who know the Christian Bible from cover to cover. They can quote at length, and they can tell you where thousands of passages are to be found in the book. Yet-these good souls, filled with the letter of the Bible, know absolutely nothing of the Power of God. 

They cannot know because the letter has satisfied them. 

They read. They have read. They say "Lord-I believe”.

And they really do believe those writings are true. But they do not know the Power of God. And the reason they do not know this Power is because they have never known where to look for the Power of God. That is something they never imagined the Bible was written to disclose. 

They thought that all they had to do was make a public

confession with the mouth that they believed on the Lord Jesus, and their names were automatically written in the Lamb's Book of Life somewhere up in the sky. But they still know nothing about the present existing Power of the 

Spirit of God. And if they live to be 100, and if they can say the Bible backwards, they still will leave this life never knowing the Power of God, because they have never gone where God is, to find God. And you cannot find God, or the Power of God, unless you go where God is. And that is not in any Bible.

All bibles are written words. They are called "sacred writings". And if the Spirit of God in all bibles is discerned, then they all will lead to God. But the stories and the parables and the allegories therein in themselves do not; cannot reveal the Power of God to anyone. They are letters, and the letters kill, but the Spirit of God maketh alive. 

And so, with this priceless treasure of The Spirit of God ever available to them, the vast majority of men go thru life with no experience whatsoever with the Power of God. 

A few millions have church membership. A few more millions believe they are "saved" and therefore safe for time and eternity. What these good souls do not know is that 

God never dies. And God is in you. Therefore, you never die. You are not your body. Your body is only the human habitation of the Spirit of God, and this Spirit, in which all of your consciousness lives, goes on thru all eternity whether in the body or out of the body. 

One beautiful thing about finding the Power of God here and now is that eternal life is recognized here and now. The body dies, yes, but what has the body to do with 

Life? What has your physical body to do with the Eternal 

Light and the Eternal Life which is God? What has the

YOUR GOD-POWER 

body to do with the consciousness that is you? What has your body to do with the indwelling Spirit of God in you? ... absolutely nothing at all.

As long as you manifest on the earth as a physical being, the Power of The Spirit of God will manifest thru your physical body, whenever it is physically necessary. It is thru your physical body, in this material and physical realm, that you can transmit the truths of God to others till the whole world knows them. But there is positively no connection between the Spirit of God and your physical body. They are two different things. One is Life-the other is living death. You have the Spirit of Eternal Life or the 

Spirit of the Living God, living in your physical body, and yet not of it. Nothing about you physical is of God. It was created by God, but when you take your eyes off the physical, and begin to discover the spiritual Power of God in you, then all things material are relegated to a very minor place in your life. You then strive to find more and more of the Spirit of Life, or God, in you, and you succeed.

For it is not possible for a man to grasp the tremendous significance of the Spirit of Almighty God existing and operating in his physical body, without being drawn as a magnet draws a needle, to that Spirit. Many an hour you will have spent alone with God if have followed me earnestly. And, as the silent, dynamic, potent, spectacular, invisible Realm of the Spirit of God has become known to you, there has been established a recognition which can never die. You can go back into the world if you care to, but you will never again be of the world. You cannot. You will never forget that two and two make four. Nor will you ever forget, once you have learned it, that The Spirit of Almighty God lives in you, ever and only awaiting your throwing that staggering Spiritual Power into play. you

How can you forget it? I don't know. With me it is a ravishing flame. It's a curtain of fire which envelops me day and night. I should no more dream of forgetting the 

God-Power in me than I should dream of jumping off the 

Brooklyn Bridge. And if I would, I couldn't. For don't you see-when you realize the existing Power of God in youthat knowledge and that consciousness can never leave. 

Your eyes have been opened to the truth. You can do with it what you will, it still remains truth. You may use it, or you may leave it. I don't care and neither does Almighty

God. Once you recognize the fact that the Spirit of God lives in you as your life, you have made the discovery of the ages. And you can never get away from that Power.

And the reason you can never get away from it is that the Power of the Spirit of God permeates the entire universe. 

There is no more possibility of your ever getting out of the influence of the Power of God than there is of Hitler being victorious.

Man will not go on forever living in his present state of blissful ignorance concerning the Power of The Spirit of God. Some of these fine days, the consciousness of the 

Spirit of God will break on this old earth like a bolt of thunder. It will shatter the world. It will flash from pole to pole, and in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye, this world will be re-born. It will be re-born because it will have made the staggering discovery you are making—that the Power of Almighty God lives not only in you, but thruout all space.

Limitless Power. Limitless Space. Limitless Love. Limit less Peace. Limitless supply-and all these here and now because you have discovered the Power of the Spirit of God-in you, and in and thru all creation. There is not an atom of life anywhere that does not contain in it the Spirit

of Life, or the Spirit of God. Not one. Yet we have been so foolish as to believe that we never could know God while on the earth. 

My friend-let us get as far away from that false philosophy of God as we can. Now that we know the Power of 

God is here-in us-let's spend a lot of time in meditating on that fact. Let's spend so much time that the Power in us, which is God, comes to the surface and flows thru and thru us, and then, to others. Let's help make Almighty God the greatest Living Reality this world has ever known. He is-but the world doesn't know it yet. Nevertheless-you can do your part in bringing this knowledge of the Spirit of God to others, wherever you may go. 

Let me give you a word of warning. Feelings never enter into the Realm of The Spirit of God. There comes to each one who experiences the assurance of the Power of God in him or her, an emotion once in a while. But that emotion is very far removed from the Spirit of God. It comes into the consciousness every so often, because the physical body realizes the priceless truth of the indwelling Power of God. 

But that emotion is not God. It is the material self conscious of the Power of God in the body. 

The Realm of God, I must impress upon you again, is not a Realm of feeling, it is a Realm of limitless, emotionless, feelingless Power. The result of the application of the 

Force or Spirit of God in any manner, is tremendous; but the Power itself, in essence, is emotionless. So don't look for emotion-look for Power. For Power-silent-dynamic -motionless-emotionless Power-all these are God. This deep indwelling Silence-is God. For at the very heart of 

God is Spiritual Silence. Impregnable Spiritual Power but it manifests as silence. Can you grasp that? I am sure you can. If you cannot, think it out. It will come to you.

When you have grasped it, you will be able to use the God-Power you consciously have living in you, but you will not be conscious of how you use it. It will not cause you to shout "Glory to God" very often, although I have said that many times. However, I consider those moments when I utter such emotions, moments of weakness rather than moments of strength. God is absolute strength, and strength-the strength that is God-is silent in its operations. Never forget that.

But firm as the Rock of Ages stands the Power of Godthat Power you have so often wanted to find, but never thought you could find this side of the grave. My little 

Florence, as I write this, is sitting at the piano in the large living-room. She is playing: "Oh God, our help in ages past; 

Our hope for years to come; 

Our shelter from the stormy blast; 

And our eternal home."

Little does she know the staggering import of what she is playing. She is only a child. But there, as she sits there, in her and all around her is the Spirit of the Living God, full to the overflowing with dynamic Spiritual Power, which she and every other man and woman can use here and now, to bring into their lives everything they can possibly need, both material and spiritual. Yes-God is still 

God. The Power of God, practically unknown on the earth until we first called attention to it some sixteen years ago.

This journey thru life with God is a very interesting one.

In other words, God is using a thing that is not to bring to naught things that are. He is using the stone which everyone else rejected, and He is making that stone the head of the corner of a new religious era in which God, and God alone, shall be the ruling and reigning Power.

IN THIS LESSON I'm going to tell you where Christianity and the rest of the world's major systems of religion have fallen down. This will tell you why the world is in the shape it's in. This will tell you why the Power of Almighty 

God is an unknown entity in human lives, both individual and national. What we have, is a group of systems of religion, but no knowledge of the present existing Power of God. We have millions of church-goers, all of whom believe they are "worshipping" God. What they are really doing is making a laughing stock out of the Almighty. 

They name the name of God but they know nothing about the Power of God. They have a form of religion but they will tell you very freely that as far as having any experience with God, well they just don't know who, what, or where God is, so they cannot have any experience with God.

Religion today is predicated on an absent God. It is predicated on a "crucified" god, if that were possible. 

Fortunately it is not possible. God is the essence and the originator of Life, therefore no one ever killed Almighty God. Every system of religion of any importance teaches that its "god" was "killed on a cross by cruel hands", but this is only proof that the god of religions is not, and cannot be the Creator of the universe. For, I repeat, no one ever killed God. And if the story of Jesus killed on a cross is true, and it could be, it was as a man, and most certainly 192

not as a God. There is only one God. If Jesus was God, and someone killed him, that left the world without a God, or without a Creator. 

Then who ran things while God was dead? What kept yon stars and planets swinging in space? What caused the life-blood to course thru the veins of millions of people while God lay for three days dead in a tomb? And if Jesus was God, then what about the millions who lived before God was born? Although this is the theory of many major systems of religion, and although millions of good loyal Americans say they believe that, or rather, their church teaches it, the story is too impossible to be true, no matter who teaches it. 

No one ever killed God. God is Spirit, therefore death to the Life-Spirit is a total and utter impossibility. We are familiar with the theory that God created a son by miraculous means, and sent that son into the world to save only those who believed that he was God. Religions tell us very plainly, as does the Christian religion, that there is none other name given under heaven whereby men may be saved, whatever that may mean. This is a good philosophy for any system of religion to have. In the first place it is utterly incapable of proof, and in the second place, it accounts for the inability of the followers of these religions to prove one thing about the existing Power of God. 

God, they say, was dead. But he rose again, and went away to heaven where he now sits at the right hand of 

God Almighty. Some day, they say, he will come back again. We have had many sects here in the United States who have told us that on a certain day, Jesus, whom they consider God, would descend from heaven with a shout and with the voice of an archangel, and all who "believed", and only they who "believed" will be borne away up to

YOUR GOD-POWER 

heaven, while the rest of us are left down here to rot. That story is not true. It is the greatest stumbling block in man's ascent to the truths of God.

In the first place there is nothing for man to be saved from. In the second place, if there were, man's salvation would be a matter of his own belief, and that would be the surest way for man to go off the trail. What man believes or does not believe is entirely aside from the point. Man may be wrong. Speaking theologically, man is wrong, as one look at the world today proves. Man has never had need for a “savior from sin" for the simple fact that man did not commit any sin when he was born. He came into the world without his consent, and he will leave it without his consent. When the Almighty makes a new creation, as 

He made man, you may depend upon it the work is perfect. If it were not, God had nothing to do with it. 

The creation of man took a long time-an awful long time. It may be true that man was formed of the dust of the ground, but that is physical man only. You will note that after God had created the physical man, if we accept the Christian theory (which we do not), then God thought enough of the material man of dust He had created, to breathe into his nostrils the Breath of Life. So, regardless of what our churches think, Almighty God considered man a pretty good creation, or He would not have taken up 

His abode in man.

The churches, however, in order to propagate their respective theories of God, say that man committed some sin or other, and fell from grace. And, they tell us, it required the death of Almighty God in the form of his son, to atone for the sin man committed. Brother and sister, let me tell you what the only sin in the world is today—it is unbelief of the fact that Almighty God exists here and now

in every living soul. It is doubt that God has ability enough to manifest Himself to the human race while it lives here on the earth. Murders, of course, there are, and there are a dozen other crimes listed in our books of crime. But every last one of them is a direct result of man's not knowing the first thing about the actual existence of The Spirit which is God, in every human life, just as sure as that life exists. 

Almighty God made a good man. After scores of thousands of years of creation, holy-men and witch-doctors came into the picture, and for their own profit, started a theory that God was "mad" at man, and if man did not follow the dictates of some priest, he most surely would end up in hell-fire, there to be tormented forever with the devil and his angels. That is what caused doubt of God.

That teaching is the reason man does not know God today. 

Our churches, therefore, instead of revealing the Power of The Spirit of God to man, are very effectively hindering man from finding God, by bringing to man a heathen fairystory which they advocate as being the truths of Almighty God. And so long as systems of religion teach such asinine theories of God, this world will never know God thru any effort the churches can make. It would be an utter impossibility. 

Man has nothing to be "saved" from. Certainly not from ´sin” as the churches teach sin. Almighty God asks no one to worship Him. All Almighty God wants is for the human race to accept and understand the fact that we can never get closer to God than we are now. He wants the human race to know that He lives here, in us and with all His amazing Power-here and now. 

God wants you and me to know that no matter what the emergency may be, here, in us, closer than Life or death, is The Spirit of the Living God. He wants us to know that,

YOUR GOD-POWER 

being God, we have at our disposal, everything we can possibly need, in this life and in whatever other life there may be. God wants us to throw into the garbage-can false heathen theories of God which teach that we can never know God unless we are saved, and that only by believing in what some church organization teaches. In other words, the Christian church says that if you believe that Jesus was God, you shall be saved. The Church in India says that if you believe that Chrishna was Almighty God you shall be saved. Hinduism says that if you believe that Brahma was 

Almighty God, you shall be saved. Buddhism says that if you believe that Buddha was Almighty God, you shall be saved. Confucianism says that if you believe that Confucius was Almighty God, you shall be saved. When?-When are you to know the Power of God-why after you die of course, never here on the earth where you need God the most.

So then, Christianity is only one, and, with one exception, the most recent of the eleven major systems of religion operating on the earth today. Those of us who have made research, know that the entire story of Christianity, with its God Jesus, was stolen completely from the religion of India. 

I won't go into that fully here, as you have probably read in other places what I have to say about that. But you may take my word for it, the entire story of Jesus Christ, immaculately conceived, born of a virgin, killed on a cross, resurrected from the dead, was stolen from the religion of the Hindoo, who had that story complete, in its every detail, 1500 years before Christianity was ever heard of.

All major religions on the earth today are bodies who say that their god is the only true God. Yet not a single one of them has enough confidence in God, or knows enough about the Spirit of God, to claim that their fol-

lowers can know the fullness of the Power of God here and now. They do not have enough faith or confidence in the Almighty. They tell us that the first human creation 

God made was so rotten that God had to drown it all out like rats in a trap. 

But did man make himself? Did man ask God to create him? If Almighty God created a man who was so depraved that it took the death of Almighty God on a cross to make up for the mistake God made when he made such a bad man. well... that sort of a "god" ought to die on a cross. Truly the True God, the Spirit of The Living God did not descend to such low depths. No man or woman in thinking mind, and using the gem of reason, can ever believe such a monstrous theory of God. But that's all we have offered to us today. True, the churches don't believe that story themselves, but you just try telling them that the story is not true and see how they will puff up, hold aloft their dukes and say, "Come on-if you say our story isn't true-let's fight." And fight they do. 

And still they cannot reveal God to humanity. What this world needs today is not a higher church attendance, it's a new God. Rather should I say a new concept of God. 

The Jesus of the cross, or the Buddha of the cross, or the Chrishna of the cross, none of these were able to reveal to the human race the staggering fact that the Spirit of Almighty God lives in each human life. They couldn't do that. And because they couldn't do that, man is searching for God in places other than our churches. And it is not thru the church the Power of God is coming to humanityit is entirely outside of this organization. Almighty God was very patient with the churches. He gave them every chance to find and know the Power of God. But they would have none of it. They chose to stick with the old heathen,

YOUR GOD-POWER 

unprovable traditions their forefathers had taught them, and as a result, in this day of the world's greatest crisis, God is revealing his Power to the human race, not thru the churches, but thru your humble author, and thru a few others who have bled thru to God.

There was nothing else God could do. The churches insisted on refusing to believe that God had the power to do one blessed thing for anyone this side of the tomb. They insisted on telling the whole world that unless it believed that Jesus was the son of Almighty God, and the only son God ever had, the rest of the world would land up in hell. 

And the rest of the major systems of religion told practically the same story. So there was nothing else God could do except raise up a man and a Movement thru which the actual and literal Power of God can operate. The churches should not blame me for our staggering growth. They too will grow, when they can convince the world that they have discarded the foolish theory of Jesus Christ as God, and instead begin to teach the actual and literal Power of Almighty God, right down here in every human life. 

But you cannot teach a false God when the human race is hungry for the True God. You cannot give them a stone when they ask for an egg. You cannot give them a scorpion when they ask for a fish. That's what Christianity has been doing, and that's all it has been doing. As a result of a Godless church operating in a God-less world, the Almighty has raised up your author, who, thank God, had courage enough to believe that no one ever killed God. No-no one ever killed God. For the Almighty God is the most real thing on the earth today, and I have faith enough to believe that the Power of God can operate here and now, any time we recognize the actual Power and Presence of The 

Spirit of God in us.

The most pitiable religious sight on this earth today is the poor old Italian Pope, who, thinking he is the “one and only" representative of Almighty God, sits there on his throne in Rome, unable to do a blessed thing to stop this war. Unable to do anything else except tell the world that he, and he alone, is the sole bargaining-agent of Almighty 

God on the earth. Is it any wonder Almighty God decided it was about time to raise up someone else who really would tell the people that the Power of God lives in them all, as free as the air we breathe. Is it any wonder, when

God looked down and saw the rank idolatry of the churches, most of all the Roman Catholic Church, that He decided that the farce of religion had gone about far enough.

Hitler did not start this world conflagration. He was the product of the times. Had it not been Hitler it would have been someone else equally insane and equally demon-possessed. But why did Hitler arise? Let me tell you. You church members won't like this, but I'm telling it to you anyhow. Hitler came to power because he believed he could take complete control of the earth, and be God. 

And, shame of shames, there wasn't a religious organization on the face of the earth that had enough experience of the Power of God to challenge the man. Not one. Not even the old Pope who, we are told, played ball with Hitler in more ways than one. Think of it-in the entire Christian structure of religion there wasn't a single church organization or a single man in it who knew enough of the Power of God to bring Hitler to his doom, before he had a chance to plunge this earth into the most ghastly slaughter of all times.

The church has always been right in the middle of every major war, just as it's in the middle of this one. But this

YOUR GOD-POWER 

war will do one thing. It will wash away the sham of present-day theology, and in its place will be found-not theories of God but the actual and literal Power of God. 

And we won't have to wait until we die to find it. And we won't have to pay priest or preacher to sell us his concept of Almighty God. No. The thing is-this world has never known God. The churches have never known God. Until they throw away the hideous theory of Almighty God lying dead in a tomb for three days, after being born of a virgin, it will never be able to bring God to humanity. In time of stress and trial, such as we are going thru now, the church has utterly failed. Only right can neutralize wrong. Only 

God can stop the forces which will not see God. And no church can offer to this bleeding, dying world anything about God, except the promise of a "home in heaven" after we die. 

So then I say, no wonder God Almighty said, “Enough—

I will raise Me up a prophet who will tell the people that no one ever killed Me-I live." And God did. He has raised up over the years, many prophets. Many of them have been deified after they died. Take Jesus for instance. I don't think there is any question but what a man named Jesus lived, for we find profound spiritual truths in what he taught. If he did not live, Chrishna did, and as their sayings are very very similar, it makes little difference, as one of them most certainly lived. But a prophet is a man, not a God. God does not raise up Gods to dispute with Him.

He raises men, to whom is given a prophetic vision. 

So profound are the truths taught by these prophets that after they die, and sometimes while they are alive, they have a large following. Then, when the fullness of the spiritual truths they taught becomes evident, their followers, impressed with their teachings, used to attribute

to them Godhood. Buddha, Jesus, Chrishna, Prometheus, Osiris, Confucius, Mohammet, and a score of others have flashed across the horizon, and they have left in their wake a flood of spiritual power and truth. And a careful study of their lives and teachings convinces the student that the message of all of these prophets was the same. In every case, these true prophets came to this earth to point out to humanity the amazing Power there is in the Spirit of 

God-our Creator.

Sometimes these men were crucified. In no case did the full significance of their message break upon the earth. 

They secured a large following, but those following other prophets stuck by them, and so we have so many different systems of religion on the earth today. In each case, a true prophet of God started these religions. In no case however, until now, has the full import of what these prophets came to teach been fully realized. 

It is impossible to study the writings which the church has attributed to Jesus without knowing that that man knew God. It is impossible to study the sacred writings of any of the other major "faiths" without realizing that the prophet after whom they followed knew God. Confucius knew God. Buddha knew God. Chrishna knew God. Jesus knew God. And there have been hundreds more who have known God. The message has always been the same. They all came, not to glorify themselves, but to glorify "their Father", which of course means the invisible Spirit of God. 

But the foolish human race, led by equally foolish priests and preachers, mistook the messenger for the message, and immediately proceeded to build a system of religion about the messenger, calling him God. In no case was the messenger God. Your author is not God. Neither was Jesus. 

Neither was Abraham Lincoln. But your author, and Jesus,

YOUR GOD-POWER 

and Chrishna, and Buddha, and a score of other prophets which have the misfortune to have god-ship tacked onto them, were but men, even as you and I. They knew God. 

They had been with God. They walked and talked with 

God. The Spirit of the Living God ever dwelt in them as that Spirit dwells in you and in me. But, and mark this well, no one who ever appeared in the flesh could possibly have been Almighty God. For flesh and the Spirit of God are two entirely different things. 

The Spirit of God lives in us all. It lives in you and it lives in me. But the Spirit of God or the Spirit of Life are not flesh and never can be flesh. The two can never mix. 

What you and I are put on the earth for is to manifest and demonstrate the actual living Power of God in us, so that others, following after us, may come to know too that Great

Spirit which, to know aright, is Life Eternal. 

Christianity has failed, as has every other major system of religion on the face of the earth. None of them has been able to reveal the Power of God to humanity. And the reason they have failed is because they have insisted on preaching exclusively about Jesus, and the Apostle Paul, leaving Almighty God completely out of the picture. This is a fatal mistake. Until this mistake is rectified, and until

Christianity forgets all about Jesus and begins to teach in its churches the facts of the ever-abiding Spirit of The Living God, in each of us, Christianity will continue to fail. You cannot give men and women falsities and ask them to accept them as truths, especially when you are dealing with the Realm of the Spirit of God. Man knows today that God exists. Man knows that Jesus was not God. 

Man also knows that he was not born "in sin" and he also knows that he can never find God thru anything any

church teaches. But man will find God, because he will go where it is evident God is. 

It makes no difference whether or not the church calls the man who is revealing God an atheist or not. The church couldn't do anything else, for anyone who does not believe what the church asks him to believe is, according to it, eternally damned. Well it so happens that Almighty God is revealing His truths to the world thru a man whom the church says is eternally damned. But God cares little for that. The time has come in human affairs when this world will find God, even if it has to throw overboard every last vestige of Christianity and every other "anity" there is. 

And there are lots of them.

This world is dying for one little bit of the truths and the Power of God. Men, like animals and brutes, are ruthlessly murdering each other in the most ghastly human slaughter of all ages. Yet our churches who call themselves "agents of God" and who have the audacity to tell you and me that if we don't believe that Jesus was God we shall go to hell-these good churches, making their living by teaching impossible theories of God, stand idly by, utterly incapable of throwing the Power of God against anyone or anything.

One grain of the actual Power of God can straighten this world out, and the shocking thing about it all is that there does not exist anywhere, in any church, a single theory of God which promises anything more than "salvation from sin" and a "home in heaven". My friend-Almighty

God created this earth for man. He created man for the earth. The ideal way for man to live is in conscious recognition of the Power of God in him, and this of course means the power to consciously apply and use that Power

YOUR GOD-POWER 

against all conditions which have been brought about as a result of man's unbelief of, and ignorance of the present existence of The Power of The Spirit of Almighty God. 

You who have followed me so far, know by this time that the Power of God actually exists. You know that you can find and use this staggering Power. And you know that the Spirit of God in you can make you an overwhelming conqueror in the game of life. Even an imbecile recognizes the fact that with God in the life, all things can be possible. 

Yet the only concept of God offered to humanity today has nothing more to offer than "salvation from sin" and "a home in heaven.”

Every hour you spend in fellowship with The Eternal God is fraught with so much dynamic Power that you little dream what those moments can mean, not only to you but to the whole creation. I do not believe there are a dozen men or women on the face of the earth who have been so close to God that they are qualified to teach others the way to God. For to teach others how to find God means many years of living close to God. It means that the one who teaches others must have had such an experience with the Power of God that he or she has become a true mystic, and if there are twelve true mystics on the face of the earth, I do not know where to find them. 

By "mystic” I do not mean a crystal-gazer or psychological faker, of which there are many. I mean by the word "mystic" a man or woman who has been, and keeps so close to Almighty God that he or she instantly recognizes the things of God, and, by close communion with God, is qualified to teach those truths to others. It is possible for you, my friend, to become a true mystic. It will take many years. 

But what years they will be. It's something this world knows nothing about. It's something this world will have

to know something about before universal peace can come to it.

So near and yet so far. All the Power of the Spirit of God in the world, only awaiting man's willingness to understand the existence of God here and now. And yet, all our churches can offer us is an old pagan theory that Almighty God, in the form of Jesus, was killed by a handful of Italian soldiers some 2000 years ago. What a pity that concept of 

Almighty God ever came to this world.

I want you to get clearly the picture of the relationship which exists between this material world in which we live, and the invisible Realm of God which, strangely enough, is the only Realm of Reality there is. What we see here round us, is temporary only. The big trees of California may live for three thousand years, but they will eventually fade away and die. And what is three thousand years compared with eternity?

The Realm of the Invisible Spirit of God is an eternal 

Realm, and, I repeat, it is the only real Realm there is. 

Everything connected with God is eternal. Everything connected with the visible realm is fleeting and temporary. 

We must clearly understand this if we are to make much progress into the Realm of God while we live on this earth. 

Religion has pictured the "life after death" for members of all "faiths" as a place where one sings songs, plays harps, and worships a visible God thruout countless ages, and thru all eternity. That concept cannot be true, for nothing visible can be permanent. It must have had a beginning and therefore it must have an end. 

In the Realm of the Spirit of God there is life and there is Power. But this Realm must ever be invisible. It can

YOUR GOD-POWER 

never be visible. If it were, it would only be temporary. 

I wonder if you can imagine a spiritual invisible Life which continues down thru countless ages, and at the same time imagine that Realm to be one of infinite silence. I can. 

It has been revealed to me what the Realm of God is, and, more important, how that Realm operates. 

I know how the Invisible Power of God in that Realm can be contacted and used here and now, and I am trying to show you how that can be done. I am writing this on a fine walnut desk. It is a visible certainty. I see it, and everyone else who comes into my bedroom-study sees it too. But scientists can look at this desk in its reality, and find it to be a mass of circulating energy. They find that while it appears visible, and is visible to the naked eye, in essence it is but a mass of electronic energy. 

This brings us to the end of material science, and it is here that we begin to get our real insight into the relationship which exists between the physical material realm all around us, and the invisible Realm of God-in us. Note those two words "in us". For that is the only place where we can go to find God. That is the only place we can go to find "heaven". For, in us, is God. And God is the Life of us. Therefore, in us lies the answer to every problem, and in us is contained the ultimate of God. You will understand now why, in this series of Lessons, I have hammered so consistently and persistently on the fact that the Life you live is from God, is of God, and is God. 

When that fact is grasped we at once stop looking for God in the skies. We at once forget pagan theories of the 

Eternal God which would have us believe that 2000 years ago Almighty God was killed on a cross for the "sins of the world". And it is a necessity that we do discard entirely all such concepts of God. For, and I repeat it once

more, the Kingdom of God is within us, and all who look there may find. As a matter of fact, the secret of the ages and of God is in us. you

Being in us, we had better begin in earnest to contact and use the Power, for if it exists in us, as we have found, then it exists in us for a very definite and specific purpose. 

If all of God lives in us-why? Is it not that and I may discover from God what is the meaning of life? For what other purpose would Almighty God, the Creator of the universe, ever create man, and then take up His abode in man, giving unto man His Life-complete in every detail?

Was it to keep us in suspense? Was it to keep us mystified and blindfolded? Was it that we might go thru life and after that go down to the grave having in us all the time the very Life which is God and not knowing it? I don't think so. I believe that the Life of God which God gave to us when we entered this physical realm was given to us so that, thru it, we might come to know God and the purpose God had in placing us here with His own Power within us.

I have little use for theologies which teach us that we cannot know what the mind of God is until after we die. 

I believe we can know the Mind of God, and the Power of God, right here on the earth. I believe God placed us here, with His Life in us, for that very purpose. He could have had no other purpose than that. 

But why then, you may ask, has the human race lived on the earth for so many thousands of years and not discovered the facts I am now bringing to humanity? Well, there are two reasons. The first is that well-meaning but awfully credulous people believed what priests and preachers told them to believe about God. The second reason is that man has never before progressed in his

YOUR GOD-POWER 

evolution to the place where he could comprehend and logically and normally understand what it means to have 

Almighty God living in him.

It's all part of one great big evolution. You may call it part of a "divine plan" if you wish to. I don't care what you call it, for the facts are as I have stated them. Just as the seed of the wheat must be placed in the earth by our Idaho farmers before it can bring forth abundantly, so also has it been necessary for man to live in a spiritually dead condition, without authentic knowledge of the present existence of God before the facts of God could be brought to him.

But this war has brought the climax of man's degradation without God. It has brilliantly demonstrated how far wrong the human race can go without authentic knowledge of God in the human life. And God so operates that when humanity stands on the brink of total disaster, and it certainly stands there now, He will raise up some prophet who will fearlessly proclaim the good news of the Power and the Presence of the Almighty in human lives. 

The human race is now, for the very first time, beside itself. Thinking men and women are wondering what this disaster and this bloody wave of death is all about. They are asking this question: "Is there no higher Power anywhere which can take a hand in this brutal affair?” And

I am glad to answer them that there is a higher Power, not "somewhere" but right in every human life, which most certainly can take a hand, and will take a hand when man is intelligently shown Who, What, and where God really is.

Man is a very perverse animal. Fundamentally he is honest. But religion has never been honest. And we have believed priest and preacher, who for worldly gain and political power, would play fast and loose, not only with

the religious emotions of their followers but with Almighty God Himself. Well, those organizations will be the first to evaporate when the Truths of God become universally known. They will be shown up in all their hypocrisy and sham. They will be shown up in all their rottenness, for most of them are rotten at the core.

However-all this is but a passing show in a passing evolution from man to God. And you and I now find ourselves in a rather interesting situation. We find that we are physical beings, living in a physical world, which means a temporary world, and yet, all the time we are on the earth, we have available to us all the invisible Power there is in the vast Realm of Reality, which is the Realm of The 

Spirit of God. And stranger than all, that Realm of Reality lives in us and is at our instant call. 

What then is the meaning of this? Well, that is simple too. For the Almighty never would have put His Spirit in us without having a very definite purpose in so doing. 

What is that purpose? Let me tell you. That purpose is to reveal the Power and the Life of God to the entire human race. When that has been accomplished, and when all men have thrown away their man-made anthropomorphic "gods" and discovered the Realm of the Reality of the Creator, then Almighty God will wind this physical picture up so fast we'll all wonder whether we ever lived as physical beings or not.

God's plan has ever been to allow man to progress thru the process of evolution until the time came when man could, and would, recognize the existence, in him, of Almighty God-the Creator of man. So, to bring that about, God placed in man His very own Life. Had it not been for "holy-men" and others who, Hitler-like, attempted to take the reins of government out of the hands of God and put

YOUR GOD-POWER 

those reins in their own hands, man would have discovered God ages ago.

But man is ready to discover the Power of God now. 

The stage is set for the greatest world spiritual revolution humanity has ever witnessed. For nations shall be born in a day. And the glory which accompanies the recognition of the Presence of God in the Life, will so outdazzle what we now know as life that there will never be much comparison between them. We shall probably never know that we inhabited a physical, material earth as material, physical human beings. And in that day, which is so very close, God Almighty will wipe out what we think is a physical material earth, and all this will be replaced by a new earth and a new heaven, and the Light of those two will be the glory which is God.

I'm not so sure that what we believe to be actual and physical and material is not but a dream which we dream because we have lived so far from God that we have become accustomed to what appears to be material. If this walnut desk in essence is electrical energy, who shall say that when we are raised from an existence without God to an existence with God, these things which we believe to be the real things of life shall not instantly pass away?

I'm not so sure that it is all real anyhow. Only the invisible things of God are real. Incidentally, here is the answer to a question that is often asked me: "Shall we know each other beyond the tomb?" And the answer is "No". You cannot possibly know each other beyond the tomb, and when you get into the Realm of the Spirit of the Eternal God, to live forever in that Great Presence, you won't want to know each other even if you could.

You and I live here as material physical beings. The only way we know each other is as physical material

beings. But these bodies decay. The worms come and eat them How then can you up. know your loved ones in heaven? You can't. But don't let that upset you. For when you drop this physical body, the Life which is the Real You goes back to the One Great Spirit which placed that Life of His in you in the first place. Then, and only then, when the human race has seen and understood the actual Truths of God, shall we all be one, and in One. 

The most comforting thought I know is this: From the cradle to the grave we are so close to the invisible Realm of the Spirit of God that when we recognize that fact, we can draw from that Realm of God, not only every material thing we need here and now, but every spiritual thing also. 

As a matter of fact, it is entirely possible to get so close to God here and now that one can enter and leave the body at will. I have done this many times, and shall do it many times more. 

Of what do I consist when that happens? Why I consist of what I was before the Spirit of God ordained that, for a time, I should occupy a physical body in order that I might bring to this earth some of the Truths of God. Where was I before I came to this earth? Probably on some other inhabited planet, where the Life-Vibrations are very much higher than they are here. Well, why do I not remember? -perhaps I do. But in any event, when I came to this earth to occupy a physical body in order that the men and women of this earth might know God, I had to take upon myself the stature of the people amongst whom I came. 

My life does not belong here, beloved. Neither does yours. We cannot all become prophets of God. Very seldom does God raise up a prophet, and when He does it is always for some specific purpose. I know why I was raised 

I know how my ministry will end. And I know where up.

YOUR GOD-POWER 

I shall be after my temporary life on this earth is ended. 

I came from God. I shall go back to God. And that is exactly where I long to be tonight. This old world does not interest me one iota. It has nothing I want. It cannot have. 

For I came from God. I did not come from the earth. And so did you, beloved. The origin of your life was, and still is, Almighty God. The life you now live is now, and always was-the Life which is God. You just happen to be manifesting in the human form. 

Now how hard will you try to enter into the fullness of the consciousness of God in you? You will not get very close to God unless you want to. And you'll have to want to hard. And you'll have to be awfully humble. You'll have to become as a little child or you'll never see God. You'll have to cry out as I did many times: "Oh God, take me, break me, and then make me." And God will, when you get in earnest. If I can, by my writings, bring this absolute knowledge of the Absolute God to this earth, and I am doing just that, then my life on this earth will have been well worth while. 

A man said to me yesterday: "Well, Dr. Robinson-is there not a chance of your falling away and getting back into the world again?" Well, I have never left the world.

I have nothing to fall away from. I can go out and get drunk tonight if I want to and there's nothing can stop me.

I don't want to, but if I did that's exactly what I'd do. For when a man knows the God-consciousness in him, he still is the same free-will agent he was before. But I have yet to see the man or the woman who ever really knew God who ever left God.

There could be no object in that. For anything this material life has to offer cannot compare with what the Realm of the Spirit of God has to offer-can it? Why then should

I fool around with material physical things? My life is a hidden life. It is hidden deep down in the Realm of God, exactly where I am trying to get you to live your life. And after one foretaste of the glories of this invisible Realm of God, what can this earth have that can possibly interest me? 

You can see now, I believe, a bit clearer, where I am leading you. When you give up an hour or half hour daily to be with God, I believe you will much more intelligently understand what it is all about after this Lesson. Get the picture clearly, please. You are a physical material being. 

Yet you have in you all the Power God has. That Power is real. It is Reality-absolute Reality. It is the only Reality there can be. 

Now then-what is your play? Why it's simple. You will spend so much of your time quietly getting acquainted with God. You will do this every day. At the beginning you will use the affirmations I have given you. After a while you won't need them. And you will always realize the difference which exists between the Realm of the Spirit of God and this fleeting material world. So you will quietly wait upon God, and you won't wait very long until the invisible Realm of God begins to unfold its own staggering 

Spiritual Power to you.

And when it does, your feet will be on so solid a rock that you'll never leave it. Please read and re-read this Lesson. There is a lot of Spiritual Power in it. The Spirit of 

God was very close to me when I wrote it. Perhaps you would like to know the exact time and date. Well, it's exactly 8:00 P.M. the night of August the 11th, 1943. Many people write asking me when I was born. It was on July the 5th, 1886. But please forget me. Get the message I bring. That is what matters. That is all that matters in your dealings with me.

YOU HAVE COME FAR ENOUGH along the trail which leads to God to know that as long as you live you will know the beauties and joys which accompany the life which is hidden from the world in the Power of the Spirit of God. I mean that deep abiding Power, silent, noiseless, emotionless, peaceful Power. And, as you discover It, and when you recognize Its ever-abiding presence in you, your life becomes a life that is hidden with God. every You don't look any different to the world, but the world looks a lot different to you. You see the unreality of thing material. You see the reality of everything unseen.

As a physical being, you live surcharged with the Power of God, and that Power, being solely and completely spiritual, shows material things up in their real light, which is quite secondary to the spiritual realm of reality in you. 

It is quite possible to get so close to God that the material world around you seems but a dream. Living for so many generations, as we have, without the God-consciousness, and solely considering the material, there comes to us, when we know God, the true sense of values between the spiritual and the material.

That sense of values brings with it the absolute consciousness of the fact that when the spiritual life ascends over the material, or when the spiritual life becomes what it should be, the overwhelmingly more important part of your existence on this planet, then you begin to see things 214

215 in their proper perspective. Old things pass away. All things become new. Whereas, heretofore, you considered this physical life about all there was to look after, you now give practically all of your time, not taking care of the physical but trying to get closer to the spiritual. 

This brings many things to pass. This sense of the nearness of God to you-in you, in fact-cannot help but bring with it an overwhelming sense of confidence, not in you, but in the power that lives in you. You learn as your steps tread this pleasing way, that with the Spirit of God consciously abiding and living in you, there is little for you to worry about. If you have been breaking your neck to amass a sum of money sufficient for your old age, you at once realize the foolishness of this, because, with the Author of all Power and supply in your life, there is nothing you can need, at any stage of the game, that the Spirit of 

God in you cannot bring to you. In fact, the reason you are so absolutely sure of this is that the Life of God in you is these very things you need.

There is no Power greater than the Power of God. And with that Power in you, you have an over-abundance of all supply, ever available to you, whether you be young or old. When that Power is given to understand what it is you need, when you impress upon the Spirit of God in you, by your desire, the things of which you have need, that Power, in Its own peculiar and sure way, begins at once to bring those things to pass. And, having created the universe and all that therein is, you may rest with absolute assurance on the fact that this God-Power, which is your God-Power, is also abundantly able and willing to bring to whatsoever things you have need of. the you

There seems to be something almost automatic about way God operates. It is automatic. You have the Spirit

YOUR GOD-POWER 

of God living in you, and all the Power there is in the Realm of the Spirit of God is always available to you. All right-you need something. So your desire registers in the 

Realm of the Spirit of God in you. And what happens?

Why all the invisible Power God is, at once goes to work to bring into your life the very thing you need. You keep the desire burning brightly, and then you leave that desire at the door of God, knowing full well that God not only can, but will, bring into actual manifestation that very thing, or those very things.

You forget that you need them, and, having consciously impressed upon the Spirit of God what those needs are, you rest in the absolute assurance that they are already being made by God, in the Realm of God, and will surely some day soon come to pass. It is in the moment you take it for granted that the God of this world operates. I remember some time ago, when I was rather puzzled about certain sensations of trouble in the region of my heart. If you have read any of my former writings, you will know that I suffered two very dangerous attacks of coronary thrombosis. Well, I knew how to overcome those attacks by the Power of God, and I did. 

But some time after, there still was a sense that all was not as it should be in the chest, and it bothered me. I knew that the Almighty can here and now bring a complete and permanent recovery to anyone, regardless of what the disease may be. Yet there was this sensation of something or other not right in the heart. Well, I was driving to Boise one day with a friend, and, as usual, I was thinking about the Realm of the Spirit of God-in me. All of a sudden there seemed to come to me this thought: "Well, if God lives in you, why don't you tell God just exactly what it is you need done inside of your chest?" And I did. I just

There had been a reservation in my thinking. I had accepted healing for the coronary thrombosis, but I had made the mental reservation that naturally there couldn't be a perfectly complete healing. I thought there must be some little thing left there. So there was. But when I impressed upon the Spirit of God that I wanted full and complete restoration, it came. but So I say, it is in the moment when you pass your troubles over to the invisible Spirit of God in you the relief, or whatever it may be, will come to you. There is nothing surer in this world. Heaven and earth shall pass away, the Power of the Spirit of God shall never pass away. And if that Spirit is left, everything worth while is left. For God is all in all, and the whole material world could pass out of the picture. But if the Spirit of God was left, you would be left, and with you and God together, that's everything so far as you are concerned.

So then, as you travel along this pathway to God with me, get as quickly as you can to the place where the Spirit of God takes the ascendancy in your thoughts and actions. 

Get to the place where it is revealed to you how very shallow and hollow the things of this material life really are. 

They fade and die. You never will.

The Spirit of God in you-that Breath of Life which comes from the Almighty never dies. So you really never die. This physical body may pass away, but you'll never die spiritually, for God doesn't die, and the motivating 

Force in you is the Spirit of God. You ask what state you will be in after the death of the physical body? Well, you'll be exactly as you were before you became a physical body.

I'd find the Power of God here on earth if it were the last thing I ever did.

For if you find that Power here on the earth, if you become conscious of God in you here, that consciousness of God will never leave you, regardless of whether you manifest in the body or out of the body. So long as you live as a physical body, you have the ability to comprehend God. 

You have the will to comprehend God if you so desire.

Of course, being in tune with the Infinite, it is my life's work to bring knowledge of the Power of God to as many as I can. I have done this for sixteen years and shall continue to do it. When my time comes, I shall have the supreme satisfaction of knowing that I fought a good fight, I finished the course, and while I am not interested in rewards, I know that while I see now as in a glass darkly, I shall then see face to face. For when we lose this sense of the material-when we leave this body of death, those of us who knew God here shall know God forever and ever.

I don't care about a conscious existence after this life. 

In fact I wouldn't want one. It will be sufficient to know thru the eternal ages, not my own consciousness, but the consciousness of God will be my final end. And yours too, if you will but follow the path I have marked out for you to tread. I'm not much of a Guide compared with the staggering truths I am teaching you. But I have seen those truths. I have seen God. I do know God. And anyone who has seen God, and anyone who knows God is separated from this earth. He lives here, but his heart is not here. 

Where his treasure is, there too is his heart.

this earth. Oh-I look after my obligations, I make a good home for my family, and I give them all the love and affection I can. But beloved, I don't belong here. I never did. I live for nothing else than to reveal the Power of God to others. Rather, perhaps I should say that I live to tell others where they may find the actual and literal Power of God.

At the very core of your being-is God. You can get down to that core and you can find God. You cannot find 

God by believing that Jesus was killed on a cross. You cannot find God thru believing that Almighty God came down from heaven to earth to save a handful of humans from eternal disaster. You cannot find God that way. And the reason you cannot find God thru such "beliefs" is because they are not true. This sort of thing will have to go out of the picture. And it will go out. 

Our theological organizations will howl long and plenty. 

In fact, they have been doing that at me ever since I came into the international religious picture. But that matters little. This, and similar theories which put God "in heaven" or any other place at a distance, lead men away from God instead of leading them to God. Falsity can never lead to truth, and any philosophy which removes God from the human heart and life, replacing God with "inbred sin" etc., is a false philosophy. God is not in falsity, even though it be the church which is teaching the falsity. 

God is in truth, and there can be no religion higher than truth, can there? Of course not. No-my friend-God is in you. He is at the very core of your being, and it's up to you to dig deep down into your being till you get to the place where God's presence becomes a living Reality to

But God did exactly that. It was a very big thing to bring you and me into this world and endow us with Life. But God did that. It was a big thing to change that little squalling baby into a full-grown man.

So why do you worry and fret about the ability of Almighty God to bring to you in this life, the very few material things you think you need? If you are wise you won't be looking for material pleasures or blessings-you'll be trying to get closer to God-the Great Heart of all supply. The Great Spirit that made us all. And if you get close to that Power, you'll have everything you need, and a great deal more, too. 

You'll have over you a protecting Spirit which will never your feet slip. It will guide you safely thru the devious ways of life, and if anyone tries to harm you, It will protect you. How well I know that. I cannot define to you what God is. But I can tell you what effect the Power of 

God has on and in a human life. I cannot tell you how God made the universe, but I can tell you what the Power of God does when Its presence is realized in the life of a human being. As the poet so aptly said: let "I know not where His islands reach;

In the big things, of course, the guidance and Power of God are an absolute surety. He knew that you were going to make a journey and He knew that you would need accommodations. There is nothing-absolutely nothing the Spirit of God does not bring, when you know that Spirit lives in you. 

You see-if you have the Power of God in you, and you need anything, you automatically turn your thoughts and desires to the God-Power in you, and even before you have begun to ask it, the Spirit of God has already begun to bring into actual manifestation the very things you need. 

And probably a good deal more too. It is not a case of begging God, or trying to incur favor with God. It is simply a case of allowing the Spirit of God to work and manifest in and thru you. When I was called to this ministry I knew what a religious phenomenon this Movement would be. I knew the enormity of the task of bringing to the world, for the first time, the actual truths of God. The immensity of my task staggered me quite a bit. But I knew that I had sufficient of the Power of God in me to carry me over every obstacle. 

However, I started out on my mission for God with a full head of steam and the safety-valve blowing over. Believe me, I minced no words when dealing with man-made traditions which have for so many years masqueraded as being of God. And it was right and proper that I should

225 do this. In no other way would we have received the international recognition we now enjoy.

I put a lot more of the Spirit of God in what I write now than I ever did. After sixteen years of leading hundreds of thousands of men and women into the Kingdom of God, the peace that is God abides with me and in me to an ever increased degree. I do not pull my punches, but I do outpour the Spirit of God in whatever I write. And somehow or other, looking back over the past sixteen years, I like to be mellowed. I just like the hand of God to take me, break me, and then remake me. That's what God always does when one finds the Power of God in life. Old things shuffle off, and everything you see has a new meaning. Where there was nothing before, now there is plenty. Where there was plenty before, now there is nothing. For the normal man, without conscious knowledge of God, has a very twisted idea of things and their relationship with God. 

And the Power of God soon changes all that.

So then, as you continue to live with God and daily talk with God, your life will be mellowed. It will be made sweeter.
