God is very close to you, and always will be. But there exists a little gap which is rather hard to penetrate unless you give your full effort and your continued effort. It is of gossamer thinness. It can be penetrated. 

This gap is there because you are a physical being. God meant you to be a completely spiritual being, and you will be ere the evolution of the human race is complete. The method God decided to use is this: He placed in man, when He created man, His very own life. He placed that 

Life in a physical body. Then, He left man to himself to work out his own salvation by discovering in him that 

Divine Spirit of God-his Maker. Had man made that startling discovery right at the start, there would be none of the crime, sin, and murder in this world which is plaguing it today.

But man did not do that, and man won't do that until the human race gets so sick and tired of living without God that it turns its eyes inward to where God lives. Then, and only then, will the human race find God, and all the 

Power that is God. I don't believe God expected man to instantly discover the Spirit of God within man. But however that may be, we know with absolute certainty that the Almighty did place His Spirit in man; and we know something else—we know that this Spirit which is God still lives in each one of us. If It did not, how could we live? 

Then, it being an absolutely established and proven fact that the Life of God, or the Spirit of God does exist in man, where else can man go to find that Life? He cannot go anywhere and find it, except in himself. This is contrary to the teachings of theology, which have offered us the picture that man is in a lost and fallen state, far away from

YOUR GOD-POWER 

God. But that picture can do nothing for man except alienate him from God. It is a negative picture. It is an untrue picture. It does not have in it the germ of the salvation of the human race. It only teaches the separation of God from man.

But man is not separated from God. Man is as close to 

God as he will ever be. The trouble is that man does not know this staggering fact because none has told him about it. When the Almighty put His Spirit in man, He did it for a definite purpose. That purpose was that man might find his hidden treasure. But man did not find It. He looked every place under the sun except the right place. “Holymen", priests and preachers came along and told the human race that they had the answer. Systems of religion came into existence, thousands of them, and they told the human race that they had the answer.

But neither "holy-men" nor systems of religion have the answer. If they had, this old earth would not now be bathed in human blood as it is. But there is an answer, and that answer is so far removed from theological teachings that there is no connection between them. The answer lies in the fact that the Spirit of the Almighty God lives in you and has always lived in you. When you grasp that fact, and when you pursue it to its logical conclusion, and find the Spirit of God in you, then you are where God intended you should be in the first place. You are where God wants you to be.

Now-realizing the fullness of God in you, you are complete master of your soul. Your destiny is in your own hands. Your Power to overcome is close to you. It is "Your 

God-Power". And the reason the Almighty gave His own breath, His own Life to you is that you can use that Life of God right here on the earth. When you do, you'll be

TEEN 

231 where God wants you to be and then God, in His infinite wisdom, can speak thru you to a world which is dying for one little bit of Truths of God.

Finding the fullness of the Spirit of God in you is a very pleasant task. You do little more than keep as close to God as you can, and every day you get a little bit closer. You sink into God, as it were. You have your definite periods which you have set aside for communion with God, and you never let anything interfere with these hours. Then, thru the day, whenever your thoughts are not occupied with the things of this world, you turn once again to God. 

You repeat one of the affirmations I have given you.

And so the days, the weeks, the months go by, and with the passing of every fleeting hour, the inner Presence of God gets more real to you. What is happening is simply this: you are recognizing more and more the fact that God is in you, and therefore you can get just as close to God as you care to. This communion with the Spirit of God is very real. It is something which none can take away from you, no matter what the effort to do so may be. For your life is in your hands. And it makes no difference where you be or what you may be doing, the Spirit of God in you can never be separated from you. If It could, you couldn't live another minute. may 

So then, just as long as you have life, you have God. And

God and you can go a long way together. I am often asked if it is advisable to attend some church when you are finding the existence of God in you. I don't advise doing that. If you do, your eyes will at once be taken off the True God and you will be asked to believe in a manmade substitute for God. You will be told that Buddha, or Chrishna, or Prometheus, or Osiris, or a score of others, were actually God.

That will get you away from God. For there is only one 

God. That One God did not have a son or a daughter or a mother or a father or an uncle or an aunt to represent 

Him on this earth. That One God is Spirit. Never was anything else. Never could have been anything else. Never will be anything else. And that One God-that Spirit-lives in you and is the motivating Power of your life. Why then waste time looking for God elsewhere, at the same time running the risk of losing the experience you have? 

For if you are successful in your search for God, you will search for God with a single eye. You will see God clearly. There will be no doubt at all in your mind. There will be no unbelievable theories to cope with. There will be no "creeds" nor "dogmas" to worry you. There will be no stories about God, for you are dealing with Almighty 

God direct. And when you deal with the Almighty direct, there is no need for any of these things. You talk with God direct. You absorb the spiritual Power of God direct from 

God. You live your life under the conscious direction of

God, and with the full knowledge that the Spirit of God does live in you. Why then place yourself in danger? So

I say, keep away from churches. I have no objection to your attending New Thought meetings, but if and when you hear Jesus advocated as God, or any other "man-god" advocated as the Almighty, stop going. It will only hinder. 

The best way to progress in the Realm of God is to spend much time in secret with God alone. There you know you are safe. There you find peace. There you find solace, and Power, and wealth, and everything else you need to make your earthly life successful. And when is your life successful? When it is getting closer and closer to God. So be very constant in your moments with God. It very advisable to have the same hours of the day for is

TEEN 

233 communion. And don't spend too much time telling God what you want. You can employ your hours more profitably in getting acquainted with God. Listen to what God has to say to you. After all, to recognize the Presence and Power of God in you-well-there's your supply.

MANY PEOPLE WRITE ME asking if they should have some church home while studying with me. I answered that question in the last Lesson, but I'll go into it here a bit more. I don't want to hurt anyone's feelings, but in dealing with truth one has to go contrary to tradition most of the time. The whole thing here is just this-"What do you gain by going to church and listening to a theory of God which, to say the least, is a thousand miles from the truth?" 

Either the story told by the churches is true, or it is not true. If it is not true, why waste time attending these good churches? About all you can get there is a dissertation, elevating or not, of what the priest or preacher thinks about Jesus, the Apostle Paul, or Peter, or some other character who lived about 2000 years ago. 

In the Roman Catholic church one gets something a bit different. There one may witness a modified form of vaudeville performance, which may be interesting to some, but which I could never see anything in. But taken on the whole, I believe you can safely stay away from all such performances as these. It is simply a case of an organization undertaking to direct the destinies of your religious life, and to do your religious thinking for you. Of course, if you are not capable of thinking for yourself, then by all means let them. But if you are not capable of thinking for yourself, then you certainly are in a most pitiable condition. The theory that Jesus Christ formed a church 2000 234

years ago, and gave to that church the "keys of the Kingdom" is the greatest piece of theological poppycock of all. 

If these organizations knew anything at all about God, and certainly if Almighty God gave unto them the “keys of the Kingdom", then most assuredly in this period when the world is tied up in the most frightful war of all times, this organization should be able to demonstrate the Power of God against the enemies of God. But it can do nothing of the sort. Therefore, it knows nothing about the Power which is God. So, why waste time with it? Almighty God does not operate like that. Whenever a man or a woman finds God, as I have previously pointed out, that contact will be personal. It will not come “thru” anyone. Nor can anyone intercede with God, or the mother of God if that foolish thing were possible, for you. All such teachings as these are the very things that keep men and women from God. They interpolate a man-made system of ecclesiastical graft and humbuggery which, to those who know God, is pitiable to behold.

Yet I suppose this had to be. For you see, the growth into God must needs have been an evolution. It is an incomplete evolution even yet. For the work of God is not yet finished. The picture the Master-Painter is painting is not yet completed. The colors have not yet all been applied. And while it is quite apparent from the incomplete picture what it will be when finished, not all can see the Master's design. But some of us can. So, I suppose it had to be that man, to find God, must crawl up thru a thousand and one systems of superstition. There were always those who, for personal gain, would impose upon the ignorance and credulity of the masses. And this has been done and is still being done. It is this which has brought into existence the maze of dissenting theological organizations. It

YOUR GOD-POWER 

is this which has hidden God from the masses. The day is close at hand when the masses, who automatically know God when He is presented to them, are pushing to one side these man-made theological lemon pies, and are headed right straight into the Realm of the Spirit of God. For regardless of how dark the theological night of organized religion may be, man will, sooner or later, struggle thru to God.

Man will do this because he instinctively knows God when he sees or hears about God, and the reason for this is simple. I have shown you that the Spirit of God lives in us all. That Spirit knows Itself. And until man works out his final and complete "salvation”, if you want to use that word, theological lemon pie will not satisfy. It will for a while. But eventually man will plow thru to God. If he cannot get the aid of our churches he will leave them alone and find God entirely outside of them. This is exactly what is happening today. Honest man, naturally seeking for 

God, heard our churches say that they had Him. So man went to find out. And he did find out. For he does not waste any time on them further. He went to see, he saw, and what he saw did not bear the true stamp of God.

So now, on Saturday and Sunday nights, and to a lesser extent every night in the week, our young boys and girls flood our saloons, drinking-places, brothels, etc., until today the morale of our young folks is lower than it has ever been. They scoff at God, and there's the pity. For they have mistaken the churches for God. So they try to find satisfaction somewhere else. 

And I don't blame them. The churches are to blame for our young sons and daughters frequenting these places of drinking, lust, and low morals. If they knew the truths of 

God, and if they knew the Power of The Spirit of God,

and if they taught these truths to the young people, they would rush to hear them. But no. Our churches have to "stand fast in the traditions" they learned in their childhood, and the whole world can go to hell for all the churches care.

They have to have their "Jesus-the Light of the World”. 

They have to have their "masses" and their "Lord's Suppers". And they have to have their confession boxes where the priest forgives "sin" with one hand and takes in the shekels with the other. Oh the blight of human religious superstition. How long, Oh Lord, will it take to make men and women see that the Great Creator-the Spirit of Godcan never be found in a church-house, but can be found deep down in the human heart?

What then is there we can have to take the place of these "god-houses"? Nothing. For nothing is necessary. 

When you meet God, and commune with God, you don't want anyone around. You don't want to get out in a congregation-you want to be alone with God. For only in the silence, alone with God, can you find the Power of the Almighty. It is a personal discovery. The thrilling Power which comes sweeping into your life will never come in a church-house. Too many go there just to be seen, and from force of habit. I'm not saying they are bad. I am merely saying that God is not to be found there. Why bless your soul, if the preacher or priest of one church in America knew the indwelling Power of The Spirit of God, he would throw away his priestly robes so very quickly that his audience would think he had gone out of his mind. 

For God is universal. God is free. God is very far removed from the theory of God advanced by any system of theology today. And if proof of this should be needed, ask yourself why it is that all these "agents of God" stand idly

YOUR GOD-POWER 

by, absolutely powerless in this time of the world's greatest disaster. Do you mean to tell me that the Power of God is incapable of dealing with such madmen as Hitler, Mussolini, and Tojo? Then why is it that no church organization on the face of the earth can show men and women anything about the Power that most certainly can step into the picture and drive such madmen into permanent oblivion? The answer is simple-they have a form of Godliness but they have no Power in it. In other words, it's a hollow sham. An honest sham, but a sham nevertheless. 

And God is found thru truth, and not thru sham.

No-the smart man is the one who has learned the secret of the abiding presence of the Spirit of God in his life. 

That man never needs religious exercises. He knows God. 

He lives with God. And he has at his disposal every hour of the day and night, the fullness of the Power of the Spirit of God. And that is more than sufficient for all his needs. 

It is interesting to note that in these dying days of religious lemon pie and mush, men and women by the hundreds of thousands are turning to institutions outside of the churches, and there they are finding the Power of God. 

When the war first started there was quite an increase in church attendance. Latest figures, however, show an equally great decline. The reason for this is that in the stress of total war, the human heart wants God and will not be satisfied with anything less.

When Eddie Rickenbacker and his crew were lost and facing death on the broad expanse of the Pacific, they didn't call for a priest or a preacher-they called upon God direct, and the seagull came, as it always will. You will perhaps see what I am driving at. You will perhaps see now why I am so insistent in exposing the false notions of God held by Christianity and every other "anity" on

the earth. I have nothing against them. I do not hate them.

But they are offering the common people a theory of God that is not true. And in offering that theory they are blinding the eyes and the minds of the people to the fact of the present existence of the True Spirit of God.

We are living in a changing period of time. Unfortunately this Jesus idea of God has had many years behind it in which to grow and get a hold on the people. We shall therefore have to go thru a period which completely banishes such false and impotent theories of God and replaces them with the actual and literal Power of God, which is the only true road to complete liberation of the world from the unbelief which wraps it up like a brown paper bag today.

The idea I want you to get is that there does exist, in you, the Power of Almighty God. You can contact and use that Power at any hour of the day or night. Naturally, when you can find and use the actual and literal Power of God in your life, hundreds of millions of others, sooner or later, will discover the existence of God in them, and the world will enter a new era—an era in which the Spirit of God is the centre of attraction. In that era, man will find himself because he will find God. And to find God is to find oneself.

The old idea of an absent, crucified "god" came to us from the dark ages of the past. It originated in human superstition and fear. It was originated by priests. It is false in its entirety, and more than that, it is the one thing that is driving men and women away from the Power of God, because they think that the church is God, and they want none of it or its theories. That, and that alone, is the reason I write against the church as I do. It is not teaching the Truths of God. Almighty God, the Creator of this uni-

YOUR GOD-POWER 

verse was not born in the womb of a virgin 2000 years ago. 

Almighty God was not crucified on a cross, nor did anyone kill Almighty God anywhere, nor did Almighty God rise from the dead. Admit this, and even if you admit the crucifixion of Jesus or Chrishna as a man, the whole theological structure is blasted to atoms. 

Either Jesus Christ was a man or he was a god. If he was a god, he did not die on a cross, for no one can kill God. If He was only a man, His death means no more to the world than yours or mine. So let's get away once and for all from any and all philosophies which teach that God is only to be found by believing what Christianity teaches. 

Nothing could be farther from the truth. The Christian will tell you plainly that you cannot find God outside of his teachings. And so do the rest of the religions tell you the same thing. The fact of the matter is that it is an utter impossibility to find God, or to discover the Power of the Spirit of God in or thru anything any church teaches. And the quicker the American people discover that fact, the quicker will they find God, in Whom alone lies complete liberation from the unbeliefs which, since time began, have crippled man and the human race, and have, in the place of the Power of God, placed the impotency of pagan superstitions and traditions.

You will understand now the immensity of the task given me by the Almighty. You will perhaps understand a little bit what the past sixteen years have meant, and the concentrated opposition which has been thrown against this Teaching. You will also note, however, that because of the existence of the Spirit of God in and thru the Movement, it is still going ahead faster than ever before, and, as our weapon against attack was the silent, powerful Spirit of

God, no weapon formed against us has prospered, or ever

shall. This is a stupendous work. My message is a worldshattering message. It exposes religious mistake and supplants it with the actual Power of God. 

This is a glorious work though. It requires that I spend much time alone with God, and the more time I spend alone with God, the more of the Power of God is manifested thru me to the rest of the world. Soon-perhaps sooner than most people realize, this message of the present existence of the Spirit of God will travel round the world, and then, for the first time in the history of the human race, men and women will begin to really live, because they will have found that God lives in man, with all His Power available at all times to whosoever will. I do not say the "Psychiana" Movement will be the only one which will teach these truths. Already the Methodist Church and other churches are copying my writings and advocating their use. Of course, they are not honest enough to give credit to me for the teachings. But that's all right. It's the 

Truths I teach that count-not your author. And whoever teaches the Power of the Spirit of God will be able to swing men and women by the hundreds of thousands. 

The reason is simple. Forms of religion, priests, preachers, and others engaged in teaching Christianity or other systems of religion, have nothing substantial to offer the human race here and now. All they have to offer is a “home in heaven after you die". But the one who teaches the 

Power of the Spirit of God has the advantage over the one who offers nothing but tribulation here, and a nice home "over there". For the man or the organization that teaches the present-existing Power of the Spirit of God can offer, in addition to a home in heaven, a super-abundance of everything good while on the earth. For that man and that organization teach the Power of God. And that Power is

YOUR GOD-POWER 

not limited in performance to “beyond the tomb”. It exists and manifests here and now. And there is nothing that 

Power cannot bring into actual manifestation on the earth. 

And that's much better than only a "home in heaven after you die".

Had this world ever known the Power of God, it never could have been in the awful shape it's in today. For the 

Power of God banishes very speedily everything that is without God. The Power of God in a human life is a veritable dynamo of spiritual Power, and no material power can compare with the Power of the Spirit of God. So why our churches insist upon talking about Jesus, or Paul, or James, or Timothy, or John, I don't know. It is, of course, evidence that they know nothing at all about God, or they would not be teaching these other things which are so foreign to the Power of God. 

Who cares whether or not Jesus was killed on a cross? 

And if He was, what was the object of it? What good has it done the world? The whole idea is so fantastic that no one who has the capacity to think can accept the theory for one moment. But all men can accept the fact that the 

Spirit of God actually lives in each one of us. Even a fool knows that it is the Life which is Almighty God that causes us to exist at all. Even a fool knows that there is no Power higher than the Power of the Almighty. And even a fool knows that if the Spirit of God lives in us, It lives there to be used. God was 'not playing round with man when He gave His own Life-breathed it into man. No. God did that for a purpose. God placed His Life in man that man might use the Life and the Power of that Life to bring to himself everything God can provide.

Almighty God made man to reflect the glory and the Power of God. He created man that man might enjoy to

the very full the presence and Power of Almighty God. 

And you won't know of this Power very long before you begin to use It. And when you recognize the Power of God in you, and begin to use that Power, you will, for the first time in your life, really begin to live. You will exude Power. 

You will live Power. You will brighten the corner where you are, and the world around you, knowing that you have been with God and found the Power of God, will honor you. It will come to you for the knowledge you have. For this world wants God. It has never wanted God so badly. 

And it is not ashamed of the fact that it wants God. It is too bad that it has to go to find God in a saloon or a brothel because the church, the one organization that says it represents God on the earth, has so little of the Power of God that it cannot compete for the affections of men and women against a saloon or a brothel. These are the facts. Let's face them.

One may always find God by facing the facts and thinking God thru. Blind belief will never find God because it is blind. It has to go on "faith" instead of sight. It has to believe in age-old, dark-age superstitions instead of in the Power of God. And superstitions and age-old traditions cannot reveal God to the world. That is why they have all failed. There had to come a time in human affairs when religion experienced a complete house cleaning. That time is now. The culmination of human efforts to run this world without God led to the brutal world conflict known as the "Second World War". And a gruesome thing it is.

Every church organization in existence should hang its head in shame at ever mentioning the name of God, and at the same time being completely impotent to manifest the Power of God against three brutal madmen. Not a single thing can they do except tell you to believe in Jesus.

YOUR GOD-POWER 

But that is not enough. While these good church-members on the home front were "believing that Jesus was God”, there were some real spiritual pioneers who had just a little bit of experience with the Power of God. And these few people threw the Power of God to the best of their ability and the results were evident. 

A house cleaning in religion. That is what is in the near future. There will come to humanity such a time of joy and happiness that when the full significance of it all dawns upon a shocked church, it will wonder how it could have been so blind as to allow old unproven traditions to take the place of the actual and literal Power of God. But we should look with an immense amount of joy and satisfaction at the present changing era. We should shout for joy when we see the old church traditions giving way to the actual Spirit of God in human lives.

Quite often the Spirit of God melts me. I never try to be hard, and I'm not hard. I'm as gentle and soft-hearted as a little Persian kitten. But God has given me great Truths to bring to mankind, and it takes a fighter to fight. But every so often, after a very busy day, I go home and there, in the quiet of my study, the Spirit of God descends upon me in staggering force. And that Spirit melts me. It melts me so completely that often I cry for God to not bless me any more. I cannot stand it. And then, in those mellow moments with God, I take a bird's-eye glance at this work, and I know, beyond the shadow of doubt, that it is accomplishing that for which it was sent. It is bringing to men the actual Truth of the existence of the Almighty in this world and in the lives and hearts of men. 

I may be only sowing the seed. I know that I am. But 

I also know that the seed is falling on good ground. Never was the time so opportune for a man to come to the earth

with the actual truths of God as it is now. Never were men and women so plagued with false ideas about God. Never was the harvest so full and ripe. And God, in His infinite wisdom, chose someone who had an unquenchable faith in, and experience with, not Jesus Christ, but the Power of Almighty God.

I'm not preaching. I'm just rambling along here and writing what the Spirit of God tells me to write. As a result of this writing, the Power of God will speak to many hundreds of thousands of souls, and more work for God will be done. It is far better to write at the dictation of the 

Spirit of God than it is to doll up what you write in so much academic verbal foliage that you hide the truth. But no one misunderstands what I write. Even a child can understand it. I never was paid a greater compliment than the one which came to me in a letter from my son Alfred yesterday. He is a keen student of the things pertaining to God, and he studies very deeply. I have never tried to force my ideas on him. I gave him about everything everyone I knew had written about the true things of God. Then, when he had read them all, I sent him my Lessons. Here is what he said yesterday: "Dad-what you have written is the clearest definition of them all. I know what you are talking about." 

Coming from my own son, that was a compliment. And so I write. And the Spirit of God shines thru what I write.

And those who read, learn of God, and thus is the will of God being fulfilled. At the end of this Fifteenth Lesson I have no special instructions for you. I am covering the ground in a general way here, and shall give you in this and the remaining Lessons the most important things you should know.

But the Spirit of God will lead you thru what I write.

So keep silent with God. When the Power of God shines thru you, let it. Never try to stop it. And then, in the consciousness of God, let your light so shine before men that they shall know that you live in the Power of God. TEEN 

LAST NIGHT, SUNDAY, after having returned from a hard trip to the Coast and back, I was seated at the console of my pipe organ. My fingers were lazily toying with the keys of that wonderful instrument, endeavoring to draw from it some melody which would bring the Realm of the Spirit of God a little bit closer to me.

On the music rack in front of me was an old hymn book. 

I like to browse around old book stores and I pick up hymn books of every description whenever the opportunity arises. 

While idly turning the pages of this old hymn book, my eyes fell upon a few verses, which verses set me to thinking. I read them over many times and played them to the old Lambeth tune. As I played the melody, I read the verses of this beautiful old hymn which was written by the Reverend John R. Wreford nearly one hundred years ago. 

My object in writing of this experience is to try and show, if possible, the difference between the spirit which caused this brother to write the hymn and the Spirit I would have manifested had I been writing the same hymn today. There is an attraction about these old melodies and verses, and one cannot read them and play them without being aware of the fact that the writer must have been in conscious union with the great Realm of God. 

If you take all of these old hynms, however, and analyze them, beautiful though they are, you will find running thru them the same thread of doubt and inferiority which per- 247

YOUR GOD-POWER 

vades these beautiful stanzas which I shall reproduce in just a few moments. If I were writing this hymn today, doubt and fear would be eliminated, I assure you. It seems as though these old saints, while recognizing the existence of God, were saturated with a spirit of fear. Had this brother been writing this hymn today in the light of what my students and myself know of the actual power of God, every last sentiment of fear, doubt and inability to grasp God would be conspicuous by its absence. 

Take the first verse: 

"Lord, I believe; Thy power I own, Thy word I would obey; 

I wander comfortless and lone 

When from Thy truth I stray."

The writer believes in the power of God. He says he owns this power. He says the word or law of God he would obey. Then he says that he wanders comfortless and lone, when from the truths of God he strays.

Now if this good brother believed and owned the power of God and if he were willing to obey the mandates of this great Realm of the Spirit of God, it would be almost impossible for him to stray from these truths, wander comfortless and alone. If this good old saint wandered from the truths of God, he did it deliberately, and therefore, whatever penalty he paid in lonesomeness and discomfort was brought by him upon his own head. You see thruout this entire verse, while recognizing the existence and the power of the great law of God, there is a doubt as to his ability to constantly follow, keep in close harmony with this great God. As a matter of fact, it is not possible to get away from the presence of God and the Reverend John R. 

Wreford in 1837, when he wrote this beautiful hymn, was

TEEN 

249 either conjuring up bogey men in his mind or was afraid of the great power in which he said he trusted. 

Now the second verse gives us the picture just a little bit clearer. Here it is: "Lord, I believe; but gloomy fears 

Sometimes bedim my sight; and tears,

I look to Thee with prayers cry for strength and light.” And

He repeats the statement that he believes and I am inclined to think that this constant repetition of that statement may be an evidence of doubt. However, the good brother says he believes, so we will give him the benefit of the doubt, for I really think that he thought he really did believe. The entire first line of the second verse is spoiled in the middle because he brings in that word “but”. 

He says, "Lord, I believe; but gloomy fears sometimes bedim my sight; I look to Thee with prayers and tears, and cry for strength and light.” Now if these gloomy fears hemmed this good brother in on every side and if fears bedimmed his sight of Spiritual Truth, these fears must have been self-originated. Either that or he believed too firmly in the story of religion as handed down to him by religious advisors. No gloomy fears ever originated in the great Realm of God, and if, as he claimed in the first verse, he accepted or owned the Power of God, he didn't own it or accept in a great enough degree, else these gloomy fears would not have bedimmed his sight. 

When a man or woman knows the Power of the Spirit of God, fear is the last thing in his life. If he believes in religious tradition, fear is the greatest thing in his life. The 

Bible philosopher spoke truly when he informed his hearers that "perfect love casts out fear”, and the Realm of God is,

YOUR GOD-POWER 

if anything at all, a Realm of Life and a Realm of Love. 

It is not a sanctimonious, weak-spined, cringing spirit of life and love, but a veritable, fearless, steel-spined Spirit or experience which knows no fear and which knows no defeat. Therein is the Love of the Spirit of God manifested. 

This good old saint, in the second verse, tells us that he looks to God with prayer and tears and cries for strength and light. What a pity this good brother did that. Whenever either of my two kiddies wants an apple, a banana, or an orange, he goes and takes one. There is a dish of them handy at all times and while it would be perfectly all right for them to ask me if they might have one, yet how foolish for these little nippers to come to me crying and praying and begging me for a piece of fruit when such a bounteous supply is within their reach. 

Had this good old apostle of the gospel known a little more of the Power of the Spirit of God, instead of praying with tears running down his face and instead of crying to the great Realm of the Spirit of God for strength and light, he would not have thought twice about it, but would have gone direct to the Source of supply of strength and light and would have taken whatsoever he desired. 

Now in the third verse: "Lord, I believe; but Thou dost know 

My faith is cold and weak; 

Pity my frailty, and bestow 

The confidence I seek."

He reiterates once more his belief in God and then, more the pity, he puts in this “but” again. ‘Lord, I believe, but thou dost know my faith is cold and weak."

Now, I am somewhat at a loss to know how this good man could he believed in God and then in the same say

TEEN 

251 breath tell the world that his faith was cold and weak. 

If he believed in God, his faith was not cold and weak, and if his faith was cold and weak, he did not believe in God. There is no such thing as weak faith. There is no such thing as cold faith. Weak faith is unbelief and cold faith is absolute doubt. So you see, I am led to question the belief of this good brother in the actual, literal power of the Spirit of God.

In the second half of the third verse, he says, "Pity my frailty and bestow the confidence I seek.” Now again, I cannot imagine the Realm of God pitying the frailty of one who continually confesses his belief in that Realm. He asks 

God to bestow upon him the confidence or faith he seeks. 

Now if he asks God to bestow this confidence, is that not an admission that this confidence is lacking, and does it not disprove his opening statement, "Lord, I believe”?

If a child were to question his right to take an apple and if he were to ask me for the apple, questioning my willingness to give it to him, I would sit that boy down and go into a detailed explanation. I would show him that the fruit had already been provided; all he had to do was to take it. I might chide him a little for displaying such a lack of confidence in me.

Now this good brother asks God to bestow upon him the confidence he seeks. Do you think I could bestow upon that child the confidence he would seek if he were lacking in that confidence? I do not think so. It would be purely and simply a matter of putting me to the test. He would have to manifest the confidence needed himself, and this he could do only by going and taking the fruit and then seeing if I interfered with him. 

The Realm of the Spirit of God does not pity anyone's frailty, nor would this great Realm bestow on anyone the

YOUR GOD-POWER 

confidence blindly sought. The Realm of the Spirit of God exists. It is a Realm of Spiritual Law. If a person does not avail himself or herself of that law, do not for one instant think that the law will pity the weakness of such a one, for that is not a fact. Nor will the law come to you or to me and give us this sought confidence. It would be a violation of the law if it did. The great Realm of the Spirit of 

God exists and if you and I care to take advantage of the marvelous wealth of Spiritual power in that Realm, we find this wealth of Spiritual power will carry us onward and upward to heights of Spiritual experience undreamed of.

On the other hand, if we choose to ignore this great Realm of the Spirit of God, that is just too bad for us. The law will operate regardless of whether or not anyone takes advantage of it. There it is. Everything possible has been done by the law of the Spirit of God. The work is complete. The ocean of power exists. It is a matter for each individual to decide for himself or herself what use he will put it to, if any. We may play with a little idol and we may formulate our own theory as to just how the great God- Law should work, but playing with idols and formulating theories is a very futile and powerless pastime, for the Realm of God goes on and exists, regardless of anyone's theories and regardless of anyone's ideas.

Sad the man or woman whose present and future life is dependent upon some philosophy of life or some theory of religion, which philosophy of life and which theory of religion is not according to the law of the Spirit of God. Sad and unhappy the lot of the one who blindly cherishes to his heart some old-time tradition, believing it to be the truth, if that tradition be not the truth, and the truth of

TEEN 

253 any belief or tradition lies in the power accompanying that belief or tradition. 

Wherever the Realm of the Spirit of God is, you may depend upon it, there also is Power. There also is Life. 

There also is Peace, and there also is Infinite Love. 

The ministers do not understand why so often I write against them as I do, but perhaps when some of them read this article they will understand. If any of my readers should think for one moment that I try to be sensational in what I write and in the things I do, let me disabuse their minds. I have a good friend in Moscow in the ministry who is convinced that all I am is a clever advertising man and a sensationalist. This brother is entirely wrong, for underneath every move I make there lies a knowledge of the Spirit of Truth. These good ministers may not like the things I say and they may not like the way I say them, but this is only because there is Life and Power and Peace and Love connected with this entire movement. We are doing things. We are achieving results. We are adding followers by the thousand. If we were doing nothing at all, then, of course, we would attract no attention and our doctrine would not be true. 

I would like every reader, every Catholic priest and every preacher in the land to know that in the life of the founder of this movement there lies a deep, rich, true experience of the power of the Realm of the Spirit of God. 

I shoot straight from the shoulder, to be sure. I cannot operate any other way. I am blunt sometimes, and at all times, I think, very forceful. That is only because I am sure of my ground and only because I have a message for the church and the world too. If the church does not want the message, and it is quite reasonable to think that it does not want it, then the world will accept it.

A number of years ago, my friend, the pastor of a local church, decided to let me speak in that church. Many of the officials agreed with him that I should be invited to speak. One fine old gentleman, though, put his foot down on it and I did not speak in that church. Now I think it safe to say that any church in the United States may feel perfectly free in asking me to address the members of its congregation if it has the Spiritual welfare of itself and its congregation at heart. I have never yet spoken anywhere that the message has been anything else than a message directly relating to the union of the Realm of the Spirit of 

God with the human race.

Speaking in a large eastern city not long ago, I entered the building without anyone being aware of who I was. 

I mingled with the crowd and was very much amused at hearing one minister say to a nationally famous poet, “This fellow Robinson is dynamite. He will blow the top off religion tonight." As a matter of fact, before that meeting was over, after having listened to me for over two hours, the audience completely broke down and if ever the actual presence of the Spirit of God was felt anywhere, it was felt in that meeting. I am mentioning these things to try and intelligently show those thousands who read this article, the difference between the "faith of our fathers" and their faith today. I hope I make my point.

In the last stanza of this good old saint's wonderful hymn, he admits once more his belief in God. "Yes, I believe; and only Thou 

Canst give my soul relief; 

Lord, to Thy truth my Spirit bow; 

Help Thou mine unbelief."

255 If this good brother believed, he would need no relief of soul of any kind. With the recognition of the power of the Spirit of God comes soul relief instantaneously, and had this good brother really believed, his soul would have been the last thing to have troubled him. 

Now, he says in the last two verses that he bows his spirit to the Truths of God and asks God to help his unbelief. I have already gone into that sentence in this article, so I will close by stating that it is utterly futile to depend upon the Realm of God or to ask the Realm of God to help anyone's unbelief. Every normal human being has a brain and has thinking ability. This brain and thinking ability will make every intelligent human being admit the fact of the existence of the supreme Power. Now if this supreme 

Power exists, isn't the logical inference that it exists for us human beings, or does it exist for the cats and the dogs? 

If this great Supreme Power exists for us human beings, then isn't the logical inference that we should be able to use this supreme power?

What is there logical about the theory of salvation offered to us by the church today? Do you mean to tell me that the story of the crucifixion and miraculous birth and resurrection of Jesus Christ is logical? There is nothing in the Realm of God contrary to human reason. As a matter of fact, a child can understand the truths of the Realm of God far better than can some of the college professors or theology-loaded preachers.

It seems to me that I hear the echo of some words being wafted to me this morning down thru the ages. It seems to me those words say something like this, “Except ye become as a little child." I would not exchange the confidence, the simple childlike confidence my kiddies have in me for all

the theological arguments the world has, and so it is in the Realm of God. Supposedly clever Bible philosophers and theologians can present supposedly learned dissertations on theory of religion. The trouble is that no two of them are alike, but in the last essence, there is no way I know of that anyone can grasp the simple truths of the Realm of God by any other method than simple, childlike faith. The Spiritual Realm is a big Realm, but it doesn't take a big man to enter. It does take a big man to dispense the Truths of God, these days, and there are very few who are doing it. There are many dispensing church truths, but church truths and the Truths of God are two entirely separate things.

It is rather a coincidence, but as I was dictating this message, I turned to the hymn book and noted the hymn next to the one I have written about. I shall quote only the first verse and I shall not go into that in detail. I quote it only to show that you can go thru this entire hymn book and you will find there practically all the hymns saturated with the spirit of poverty and weakness and blindness and inability to do anything. What kind of a God do they believe in? This second hymn is entitled "Trusting", and here is the first verse: "I am coming to the cross; 

I am poor and weak and blind; 

I am counting all but dross; 

I shall full salvation find."

Now what the writer meant here was that he was playing around with a story advocated by his own religious denomination. He is attempting to find salvation by believing in an old fable, and if any of you should think it is not a fable, let me ask you to read my book, Crucified

TEEN 

257 Gods Galore. You will know then that if it is not a fable there were many other Gods beside the one whom this good brother says he is trusting. I do not want to enlarge upon this here. I merely want to bring out the thought that this precious hymn writer, an old Scotchman, by the way, had the same thought that Reverend John B. Wreford had in 1837. He was poor; he was weak; he was blind; he was counting everything in life, all these beautiful things we see around us, as dross. He was forsaking the beautiful of the present manifestations of the Realm of God for the story of the cross of Calvary, true or false. If belief in the cross of Calvary necessitates poverty and weakness and blindness and incompetence and inferiority, I want nothing to do with that belief. 

We are told that before the voice of the Lord, walls fall down. We are told that the sun was stopped in its tracks, although we now know that the sun does not move. We are told that a fig tree instantly withered at the voice of God. We are informed that the dead were called back to live again, that lepers were cleansed and health and healing followed the ministry of God. Sight was restored to the blind, the stars fell from heaven and gross darkness covered the face of the earth for one divine command. If those things are true, they are manifestations of Power and not of weakness.

The year 1943 is fast ebbing to a close, the tide is receding. The sun of 1943 is fast setting and the eventide of the year is upon us. Soon, this year will be history, but another year lies ahead. The sun will rise shortly upon a brand new year, fraught with tremendous possibilities for those who will actually believe in the presence here and now of the great Realm of the Spirit of God, and will cast aside all fear, and putting on the whole armor of faith in

YOUR GOD-POWER 

the Realm of God, will fight a fight against the power of unbelief all around them and will emerge victorious. Every moment of the year 1944 will be filled with the hallowed presence, the sacred presence of the Spirit of God. 

No trial or tribulation shall come to them whose faith is pinned in the Realm of the law of the Spirit of God. 

Blindness, weakness, poverty, despair, inferiority, all these and many others will have no place in the lives of tens of thousands of people that I know, and millions more, if they will come to a sane, sound, logical belief in the power which caused this Universe to be and which momentarily sustains both it and all human life existing in it. We do not need any unprovable, impossible, improbable story to base our belief on. All we need is a belief in the existence and the power of the Spirit of God. What difference does it make whether or not the story of the Galilean Carpenter is true?

Can there be any harm, I ask you, in trusting directly in God? Do you think God will punish you for expressing doubt in a story concerning which there is not the slightest scintilla of evidence as to its truth? Do you think you will lose your eternal soul if you believe in the Realm of the Spirit of God, reserving the right to question the divinity of Jesus Christ? Granted that all those things may be true, you will be just as safe dealing directly with the present existing Spirit of God and safer than if you were to pin your entire faith around a man for whom it is claimed he was also a God.

There are a great number of people who seem to think that because faith is required to be manifested in the Spiritual Realm, this Realm cannot possibly hold very much for them. Faith is only confidence and confidence is a much better word to use. There is nothing very strange to

TEEN 

259 me about the fact that confidence in the great God Realm must be manifested, for is it not a fact that we display confidence every day in everything around us, and besides, what sort of a Spiritual Realm would it be and how could we possibly contact it if confidence in it were not necessary? It would be a psychological impossibility for you or me to obtain anything at all from the great God Realm if we had no confidence in it.

Now, confidence in the great Spiritual Realm is divided into two parts. First, there must be confidence in the existence of the Realm itself. Second, there must be confidence that the Law operates. I do not think that any of my students doubt the existence of the Spiritual Realm, nor do I think that many of them doubt the ability of this unseen Realm or the willingness of it to fulfill the desires of those who do believe. At the same time, I find thru my correspondence that there are thousands of people who accept the existence of the great God Realm. There is no question whatsoever about this existence in their minds. 

These good folks also say that they are convinced that this great Realm certainly can provide whatever is necessary for their happiness, peace, and power. Yet they write to me, many of them, and ask me what the trouble is. Most of them very freely admit that the trouble must lie in themselves. This is undoubtedly a fact and it is a fact also that wheresoever this condition exists, it is purely and simply a matter of education or understanding. Once this understanding is clarified and the proper perspective of the Spiritual Realm is obtained, it all clears up so quickly and so beautifully.

At the present moment, as I dictate this, my car is over in the garage being washed up. Within the next half hour,

I shall leave this study and go over and get the car. The

YOUR GOD-POWER 

question of whether or not the car will be there certainly never enters into it because I have confidence in the motor company which has charge of that car. 

Last week, I was in Seattle, Washington, and while there I received a wire to make a certain purchase. There was no question in my mind that the goods I needed would be obtained. I did not have enough money in my pocket to pay for these goods then, but the question of receiving them did not enter into my mind, so I walked into one of the largest stores in Seattle and purchased the merchandise. I have an account with that store, although they had never seen me personally, but they have confidence in me and they know that when they send in the bill, it will be paid.

Now concerning the Spiritual Realm, very much the same thing happens. Any thinking person knows that there must exist, somewhere, a great Creative Power Realm. We know that somewhere and at sometime or other, certain forces were in motion, which forces have resulted in the human race as we see it today. It doesn't make any difference whether the power behind the throne is a selfcontained or self-sustained Spiritual force or whether that force is embodied in one personal being who sits beyond the sky directing operations from up there. The fact that we are here and the fact that we exist is absolute proof that there is a Creative Intelligence of some sort or other which is directly responsible for everything in the Universe. None but a fool would deny that statement. 

Now let us accept that to be a fact and the connecting thought here is that granted the existence of this Creative Intelligence, we must also grant its ability to sustain the work it created. If it were able to create in the first place, then it is still able to create. If, as explained in the first

261 Advanced Course, this great God Realm is capable of forming a perfect human being from two little microbe cells, then it certainly is capable of providing the necessary things thru life. This Universe with us human beings was not haphazardly called into being and then thrown into operation and told to shift for itself, by any means. 

The sustaining and creative power is still here. 

I think that all the members of "Psychiana” will admit this. The only difficulty that might arise is in the fact of the invisibility of the great Creative Realm. As a matter of fact, it may only be invisible to us. Our vision may be limited. We may not have reached the height of evolution yet, but the greatest forces in life are invisible, so I do not see why the invisibility of the Spiritual Realm should cause any doubt as to its existence. 

We acknowledge that the great Creative God Realm exists and we acknowledge that such a Realm must of necessity be able to supply whatsoever things we need in this life. In other words, we have Confidence that the great 

Creative Realm exists and we have Confidence that it can supply these things. That is exactly where so many people find themselves today. They believe these two points but they stop there and at this place Mister Unbelief comes in and he says something like this, "Yes, I know that the great God-Law exists and I know that it can provide for me the Spiritual and material blessings I need, but I do not seem to be able to make contact with this great God-Law." To put it plainly and simply, there is confidence in the existence of the Law. There is also confidence in the ability of the Law to perform, but there is no confidence, it seems, in the ability of these good folks to receive, for in the last analysis, that is what it amounts to. 

If belief in the existence of the great God Realm is

present, and if belief in its ability to perform is present, if also, belief or confidence in one's ability to receive were present, the triangle would be complete. So it is in one's ability to receive that the trouble lies, it seems to me, and perhaps to clarify that a little, let me say to the members of “Psychiana” that if they should be in this condition, the answer lies in the fact that they are not in earnest enough about the things they desire, because if they were confident in their ability to receive, it would certainly be manifested.

I have never been able to understand people who tell me that they are sure of the existence of the great God Realm; that they are sure of the willingness and the ability of the great God Realm to provide, yet they admit that they do not receive, and there can only be one possible explanation of that, and this explanation is that they, themselves, are either not willing to receive or have no confidence in their own ability to receive. 

Not long ago, a man came up to me on the street and asked me for a little money to get something to eat. He saw me walking down the street and probably had lots of confidence that I had the money to give him. He evidently must have had confidence that I would give it to him. 

Now do you suppose for one moment that this man had any doubt in his ability to receive what I gave him? I do not think so. The man would have been foolish had he even thought of such a thing. I stuck my hand down into my pants pocket and already his hand was held out waiting for the coin he received.

If you can adopt that attitude, or rather, if you will adopt that attitude when dealing with the Spiritual Realm, you will receive. If you do not doubt your own ability to contact the great God Realm and receive what it has for

Let me give another simple illustration, for I want to try to drive home this highly important fact to every student I have. Suppose I walk into a drug store and ask for a tube of tooth paste. The druggist goes and gets it and wraps it up and puts it on the counter in front of me. Now 

I need the tooth paste all right, but if I don't stretch forth my hand and pick it up, what good will it do me? I can stand in that drug store looking at that tooth paste until the crack of doom and it will not do me any good until I reach for it and receive it. I must take hold of it and transfer it from the possession of the druggist into my own. 

I know full well that thousands of people are going to say to me right at this point, "Yes, but how am I going to do that?" If you have perfect confidence in the existence of the great God Realm and if you have perfect confidence in its ability to do for you the things you desire, which things you cannot do for yourself, then if this confidence exists, the question of how to receive these things just simply isn't there. It doesn't exist. 

It never enters into the head. How foolish it should be if it did.

Forget religious emotions and quietly dwell on the common-sense, reasonable, logical fact of the existence of the great God Realm as it is, in you. There is nothing supernatural about it at all, nothing miraculous about it.

The first inkling of the Power of God in me came to me in a rather amazing manner. Many who read these Lessons will have read the unusual story in my Autobiography. 

But for the benefit of those who have not read that story, I'll give it briefly here. For many years I sought for God. 

In fact, when but a little child my mind was dwelling on the subject of God. There seemed to be in me a strange tie which seemed to bind me to God. I seemed instinctively

TEEN 

273 to know that the Power of God could actually be found right here on this earth. 

I was born in what is known as a "Christian" home. That means that the members of that family believed in the "Christian" system of religion. That's all it does mean. It does not mean that they knew anything at all about the Power of God. It does not mean that either of them had any experience whatsoever with The Spirit of God. It just simply means that they believed in the "Christian" theory of God. Right or wrong, that was their belief.

The father was then, and still is, a Baptist preacher, although he's a ripe old age now. But the last information 

I had was to the effect that he was still preaching. Now 

I wanted to know God, and here was my father, a minister of God. Surely here in my own home was the man who could disclose to me that actual Power of God, which Power I longed for, even during those tender years. But 

I saw little sign of God, or the Power of God in that father. 

I have never seen any such signs to this day.

Many evangelists would come round, and fervent revival meetings would be held. Altar-calls would be given, and there I was to be found, seeking God on my knees at some "penitent-form", only to be thrashed within an inch of my life when I got home for "making a fool of myself".

Well, it didn't take me very long to discover that there was none of God in that sort of thing, and my young heart almost broke. It seemed that the last place in which to find God was the Christian church. 

Well, the years went by, and as they flew, my longing for God increased rather than decreased. I won't worry you by reciting what happened in the years which followed. I was trained for the Baptist ministry and was told

YOUR GOD-POWER 

that "some day-God will draw the whole world closer to the cross" by my life. That's what the college professors told me. God has drawn the world a bit closer to Him as a result of my little life. But I lead men and women away from the cross-not to it. For God is not to be found in a "cross". God is to be found when we get away from the many traditional crosses, and look within, where Almighty 

God placed the Spirit of God in the first place.

One day, after years of intense searching for God, I decided to throw the whole Christian theology overboard. 

I had given it every chance to reveal God to me. It had failed. I was not satisfied to "trust and obey”. Nor was I satisfied with the Christian theory that "God's in His heaven, all's right with the world". Nor was I satisfied with the foolish philosophy that Almighty God, in the form of 

Jesus, was slain for my sins. That did not appeal to me.

For I wanted to find and know the actual and literal Power of Almighty God. And those beliefs did not, in fact could not, reveal any such Power to me. 

So I threw them away and started an independent search for God entirely outside of any system of religion. It was a simple matter of a human being trying to find his Creator. 

And my search was rewarded. I did find God.

The amazing thing about this whole recitation is the fact that when I deliberately faced God, and told Him that I did not believe in church theories, and was going to seek Him independently, the most amazing display of Spiritual Power ever to come into a human life came into mine. I was in my home in North Hollywood one Saturday afternoon. As usual, I had been down on my knees begging 

God to reveal Himself to me. As usual-nothing but silence. 

There had been nothing but silence in answer to my prayers for about forty years, and I was getting sick of

TEEN 

275 searching for the God I knew must exist, only to be continually disappointed in my search. So I came to the conclusion that I was after the wrong "god". I was trying to find Jesus, or the "god" of the Christian church. And I failed, as countless millions have failed to find God thru that method or down that avenue. 

So, that Saturday afternoon, and I remember it as plainly as if it happened yesterday, I looked up to the skies and, in my soul anguish, said, “Oh God-if I cannot find You, and if I have to go to hell because I can't find You, then

I'll go with the knowledge that all my life I tried to find 

You, God, and You would not reveal Yourself to me”. It was then that the Almighty did reveal Himself to me in no uncertain manner. For instead of feeling hopelessly damned and doomed, as I expected to feel, there was lifted from me the load which, since childhood, I had been carrying. It flew away. And for the first time in my life there came that indescribable happiness and freedom which all who find the Power of God experience. It was the most amazing demonstration of the actual Power of 

God any human has ever experienced.

I knew then, and I've known it more ever since, that 

God is not to be found via Christianity. I will go so far as to say that it is completely impossible to believe in the 

Christian, or any other anthropomorphic idea of God and ever find God. It is when the church theory of God is completely discarded that man finds God, and this is because, when theology is discarded, the man who discards it automatically comes closer to God than he ever was in his life before. He has no church theology to hinder him in his search for God.

Well-that was an experience, believe me. I have had many personal demonstrations of the Power of the Spirit

YOUR GOD-POWER 

of God in my life since then. There have been perhaps a dozen times when the heavens have opened up, and there has come showering down on me blessings from God to such an extent that I have had to cry out for God to stop showering His Power on me. You see-I'm quite weak. I know very little about God. And I cannot stand very much of the personal Presence and Power of God. So I have had to shout for joy, and at the same time ask God to stop raining down the blessings which only His Spirit can bring to me. [That was a mistake. I don't do that any more.]

When Almighty God decides to tell a man or a woman direct that he or she is in tune with The Infinite, believe me, that man or that woman never again doubts the existence in him or her of the Spirit of Almighty God. Never.

I don't look for those experiences any more, but I get them. 

In their place, there has come into my life the sweet abiding consciousness of the permanent existence of Godalways, ever, and beyond the possibility of separation so long as I live, or after I live. I don't want any more of those moments when God decided to show me once again what the Realm of God really contains. I know. And the only thing I want God to do now is to allow His Spirit to stay in me for a sufficient number of years that I may put my message of the Power of God completely round the globe. I haven't done that yet. I have, however, made a decided mark on the religious life of America, and I shall continue to teach and expound the truths of God as those truths are revealed to me, as long as I live. And all I ask is that I live many years yet. And, with the Spirit of God in me, subject to my desires, I shall live for many years yet.

You see-this little life of mine is wholly and definitely consecrated to God. It is my work, my vision, my call, to bring to men and women the actual Power of God, re-

Many, yes, so very many hundreds of thousands of people know God as a result of this little life, and to the end, no matter when that end may come, I shall be found giving my all to this wonderful work of bringing the actual truths of God to humanity, replacing thereby the old myths which Christianity has attempted, without success, to palm off on the human race as the truths of God. 

But I started in this Lesson to tell you how I keep close to God and how I am able to keep the God-consciousness bright and clear in my life. God never changes. It is the same yesterday, today, and forever. So then, while you and I are not constituted alike, the Spirit of God is always alike in Its manifestations to each and every one of us. 

The hardest battle in this search for the Power of God is to throw to one side the religious traditions and notions in which we were raised. To throw away forever all theories that Jesus or any other man was Almighty God-that is the hardest thing to do. If the Congress of the United States would spend but ten minutes a day quietly waiting for God to manifest His

You cannot come anywhere near God without becoming like God. You cannot spend an hour a day, dwelling on the staggering fact that all the Power there is in the Realm of God lives in you, without that Power exuding from you. 

Now will you give that much time? Are you in earnest enough about your life to spend one hour daily in the secret place where God is? That is for you to answer. If

For like begets like. God makes like God. And the fact that you are in earnest enough to spend one hour daily pondering on the fact of God in you, and reasoning it all out in the presence of God, is absolute assurance that your life can be what the Almighty intended it should be-filled

Not so very long ago I was watching a couple of friends of mine, nationally known evangelists, hold a series of revival meetings. I don't do that very often, but this couple really are in earnest. They are out to bring God to the world. The tragedy of it all is that they are trying to do this by telling men and women that they must “get under the blood". Well-I attended about every meeting. These good folks know that I have something they don't have, and I have hopes that before too long they will see the Light, and do a real work for God. Anyhow, one evening the "altar" had about 24 "souls" there who, we were told, were "weeping their way to the foot of the cross.' Coming to the back of the auditorium where I was sitting in a little corner, the male of the family of evangelists said to me: "Doc-give us a hand getting those folks into the kingdom". "Into what kingdom?" I asked. Looking at me, my friend said to me, "Why, into the Kingdom of God of course." Well, I looked at him for a few moments and then said, "Carl-do you really believe that these good folks can ever enter into the Kingdom of God that way?" He replied, "Well, but they have to get under the blood". "What blood?" I inquired. “Why, the blood of Jesus", my friend said.

I then asked him to take a little walk, and there, in the middle of the block I said to him: "Carl-what has the blood of Jesus got to do with those good people finding the Power of the Spirit of God in their lives?" Well, I had

There are two little blue birds that build their nest in my back yard. Year after year they come back, raise their little family and, when the summer is over, away they go to warmer climes. They are beautiful creatures of God.

It took a real God to create those amazingly beautiful little creatures. Yet they don't need "the blood of Jesus". They never heard of Jesus. They have no "sins" which need the death of Almighty God on a cross to wash away. And yet, who would say that man is not a far more wonderful creation than the blue birds? Those feathered songsters know nothing about a “home in heaven". They never heard of a virgin birth, or a crucifixion, or a resurrection. They came from the hand of God as God made them. Not only that, but in his communication he called me by name, and definitely knew, thru spiritual means alone, that I was in Egypt. 

What that man disclosed to me only corroborated what I already know. I have often thought what a meeting it would be if the few known Adepts were to be gathered together in one place at the same time. But this is not to be. Their works are different. And while all are as close to

Now shall we come back where we started this Lesson? 

I said to you that you would find more of the Power of God in the evening at bedtime than at any other time. 

That is true. Here is how it is done. 

At the end of the day, when you retire in your bed, I want you to be completely relaxed. This is essential, for you will make little progress if you are taut, nervous, and irritable. So then, when you get into bed, lie on your back and completely relax. If you have trouble in doing this raise one arm, hold it straight up, and then let it drop like a log to the bed. Do that several times. Then do it with the other arm. Then with each leg. When you feel that you are completely relaxed and ready for the night, don't move another muscle.

Just lie there, completely and absolutely still and relaxed. Your limbs will perhaps feel as if they were made of lead and that is how it should be. You will have little difficulty in relaxing your body at night. When you are relaxed, and when you are ready for sleep, don't move a muscle. Just lie there with your eyes closed. After a little while, you will feel your feet going to sleep. Then your hands, then your legs. But don't move after you have re- laxed for the night. Now while you are in this relaxed condition, slowly going to sleep, and with your eyes closed, I want you to look right straight ahead into the darkness. 

Do not try to go to sleep yet. Right in the middle of your field of vision you will see an area that is a bit brighter than the rest of the area which, of course, is dark. But there is that bright spot in your field of vision.

EEN 

While your limbs are going to sleep, look directly into that white area. After a while, you will begin to get drowsy as sleep comes. You may see different things in that bright area. You see-when the body is completely out of the way, the only part of you left to function is your Life-or-The Spirit of God in you. Now then, the closer to sleep your body gets, the less of the Spirit of God is required, so this Great Sleeping Giant which is the Spirit of God in you, begins to come into your consciousness while you are going to sleep. That is exactly what I want. After a while you will be able to go right up to the moment of going to sleep, and you will do it consciously. 

Now while the Spirit of God in you comes into the ascendancy, I want you to say the affirmations I gave you in the different Lessons. Use one until you know that it is sufficient. I'd say for two weeks. Then, the next one, and so on. I have a friend who is a Canon in an Episcopal church in the San Francisco Bay area. A few months ago I was down there visiting him. On a Sunday morning he asked me to go into his church and witness a service, and I agreed to do that. The church will seat over 500 people, yet 26 were there. I sat in the back seat, watching the performance. It was interesting to me to see these good honest 26 

Americans enter the structure, face the altar, bow, and then take their seats.

The service itself was more like a vaudeville performance than anything I have ever seen. Candles, snuffers, incense and the half a dozen robes my friend wore. He would put one on and then take it off and put on another one. All the colors of the rainbow were in those robes. To me, knowing God, the performance was disgusting. 

After the show, I said to my friend: "Lewis-for God's sake is that the closest you can come to revealing to men and women the Power of Almighty God?" To which he replied: "Frank-I don't know what you are talking about.

Almighty God reveals himself thru the church, and those who attend church and follow me thru the ritual are saved.” Of course, this is a miserable theory of God and false in its entirety. 

So I said to Lewis: "How do your people make contact with God, and how does God reveal Himself thru that performance?" To which he replied: "God never reveals

is not doing a very good job of speaking, if only 26 people out of a city of half a million are interested in what God says." There was no answer to that.

"Why, that son-of-a-- will steal the coppers off a dead man's eyes; only last week I had to go and get him out of jail, but he's a hell of a good altar-boy."
