YOUR GOD-POWER 

If you ever get to heaven you won't need the Power of God up there. But you won't get there. And if you live a life based upon the expectation of seeing God in heaven, you'll not only miss God in heaven but you'll miss Him here too.

Not so long ago I was watching two of my friends conduct a "revival" in Portland, Oregon. Their theme-song was "There's wonderful power in the blood". That is a mistake. There is no Power in the blood of Jesus, the blood of Chrishna, the blood of Mohammet, Prometheus, nor is there any Power in the blood of any of the rest of the score of "gods" who, according to their followers, came down from heaven to earth by the virgin's womb, to save man from "sin", whatever that may be.

But I can tell you where there is a lot of Power. There is Power in the Spirit of God, and that's about the only place I know where there is any Power. Christianity, and the rest of the world's major systems of religion, make the fatal mistake of worshipping the messenger instead of the message. For every prophet has the same message and it is always of the Power of the Spirit of God. But, not being far advanced enough to believe in a Power they could not see, these old religious promoters made the fatal mistake of considering the messenger divine, and they deified those prophets of God. As a result, we have eleven major systems of religion on the earth today, each worshipping a different "crucified god". Needless to say, there is no truth in any of these systems of religion. Had they considered the message, not the messenger, they would be full of truth. But they did not. So we have religious and international chaos today, instead of the serene peace which comes from the 

Power of the Spirit of God.

It is a monstrous belief which states that the blood of

has a "god" who came to earth and died 2000 years ago efficacy today. This world doesn't want anyone's blood.

It's bathed in the blood of our sons now, without messing up the picture by adding the blood of a murdered God. 

Now, this teaching deals with no blood. It wants to get as far away from it as it can. It studies all the prophets, including the man Jesus, but it sees the message behind the messenger, and we are satisfied with our work. For we are revealing the Power of God to man.

There is this difference, however. In past ages only the credulous were interested in the different religious theories advanced by systems of religion. Today, the whole world is wondering what's wrong with the picture. Every thinking American knows that there must be some superior Power somewhere which can stop this ghastly mantle of horror and bloodshed. And they wonder where such a

Power is to be found. Certainly they do not look for it in the churches. For a priest and a preacher is looked upon with suspicion by the man in the street. And he should be. 

They wear the "cloth" which is their badge of God, yet there is not a single priest or preacher alive who can reveal the Power of the Spirit of God to anyone.

So, then, it is about time that some revelation direct from God came to this earth. I have, in these Lessons, tried to the best of my ability to show you the existing facts regarding the present existence and Power of Almighty God. 

I have shown you how that Spirit actually lives in you with all Its limitless Power. I have given you certain exercises to do, and if you have done them, you have found God in your life. Not that the exercises in themselves have much merit. But it was necessary for me to get your mind off your old theology and into the Realm of the Spirit of God. 

In this last Lesson there is little I can add. How far you

YOUR GOD-POWER 

go into the Realm of the Spirit of God is your own business. I cannot make you. But one would be a fool certainly with this amazing ocean of miraculous Power ever available, not to take advantage of it. I presume every one of you has seen the vision. Usually those who follow me in my writings have little trouble in understanding what I mean. Literally hundreds of thousands, perhaps millions, have found the Power of God over the past sixteen years as a result of what I have written. This is only because the 

Spirit of God dictates what I write, and, being a part of the Spirit of God yourself, you at once recognize that what I write is Spiritual Truth, coming direct from the Realm of God.

Man is a product of the evolutionary forces of God. It was never God's intention to make man complete in the first place. For God knew that there would be changing stages in the evolution of man towards God. For God the 

Creator must be an infinitely higher being than man, otherwise God could not have created man. 

From the very instant of man's creation, however, the 

Spirit of God breathed into man, His own self. That Selfthat Spirit which is God, has been the motivating Power in man ever since he appeared on this earthly scene. That 

Power-that Breath of Life, still is the life which manifests in the bodies of us all-and it still is The Spirit of Almighty God. But man in his early stages did not, or could not, comprehend the Pearl of Greatest price which Almighty 

God had placed in man.

Then came the priests, who, for pelf and political power, capitalized on God. They set up a system of man-made religion which in reality is not religion at all. It is a system of pagan idolatry if I ever saw one. Idols, images, incense, altar-boys, masses, etc.-all these things are a million miles

from God, and there is none of the Power of God in them. 

They are idolatrous practices, originated by priests for the express purpose of scaring men and women into their church.

They teach the absolute lie that Jesus Christ, as God, founded their organization and gave them the keys into the Kingdom of God. And otherwise intelligent men and women pay their good money into the hands of these priests for that sort of twiddle-dee-dee. This priest business goes a long way back in history. It had its origin in the dark ages of the past when the superstitious mind of man, listening to the priests, thought that when the lightning came God was "mad" at the human race, and they ran and hid in caves.

Well, I won't go into the evolution of religion since the dawn of history until the present day. I shall merely say this in closing: the world has never yet had true religion brought to it by any denomination or any system of religion. So far, it has been an eternal progress towards the 

Light which is God. And we are getting there. It has been necessary, I suppose, for priests and preachers to tell us that God Almighty allowed a handful of Italian soldiers to kill Him on a cross. For man without God, and in the hands of priests and preachers, is a very susceptible being. 

However, the wide world over men and women are looking at this brutal war and they are asking themselves this question: "If my priest or my preacher knows so much about God, why doesn't he use the Power of God to stop this war?" And the answer comes back again: "Your priest or your preacher knows absolutely nothing about God, therefore he cannot use the Power of God to stop this war or to do anything else."

Let us look at this thing in its proper light, though. These

YOUR GOD-POWER 

pagan ideas of Almighty God were necessary, it seems. 

Had man but realized the priceless Power Almighty God gave to man when He created him, it would not have been necessary. But priests came. And they hid God from man.

Instead of telling man that God breathed into his nostrils the Breath of Life-the very same Life that is God, these pagan priests told the human race that they were "born in sin and shapen in iniquity" and were doomed to hell unless they paid priests to intercede with God for the salvation of their eternal souls. 

No wonder, then, that man's eyes were taken off the priceless gift of the Spirit of God. No wonder the churches have blinded the eyes of man to the Power of God, by telling man that Almighty God is going to damn into hell every man who does not say he believes that Jesus, or Buddha, or Chrishna, or any one of a score of other "mangods", was actually Almighty God in person. Instead of revealing God to humanity, our churches have taught, and still are teaching, a philosophy of God so monstrous that even our children refuse to believe it. They have been honest in the main. They are still honest, most of them.

They actually believe that what they teach is of God. But any man with a small part of a brain knows full well that Almighty God exists. He knows further that the exercises of any church, Catholic or Protestant, cannot reveal the 

Power of God to anyone.

So, Americans by the millions are turning from all branches of Jesus religion, and outside of them all they are finding some smattering of the actual Truths of God. 

There are over sixteen millions of Americans who belong to New Thought organizations. They find some Truth there. But they can never find the fullness of the Power of God there so long as these dear New Thought people

insist on basing their teachings on Jesus, instead of on God. 

Had Jesus been advocated as a man, not as Almighty God, a lot of good would have been done. But the fatal mistake was made when the churches began to preach Him as God instead of listening to the message from God this man Jesus brought. You miss the mark completely if you consider Jesus Christ to be God. Just as well consider me to be God. Just as well consider any other prophet who has swung the world nearer to the Truths of God, to be God. There would be just as much sense in it. 

World conditions have made this revelation of the Power of God absolutely necessary. If it had not come to the earth, humanity would have destroyed itself. It might yet. 

I have no assurance the whole world will accept the Truths of God. If it does not, nothing but disaster lies ahead, and in the not too distant future. One cannot look with serenity on our own beloved America without wondering what the future holds in store.

I write this on August the thirty-first, 1943. In the City of 

Detroit recently, scores of colored men were killed in riots which were a blot on the fair name of America. I hold no brief for colored men. They had the same Creator you and 

I had. They are Americans. Their vote can kill mine. They are my equal in every way. I just simply don't care to associate with them, that's all.

In Harlem, as I write this, riots are rampant. There are strikes in our war industry. Capital is against labor and labor is against capital. Every thinker knows that there is an undercurrent of something akin to an economic upheaval right here at home. Never in the history of the 

United States has there been so much internal dynamite loose. Many people blame the New Deal for it. Well-I'm not a politician. It just simply goes to show, though, that

YOUR GOD-POWER 

even in America, the land we all love, without the Power of God, the priests and preachers are powerless to do a thing towards manifesting or otherwise demonstrating the Power of God, in this hour when we all need God more than we ever did.

I'm not so sure that religion as we know it will ever accept God. I'm not so sure that when the Power of God becomes universally known on the earth, as it will, priests and preachers won't be the very last to accept that Power. 

Were they honest, they would be the first. For if there is an organization on the face of the earth which could be benefited by my message, that organization is the church. 

They could take my message, experience first the Power of God, and unitedly they could bring the Axis fiends to their end without the firing of another shot, and without the shedding of one drop more blood. For God can do that.

And if the priests and the preachers knew God, they could do it. But they do not know God, nor do they want to know 

God. They prefer the lazy way of life, living with their old traditions.

But in this evolutionary period in Time, we have arrived at the parting of the ways. The Spirit of God is making 

Its Power known to men and women. The wise men and women will listen to what this prophet of God writes. 

They will heed him. They will find, then, the Greatest 

Treasure of all time-the Power of The Spirit of God in their lives. Using this Power, they will live an overcoming life-an overwhelming life. 

Το you who want to know the fullness of the Power of God I have but one more word of counsel. Do as I have asked you to do. Spend one hour daily, quietly waiting upon God, and when you find the Spirit of God is really manifesting Its Power in your life, don't stop there. As the

days and weeks go by, this indwelling Spirit will become so precious to you and so powerful that you'll bless the day your path and mine crossed.

For there is Power in the Spirit of God. I have told you how you may find and use that Power. Now go to work, and may the Spirit of Infinite Peace abide with you forever.

As the consciousness of the Power of God comes into your life, you will find that you will gradually want to withdraw from the world, although you will be much more active in it than ever before. But the charm-the matchless wonder of the Realm of God will exert over you a pulling Power which, if you will but let yourself go, will draw you as a magnet draws the needle, close to the Great Spirit of God. There you will find that which you have for so long sought. There you will find rest. And peace. And Power.

For this silent Spirit of God is supercharged with spiritual Power.

Your life, linked with the Great Overlife that is God, will be something new. You thought you lived before?

No-you never lived before. Life-true life, is not possible without the consciousness of God in it. It is an incomplete thing. It is a physical thing. It has in it all the elements which make for overwhelming victory, but it is not conscious of those elements. From the very first day of your birth, the Life-Principle in you was the Spirit of God. You used that Spirit automatically. Now, you will consciously use It.

Whenever there comes something into your life which you do not like, you will realize that that thing comes from the physical only. It cannot come from the Realm of God. 

But an application of the Power of God against that thing, no matter what it is, can right it. If that thing be illness, you have, living in you with all Its amazingly wonderful

YOUR GOD-POWER 

Power-the Spirit of God. That Spirit made your body. 

That Spirit can repair it. 

Is it a business problem that worries you? That, too, comes from the material realm all about you. It is physical in essence. But you have within you a far greater Power. 

You have the Spirit of God in you. And this Spirit, being the Creator of everything physical and material, has at Its disposal all the Power needed to take away that thing, or to solve that business problem. 

You ask me if it is wrong to take care of your physical, material needs? No-it is not wrong, provided you take care of them as you should, thru the Spirit of God. For there is no material problem which the Spirit of God cannot solve, and solve at once. Your only problem is to live your life so that the Spirit of God takes the complete ascendancy in it. Where your heart is, there will your treasure be. If your treasure is the Realm of God, and it must be after studying with me, your whole idea of life will revolve around that Spirit. You will have to submit your life completely to the overwhelming Power of God. You cannot be half for God and half for the world. You cannot care for the material things of life and the Spirit of God at the same time. For God will woo you if you will let Him. 

A sense of apartness will come to you. You will very often seek that quiet spot where you are wont to commune with God. And those meetings with God will be hallowed meetings. They will be sacred to you. They will charge your life with spiritual Power, and thru the indwelling 

Spirit of God you will rise triumphantly over every material problem.

The affirmations you must keep up as long as you need them. You will know when they are not necessary. That time will come when the full consciousness of God overflows in your life. Then you will settle down to allow God

to lead you where He wills to lead you. You will follow.

For to follow the inner leadings of the Spirit of God means complete victory. It means that the beautiful Realm of the 

Spirit of God is ever there, only waiting your entry. You and I have the Power to win the world for God. Will you help me? I am the prophet God has sent. But a prophet cannot do things alone. He presents the Truths of God. 

He cares nothing for organization. He never organizes. He knows that his message is from God, and he also knows that the message is coming a little ahead of time. 

For if the world did not need the message of the prophet, he would not come. But this sad world does need my message.

It needs to be told what the Power of God can do in this emergency. It needs to be told that the Power of God is here-not above the skies. It needs to be told that all the 

Power in the Realm of God is available to all men, here and now. It needs to be told that man was not "born in sin” but was made in the image or likeness of God. When that likeness begins to show in your face and mannerisms, people will know that you have been with God. 

All I have tried to do in this series of Lessons is to speak direct to you from out of the heart of God. What I have written is true. The Power lives. The Power lives for you. 

The Power is as free as the air you breathe. Won't you, then, give up enough time every day to spend one hour quietly experiencing the joys the Power of God brings to you? I'm sure you will. 

I don't know that our paths will ever cross personally.

It is questionable. I do go out in large auditoriums sometimes, but not very often. For I like to keep my little life hidden in that Great Overlife which is God. In any event, forget me. Remember God. I'm but the messenger. Forget me and grasp the message. It will liberate you.

A CLOSING  

THIS VOLUME is finished. I have put into it my very best. 

In itself, that would not be much, but what I have written has been written by direct inspiration of God. That fact was evident as you read the volume. 

I have had to say many things in this book which I did not particularly care to say. I have not shrunk, however, from speaking the Truth as that Truth has been revealed to me. Truth-especially new Truth always hurts someone because there are so many who are opposed to it. 

There are so many people in this religious world who swear by all that's holy that they have the truths of God, the whole truths of God, and nothing but the whole truths of God. Naturally, when God speaks to someone and reveals direct to that one what the Truths of God really are, great consternation reigns in the ranks of those who have believed they possess all the truths of God, anything not taught by them being untruth. 

The world today, however, stands as a pitiful comment on the so-called truths of God which have been taught and advocated by our religious organizations. If they have the 

Truths of God, they have never taught them. If they have taught them, they have not been sufficient to calm this mad world down, and bring to it the peace we all so hope for. 

This world war is upon us because man does not know 

God. And if man does not know God, the churches are directly to blame, for they and they alone have undertaken to tell this world what the truths of God are. And they have, for 2000 years, told us what they think are the abso- 344

A CLOSING  

345 lute inviolable truths of God. In spite of these so-called truths, however, this earth and the mad nations on it are a sad commentary on the absolute impotence of those supposed truths of God to make any impression whatsoever either in human lives or on national governments. 

So we might just as well face the facts fearlessly. If what 

I have written hurts our churches, that must be as it may be. I don't care. My first duty is to be true to God, then my country, then myself. All these I have striven to be. 

I shall continue to be true as long as I am privileged to live-and it is a privilege. 

One thing is certain. No-two things are certain. One is that somewhere there must exist a Supreme Intelligence which created this universe and everything in it. The second truth is that if such an intelligence exists, man must be able to find it. But man has not been able to find it. 

And the world knows that.

We have a score of major systems of religion-but none of the Power of God. These systems fight each other, and some of them, especially the Roman Catholic Church, are so rotten at the core that only a dupe could believe that the organization knows anything about God. The Protestant churches, being fallen branches from the Roman Catholic Church, inherited the same sort of theology. Hence, as the Roman Catholic Church cannot reveal the Power of 

God to the world, neither can the Protestant Church.

There is a reason for this. They do not have the truth about God. They neither know God nor any of the truths of God. They tell the world that 2000 years ago Almighty

God sent a son of his into the world to "save it from sin". 

But that evidently failed, for there never was as much sin in the world as there is today. 

Then, the Italian Church tells us, Jesus Christ, the son

YOUR GOD-POWER 

God sent to save the world from sin, instituted a church organization and handed the keys thereof unto the head of this Italian system of religion, and so ordained it that priests of this church had the sole bargaining rights between God and man. And thinking Americans believe that. 

Or say they do.

Now it is supremely evident to any thinker that this sort of twaddle cannot save this world from anything. The 

Churches reply that their philosophy is only designed to "save the soul" after death. Well, I'm not interested in such theology. I believe God lives. I believe the Power of Almighty God can not only "save the soul" after death, but can so demonstrate Its Power on the earth that all nations can live together in peace.

If the Power of God cannot so manifest itself, there is no God. But there is. The trouble lies in the fact that no system of religion to date has gotten down to the fundamental facts of what God is, or how God operates. To tell the world that God Almighty only operates thru priests of the Roman Catholic Church is to insult the intelligence of thinking Americans and others thruout the world. Also, it is to expose the greatest religious racket this world has ever known. For religion as operated today on the theory of a "crucified god" most certainly is a racket. And it is not a "non-profit” racket either.

I do not say that either Catholic or Protestant churches are dishonest. I do say that their theory of God is impotent to save this world. I do say that it is nothing more or less than an old pagan superstition, every fundamental of which was known to millions of people thousands of years before Christianity was born. There is not a word of truth in the entire theory of a virgin-born, crucified, resurrected

A CLOSING  

347 God. And under that theology this world is on the verge of crucifying itself, and there is not a single religious organization that can do one thing towards manifesting the actual Power of God on this earth. 

All of them combined cannot do that, and could not do that even if we could get them all to agree, which is an impossibility. The crowning condemnation of all however lies in the fact that our churches are so manifestly impotent to do one thing for humanity thru the Power of God, or by teaching the people where the Power of God is to be found. So the whole thing must be an abomination in the sight of God, and it probably is, if God recognizes the thing at all.

It will take more than talk about Jesus to straighten this world out and reveal the Power of God to man. It will take more than talk about the Apostle Paul to reveal the Power of God to the human race. It will take a direct and actual and factual contact with the Spirit of Almighty God Itself to do that, and naturally such a contact cannot be found thru untrue theories of God-only thru the truth itself. 

This volume discloses the Power of God to everyone who reads it. The reader instinctively knows that the Power of God manifests itself thru the book. This is because the 

Spirit of God is upon the writer of the book. The concept of God herein given to the world will prove itself. One does not have to wait until the river Styx has been crossed. 

From the very first moment the reader complies with the instructions given in this book, the Power of God begins to manifest Itself in the life of that reader. 

When the Power of God is known to enough men and women, and when false theories of God, all man-made and church-made, are once and for all discarded, then perhaps

YOUR GOD-POWER 

the world will awaken from its theological slumber, and we shall be safe for time and eternity, because we know God.

This war will not end until there has come to humanity a new concept of God. This concept, which the author is bringing to the world, will be accompanied by the Power of God. That is something this world has never known. 

That is something men and women have never been able to demonstrate here on the earth. Until they do, then we can look for one war after another until the human race either decimates itself or worse.

I have no word of apology for anything I have written about religion as it exists today. I have no word of apology for the churches, Catholic or Protestant. I have a word of advice, though. I suggest that they throw overboard at once the false notions and theories of God which have nearly ruined the world as well as the churches, and teach in the place of these impotent theories, THE POWER OF 

THE SPIRIT OF GOD IN HUMAN LIVES. 

Moscow, Idaho 

September 1st, 1943

THE AUTHOR
